     1	"""E2E harness fixtures (SPEC §18.3 items 22-27). Standalone: imports nothing from
     2	tests/unit.
     3	
     4	Each test gets a real ``plan serve`` subprocess on an OS-assigned port, backed by a
     5	fresh temp SQLite DB in ``PLAN_TEST_MODE`` with the boundaries faked (echo gateway by
     6	default; ``server_factory(gateway="offline")`` boots a second instance for the offline
     7	notice). Browser contexts come from pytest-playwright's session ``browser``; the
     8	``open_page`` / ``cli`` / ``api`` helpers drive the surfaces. Every Playwright wait
     9	carries an explicit ``timeout``; the only sleep is the readiness poll's 0.1s interval,
    10	which polls a condition inside a boot budget.
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	import json
    16	import os
    17	import socket
    18	import subprocess
    19	import time
    20	from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
    21	from dataclasses import dataclass
    22	from pathlib import Path
    23	from types import SimpleNamespace
    24	
    25	import httpx
    26	import pytest
    27	from playwright.sync_api import Browser, BrowserContext, Page
    28	
    29	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    30	PLAN_BIN = REPO_ROOT / ".venv" / "bin" / "plan"
    31	FAKE_NOW = "2026-07-04T12:00:00"
    32	WAIT_MS = 10_000          # every Playwright wait
    33	BOOT_BUDGET_S = 15.0      # server readiness budget
    34	
    35	
    36	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37	class ServerHandle:
    38	    base: str
    39	    proc: subprocess.Popen[bytes]
    40	    db_path: Path
    41	    log_path: Path
    42	
    43	
    44	def _free_port() -> int:
    45	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
    46	        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    47	        return int(sock.getsockname()[1])
    48	
    49	
    50	def _scrubbed_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    51	    """os.environ minus every ambient PLAN_* key (kills shell leakage)."""
    52	    return {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("PLAN_")}
    53	
    54	
    55	def _log_tail(log_path: Path, n: int = 40) -> str:
    56	    try:
    57	        lines = log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
    58	    except OSError:
    59	        return "(no server log)"
    60	    return "\n".join(lines[-n:])
    61	
    62	
    63	@pytest.fixture
    64	def server_factory(tmp_path: Path) -> Iterator[Callable[..., ServerHandle]]:
    65	    handles: list[ServerHandle] = []
    66	    counter = 0
    67	
    68	    def make(gateway: str | None = None) -> ServerHandle:
    69	        nonlocal counter
    70	        srvdir = tmp_path / f"srv{counter}"
    71	        counter += 1
    72	        # A1: the log file is opened from THIS process before the subprocess exists;
    73	        # plan serve only creates directories later, inside itself.
    74	        srvdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    75	
    76	        port = _free_port()
    77	        base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    78	        db_path = srvdir / "planning.db"
    79	        log_path = srvdir / "server.log"
    80	
    81	        env = _scrubbed_env()
    82	        env.update(
    83	            {
    84	                "PLAN_TEST_MODE": "1",
    85	                "PLAN_DB_PATH": str(db_path),
    86	                "PLAN_PORT": str(port),
    87	                "PLAN_FAKE_NOW": FAKE_NOW,
    88	                "PLAN_LOGS_DIR": str(srvdir / "logs"),
    89	                "PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH": str(srvdir / "dispatcher.lock"),
    90	                "PLAN_WS_POLL_MS": "50",
    91	                "PLAN_UI_DEBOUNCE_MS": "50",
    92	            }
    93	        )
    94	        if gateway == "offline":
    95	            env["PLAN_GATEWAY_ADAPTER"] = "offline"
    96	
    97	        log = log_path.open("wb")
    98	        try:
    99	            proc = subprocess.Popen(
   100	                [str(PLAN_BIN), "serve"],
   101	                cwd=str(REPO_ROOT),           # load-bearing: assets mount + config.yaml
   102	                env=env,
   103	                stdout=log,
   104	                stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   105	            )
   106	        finally:
   107	            log.close()                       # the child keeps its own dup'd fd
   108	        handle = ServerHandle(base=base, proc=proc, db_path=db_path, log_path=log_path)
   109	        handles.append(handle)
   110	
   111	        deadline = time.time() + BOOT_BUDGET_S
   112	        while time.time() < deadline:
   113	            if proc.poll() is not None:
   114	                raise AssertionError(
   115	                    f"plan serve exited during boot (rc={proc.returncode})\n"
   116	                    f"--- server log tail ---\n{_log_tail(log_path)}"
   117	                )
   118	            try:
   119	                resp: httpx.Response | None = httpx.get(f"{base}/api/meta", timeout=1.0)
   120	            except httpx.HTTPError:
   121	                resp = None
   122	            if resp is not None and resp.status_code == 200:
   123	                meta = resp.json()
   124	                assert meta["test_mode"] is True, meta
   125	                assert meta["ui_debounce_ms"] == 50, meta
   126	                assets = httpx.get(f"{base}/assets/api.js", timeout=1.0)
   127	                assert assets.status_code == 200, assets.status_code
   128	                return handle
   129	            time.sleep(0.1)
   130	
   131	        proc.terminate()
   132	        raise AssertionError(
   133	            f"plan serve did not become ready within {BOOT_BUDGET_S}s\n"
   134	            f"--- server log tail ---\n{_log_tail(log_path)}"
   135	        )
   136	
   137	    yield make
   138	
   139	    for handle in handles:
   140	        handle.proc.terminate()
   141	        try:
   142	            handle.proc.wait(timeout=5)
   143	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   144	            handle.proc.kill()
   145	            handle.proc.wait()
   146	
   147

   150	    """The default echo-gateway instance every test uses."""

   169	@pytest.fixture
   170	def open_page() -> Callable[..., Page]:
   171	    def _open(
   172	        ctx: BrowserContext,
   173	        server: ServerHandle,
   174	        route: str,
   175	        ready_selector: str,
   176	        settled: bool,
   177	    ) -> Page:
   178	        page = ctx.new_page()
   179	        page.goto(server.base + "/" + route)
   180	        page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   181	        # WS-open gate: never fire an observed mutation before the socket is live.
   182	        page.wait_for_function(
   183	            "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1",
   184	            timeout=WAIT_MS,
   185	        )
   186	        if settled:
   187	            # Events preceded page-open: let the since=0 catch-up replay's one flush
   188	            # re-render land, then re-anchor on the ready selector (screen replaced).
   189	            page.wait_for_function(
   190	                "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.flushes >= 1",
   191	                timeout=WAIT_MS,
   192	            )
   193	            page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   194	        return page
   195	
   196	    return _open

     9	## 0. Files & required companion changes
    10	
    11	**File to write:** `tests/e2e/test_flows_b.py` (the ONLY deliverable).
    12	
    13	**No conftest change is needed.** Every fixture used (`server`, `context_factory`,
    14	`open_page`, `cli`, `api`) already exists and is sufficient. The `/api/test/*`
    15	endpoints are driven through `api.human_post` (header-less POST); no new helper
    16	fixture is required.
    17	
    18	**Two findings that block items as literally specified — FLAGGED LOUDLY, both OUTSIDE
    19	the test fence. Neither can be fixed inside `test_flows_b.py`; the integrator/orchestrator
    20	must decide:**
    21	
    22	1. **Item 32 (Sprint) is blocked by a missing `<script>` tag.** The app shell
    23	   `_SHELL` in `src/planner/core/server.py` (lines 57–66) loads `screens-day.js`,
    24	   `screens-review.js`, `screens-board.js`, `screens-ticket.js` — but **not**
    25	   `screens-sprint.js` (which exists and is functional in `assets/`). Navigating to
    26	   `#/sprint` therefore renders app.js's placeholder `"not built yet"`
    27	   (`assets/app.js:28-32`), so the ready selector `[data-status-group="active"]`
    28	   never appears and item 32 hard-fails at `open_page`.
    29	   **Required one-line edit (server.py, after line 65):**
    30	   ```
    31	   <script src="/assets/screens-ticket.js"></script>
    32	   <script src="/assets/screens-sprint.js"></script>   <!-- ADD THIS LINE -->
    33	   ```
    34	   Write the item-32 test as specified below; it passes once, and only once, this
    35	   line is added. (Item 33, a different ticket, will additionally need
    36	   `screens-backlog.js`; out of scope here.)
    37	
    38	2. **Item 30's "propose result *with the claim env*" cannot be honoured in-fence, and
    39	   `plan run close` is impossible.** The dispatcher mints the claim token
    40	   (`new_claim()`, random, `dispatch/data.py:53`) into `tickets.claim_lock`, but **no
    41	   HTTP/CLI/test surface ever returns it**: `ticket_json` deliberately omits
    42	   `claim_lock` (`tickets/views.py:39-40`); the tick-dispatcher report's `spawned`
    43	   entries are `{ticket_id, run_id, pid}` only (`dispatch/runtime.py:197-199`); run
    44	   JSON and events carry no token. So a test can obtain `PLAN_RUN_ID` (from the report)
    45	   but never `PLAN_CLAIM`. Consequently `plan run close` — whose route calls
    46	   `require_claim` unconditionally (`dispatch/api.py:49`) — is unreachable, and a
    47	   *claimed* `propose result` is unreachable.
    48	   **Resolution (SPEC-sanctioned, in-fence):** the dispatched-worker's `propose result`
    49	   is issued **claimless** — the CLI sends only `X-Plan-Actor: agent`
    50	   (`cli/http.py:35`), which `authctx` classifies as a *non-dispatched agent context*
    51	   (`is_claimed_agent=False`), so `propose_field` skips `require_claim`
    52	   (`tickets/api.py:296-297`) and files a normal agent proposal. This is exactly the
    53	   §7.6 exception ("proposal/recap writes from non-dispatched agent contexts … are
    54	   permitted"). It reaches every **scored** assertion of SPEC item 30 (line 309):
    55	   dispatcher claims it (run visible on Ticket), result parks in `needs_review`,
    56	   appears in the approval queue, approve → `done`. The `plan run close` sub-step
    57	   named only in the *ticket* (not the SPEC item) is **omitted**; note this in
    58	   `decisions.md`. If literal claim-env fidelity is wanted later, it requires a new
    59	   test-mode seam (e.g. add `claim` to the report's `spawned` entries, or a
    60	   `GET /api/test/claim/{ticket_id}`) — a separate src ticket.
    61

    64	## 1. Harness recap (reuse, do not reinvent)
    65	
    66	From `tests/e2e/conftest.py`:
    67	- `server` — one fresh `plan serve` subprocess + fresh temp SQLite DB per test,
    68	  `PLAN_TEST_MODE=1`, `PLAN_FAKE_NOW=2026-07-04T12:00:00`, `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS=50`,
    69	  `PLAN_UI_DEBOUNCE_MS=50`, echo gateway, **fake** spawn/boundary adapters (auto→fake
    70	  under test mode, `registry.py:35-58`). **`server` alone suffices for all five tests**
    71	  — fresh DB, no cross-test state; `server_factory` is not needed.
    72	- `open_page(ctx, server, route, ready_selector, settled)` — goes to
    73	  `server.base + "/" + route`, waits `ready_selector`, waits `wsOpens>=1`; if
    74	  `settled=True` also waits `flushes>=1` then re-anchors `ready_selector`. **Use
    75	  `settled=True` whenever the DB state was mutated before the page opened** (all five
    76	  tests open their pages after setup).
    77	- `cli(server, *args, ticket_id=?, actor=?, run_id=?, claim=?, stdin=?)` — runs
    78	  `plan … --json`, asserts rc==0, returns parsed JSON. Sets `PLAN_TICKET_ID`/
    79	  `PLAN_ACTOR`/`PLAN_RUN_ID`/`PLAN_CLAIM` env only when the kwarg is given.
    80	- `api.get(server, path)` → GET JSON (asserts <300). `api.human_post(server, path,
    81	  json_body)` → POST JSON with **no** `X-Plan-*` headers (classified as the human;
    82	  also the correct caller for the `/api/test/*` endpoints, which do no auth).
    83	
    84	Module top matches flows_a: `from __future__ import annotations`, `from
    85	playwright.sync_api import Page`, `WAIT_MS = 10_000`, and a module docstring (see §3).
    86	
    87	**Fence rules (SPEC §18.2 / ticket):** exactly ONE test per anchor
    88	`test_e28_`…`test_e32_` across the whole suite; every shared helper has a non-`test_`
    89	name; no `pytest.skip`/`xfail`/`.only`/commented-out/empty bodies; no `parametrize` on
    90	anchored tests. Assert SPEC-exact literals, never weakened.

   145	## 3. Module docstring (mirror flows_a's discipline)
   146	
   147	```
   148	"""E2E flows B — SPEC §18.3 items 28-32 (Playwright, chromium; two contexts in e32).
   149	
   150	One test per acceptance item, its name carrying the ``test_eNN_`` anchor the verify
   151	scorer matches: exactly one anchored match per item across the whole e2e suite, so no
   152	parametrize and every shared helper below has a non-``test_`` name. Boundary/dispatch
   153	ticks are driven synchronously through /api/test/*; time through /api/test/set-now.
   154	Assertions use the SPEC's exact values (planning dates, fake-adapter strings, node
   155	statuses, ticket states); every wait carries an explicit timeout and precedes its
   156	assert — no sleeps."""
   157	```

   161	## 4. Shared non-`test_` helpers
   162	
   163	```python
   164	def _set_now(api, server, iso):
   165	    r = api.human_post(server, "/api/test/set-now", {"now": iso})
   166	    return r  # {"now":..., "planning_date":...}
   167	
   168	def _tick_boundary(api, server):
   169	    return api.human_post(server, "/api/test/tick-boundary", {})   # pinned 4-key report
   170	
   171	def _tick_dispatcher(api, server):
   172	    return api.human_post(server, "/api/test/tick-dispatcher", {})  # pinned 5-key report
   173	
   174	def _wait_node_note(page, node, text):
   175	    page.wait_for_function(
   176	        "a => { const n = document.querySelector('[data-node=\"'+a.node+'\"] .plan-node-note');"
   177	        " return !!n && n.textContent === a.text; }",
   178	        arg={"node": str(node), "text": text}, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   179	
   180	def _grant_and_advance(server, api, cli, tid, ceiling):
   181	    # Human grant (header-less → human; grant_ticket rejects agents, tickets/api.py:359).
   182	    g = api.human_post(server, f"/api/tickets/{tid}/grant",
   183	                       {"ceiling": ceiling, "at_cap": "propose"})
   184	    assert g["ceiling"] == ceiling and g["at_cap"] == "propose", g
   185	    # Claimless CLI proposals auto-accept up the chain to in_progress (like flows_a e27).
   186	    for field, body in (("success", ...), ("approach", ...), ("plan", ...)):
   187	        cli(server, "propose", field, "--body-file", "-", ticket_id=tid, stdin=body)
   188	    d = api.get(server, f"/api/tickets/{tid}")
   189	    assert d["state"] == "in_progress", d
   190	    return d
   191	
   192	def _reload_settle(page, ready_selector):
   193	    # open_page opens FRESH pages; after page.reload() re-apply its wait discipline.
   194	    page.reload()
   195	    page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   196	    page.wait_for_function(
   197	        "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1", timeout=WAIT_MS)
   198	```
   199	(`_grant_and_advance` bodies come from the caller's E30_*/E31_* constants; inline the
   200	three-item loop with the right constants per test, or pass a mapping — implementer's
   201	choice, but keep it one helper, non-`test_`.)
   202	
   203	---
   204	
   205	## 5. Per-test blueprints
   206	
   207	Screen/route facts (from `assets/`): Day route `#/day`, screen root
   208	`section[data-screen="day"]` set by `screens-day.js:23`; Board `#/board`,
   209	`section[data-screen="board"]`; Ticket `#/ticket/<id>`,
   210	`section[data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id][data-state]` (`screens-ticket.js:427-430`);
   211	Review `#/review`; Sprint `#/sprint`, root `[data-screen="sprint"]` (a `div`, set
   212	`screens-sprint.js:313`). The `open_page` `route` arg includes the leading `#`
   213	(e.g. `"#/day"`), exactly as flows_a passes it.

   215	### test_e28_day_boundary_accept_all(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   216	
   217	Satisfies SPEC line 307: "driving `POST /api/test/tick-boundary` at fake-now 05:01
   218	produces the brief and a proposed tree; Accept-all adds the child tickets to the day
   219	list." Guarantees: boundary job §6.2 (`days/boundary.py:34-90`), carryover
   220	(`carryover.py:20-36`), fake judgment children-from-carryover (`fakes.py:50-62`),
   221	tree render (`components.js:558-617`), accept-all + AddTicketToDay (§6.3 line 137,
   222	`tree.py:101-114`, `days/data.py:185-207`).
   223

   229	   (`cli/main.py:514-520`; route materializes the day, `days/api.py:129-137`). This is
   230	   yesterday's day-ticket, NOT a plan of 2026-07-05, so the §6.2 human-planned skip
   231	   (`boundary.py:170-182`) is not triggered.

   233	4. `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`. **Assert the pinned report**
   234	   (`scheduler.py:268-273`): `rep == {"planning_date": DAY_CUR, "ran": True,
   235	   "judgment": "ok", "replan": None}`. (`ran` True: first tick for 2026-07-05;
   236	   `judgment` "ok": success path recorded `boundary.py:90`.)
   237	5. `page = open_page(context_factory(), server, "#/day",
   238	   'section[data-screen="day"]', settled=True)`.
   239	6. **Brief** (§6.2 stored brief → `markdownBlock`): assert
   240	   `page.inner_text('.day-main .markdown-block h1') == FAKE_BRIEF_H1`.
   241	   (`# Brief for 2026-07-05` → `<h1>`, `markdown.js:124-129`; day-main's only
   242	   markdown-block, `screens-day.js:113`.)
   243	7. **Proposed tree** (`planTree`): wait `page.wait_for_selector('.plan-tree [data-node="0"]',
   244	   timeout=WAIT_MS)`, then assert:
   245	   - `page.inner_text('.plan-tree [data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus') == FAKE_FOCUS`
   246	   - `page.get_attribute('.plan-tree [data-node="root"]', "data-status") == "proposed"`
   247	   - `page.inner_text('.plan-tree [data-node="0"] .plan-node-note') == E28_TITLE`
   248	     (child note = carryover title, `fakes.py:53`)
   249	   - `page.get_attribute('.plan-tree [data-node="0"]', "data-status") == "proposed"`
   250	8. **Accept-all** (top-level control, `components.js:562-565`): capture
   251	   `f0 = page.evaluate("window.__plannerDebug.flushes")`; `page.click('[data-accept-all]')`.
   252	   No reload — the child ticket can only reach the Today panel via a WS-flush re-render.
   253	9. Wait: `page.wait_for_selector(f'.day-ticket-row[data-ticket-id="{tid}"]', timeout=WAIT_MS)`.
   254	   Assert exactly once on the day list AND a fresh flush landed:
   255	   - `page.eval_on_selector_all(f'.day-ticket-row[data-ticket-id="{tid}"]',
   256	     "els => els.length") == 1` (idempotent add, `days/data.py:114-119`).

   258	   - Cross-check the canonical source: `d = api.get(server, "/api/day/today")`;

   260	     `d["plan"]["root"]["status"] == "accepted"`, `d["plan"]["children"][0]["status"]
   261	     == "accepted"` (accept-all cascades, `tree.py:106-109`).

   263	### test_e29_invalidation_root_and_child(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   264	
   265	Satisfies SPEC line 308: root invalidate → replacement tree; child invalidate →
   266	only that child replaced, others keep status. Guarantees: `invalidate_child`/
   267	`invalidate_root` (`tree.py:117-144`), **latest-wins replan queue is NOT executed in
   268	the request** — the invalidate endpoint marks-invalidated + `submit_replan` (enqueue
   269	only) post-commit (`days/api.py:191-195`), and execution happens only when
   270	`process_pending_replan` runs, which in test mode is reachable ONLY via
   271	`POST /api/test/tick-boundary` (`scheduler.py:227-273`; no background loops in test
   272	mode, `server.py:92`). So every invalidate is followed by one tick-boundary.
   273	
   274	**Ordering (justified):** `replan_root` returns a tree with **zero children**
   275	(`fakes.py:70`). Do **child-invalidate first** (children still exist), then
   276	root-invalidate. Both halves live in this one `test_e29_` function.
   277	
   278	Setup (two carryover children):
   279	1. `a = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E29_A_TITLE)["id"]`;
   280	   `b = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E29_B_TITLE)["id"]`.
   281	2. `cli(server,"day","add-ticket",a,DAY_PREV)` then `cli(server,"day","add-ticket",b,DAY_PREV)`
   282	   → positions 0,1 on 2026-07-04 (`_read_yesterday_tickets` orders by position,
   283	   `boundary.py:106-116`).
   284	3. `_set_now(api, server, NOW_0501)`; `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`; assert
   285	   `rep == {"planning_date":DAY_CUR,"ran":True,"judgment":"ok","replan":None}`.
   286	4. `page = open_page(ctx, server, "#/day", 'section[data-screen="day"]', settled=True)`;
   287	   `page.wait_for_selector('.plan-tree [data-node="1"]', timeout=WAIT_MS)`.
   288	   Assert initial: `[data-node="0"] .plan-node-note == E29_A_TITLE`,
   289	   `[data-node="1"] .plan-node-note == E29_B_TITLE`, both `data-status="proposed"`,
   290	   root focus `FAKE_FOCUS` `data-status="proposed"`.
   291	
   292	Child invalidate (node 0):
   293	5. `api.human_post(server, f"/api/day/{DAY_CUR}/plan/invalidate", {"node": 0})`
   294	   (`node` an **int**; the server rejects string positions, `days/api.py:83-90`).
   295	   Use `api.human_post` (blocks until committed+enqueued) so the following tick sees
   296	   the queued request — a UI click would leave an async gap where the tick could drain
   297	   an empty queue (race). `human_post` is header-less → human → `reject_agents` passes
   298	   (`days/api.py:181`).
   299	6. `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)` drains the replan. Assert
   300	   `rep["ran"] is False` (2026-07-05 boundary already ran; idempotent guard
   301	   `boundary.py:44`), `rep["judgment"] == "ok"`, and
   302	   `rep["replan"] == {"attempts": [{"day_id": f"day_{DAY_CUR}", "scope": "child",
   303	   "node": 0, "outcome": "stored"}]}` (`scheduler.py:224`).
   304	7. `_wait_node_note(page, 0, FAKE_REPLAN_CHILD)` (WS-driven re-render). Then assert
   305	   "only that child replaced (others keep status/note)":
   306	   - `[data-node="0"] .plan-node-note == FAKE_REPLAN_CHILD`, `data-status="proposed"`
   307	     (replan_child returns proposed, position preserved, `fakes.py:78-83`).
   308	   - `[data-node="1"] .plan-node-note == E29_B_TITLE`, `data-status="proposed"` (unchanged).
   309	   - `[data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus == FAKE_FOCUS`, `data-status="proposed"` (unchanged).
   310	
   311	Root invalidate:
   312	8. `api.human_post(server, f"/api/day/{DAY_CUR}/plan/invalidate", {"node": "root"})`
   313	   (string `"root"`). This marks root + every child invalidated and enqueues one
   314	   ReplanRoot (`tree.py:117-127`).
   315	9. `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`; assert
   316	   `rep["replan"]["attempts"] == [{"day_id": f"day_{DAY_CUR}", "scope": "root",
   317	   "node": "root", "outcome": "stored"}]`.
   318	10. Wait for the replacement tree to render (zero children, `fakes.py:70`):
   319	    `page.wait_for_function("() => { const f = document.querySelector('[data-node=\"root\"]"
   320	    " .plan-node-focus'); return !!f && f.textContent === '" + FAKE_REPLAN_FOCUS + "'"
   321	    " && document.querySelectorAll('.plan-tree .plan-node--child').length === 0; }",
   322	    timeout=WAIT_MS)`. Then assert:
   323	    - `page.inner_text('[data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus') == FAKE_REPLAN_FOCUS`.
   324	    - `page.eval_on_selector_all('.plan-tree .plan-node--child', "els=>els.length") == 0`.
   325	    - Cross-check: `api.get(server,"/api/day/today")["plan"]` →
   326	      `root.focus == FAKE_REPLAN_FOCUS`, `children == []`.

   328	### test_e30_dispatcher_e2e_to_done(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   329	
   330	Satisfies SPEC line 309 (scored assertions): tick-dispatcher claims an eligible ticket
   331	(run visible on Ticket); the worker's `propose result` parks it in `needs_review`; it
   332	appears in the approval queue; approve → `done`. Guarantees: eligibility §7.2
   333	(`eligibility.py:36-59`), claim+run (`dispatch/data.py:42-67`), fake spawn ok
   334	(`fakes.py:27-33`), result routing §4.4.5 (`resolution.py:108` + `machine.advance_target`),
   335	approval queue "review" (`carryover.py:103-106`, `tickets/views.py:298-309`), approve
   336	(`resolution.py:190-199`). **Claim-env caveat: see §0 finding 2 — `propose result` is
   337	claimless; no `plan run close`.** No `set-now` needed (dispatch works at the baseline
   338	clock; claim TTL 900s never expires under the fixed test clock).
   339	
   340	1. `mid = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E30_TITLE)["id"]`.
   341	2. `_grant_and_advance(server, api, cli, mid, "needs_review")` using E30_SUCCESS/
   342	   APPROACH/PLAN → drives `needs_success→…→in_progress` (each target ≤ ceiling
   343	   `needs_review` ⇒ auto-accept, `resolution.py:108`). Post-assert state `in_progress`.
   344	3. `page = open_page(context_factory(), server, f"#/ticket/{mid}",
   345	   f'section[data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id="{mid}"]', settled=True)`.

   347	   `page.query_selector('[data-marker="running-claim"]') is None`;
   348	   Runs panel shows `(no runs)` (`page.inner_text('[data-run-history]')` contains
   349	   `"(no runs)"`, `components.js:920-921`).
   350	4. `rep = _tick_dispatcher(api, server)`. Assert (`runtime.py:127-218`):
   351	   `rep["skipped"] is None`; `len(rep["spawned"]) == 1`;
   352	   `rep["spawned"][0]["ticket_id"] == mid`; `run_id = rep["spawned"][0]["run_id"]`
   353	   is truthy. (Only `mid` is eligible on this DB, so exactly one claim.)
   354	5. **Run visible on Ticket, no reload** (WS flush from `run_started`):
   355	   `page.wait_for_selector('[data-run-history] [data-run-row][data-run-status="running"]',
   356	   timeout=WAIT_MS)`; `page.wait_for_selector('[data-marker="running-claim"]',
   357	   timeout=WAIT_MS)`. Cross-check API: `api.get(server,f"/api/tickets/{mid}/runs")`
   358	   → `runs[0]["status"] == "running"` and `runs[0]["id"] == run_id`.
   359	6. Worker files the result (claimless — §0 finding 2):
   360	   `r = cli(server, "propose", "result", "--body-file", "-", ticket_id=mid,
   361	   stdin=E30_RESULT)`. Assert `r["state"] == "needs_review"` (auto-accept →
   362	   `needs_review`, ceiling≠`done` ⇒ not straight to `done`, §4.4.5).
   363	7. Ticket page flips without reload: `page.wait_for_function("() => { const s ="
   364	   " document.querySelector('section[data-screen=\"ticket\"]'); return !!s &&"
   365	   " s.getAttribute('data-state') === 'needs_review'; }", timeout=WAIT_MS)`.
   366	8. Approval queue + approve via the Review "review" card (SPEC §10 screen 2):
   367	   `card = f'[data-review-card][data-entity-id="{mid}"]'`;
   368	   `rpage = open_page(context_factory(), server, "#/review", card, settled=True)`;
   369	   assert `rpage.get_attribute(card, "data-kind") == "review"` (`components.js:769`);
   370	   also cross-check `api.get(server,"/api/queues")["approvals"]` has one entry with
   371	   `entity_id == mid`, `kind == "review"`.
   372	9. `rpage.click(f"{card} [data-approve]")` (`components.js:727-732`; wired to
   373	   `POST /api/tickets/{id}/approve`, `screens-review.js:123-127`). Wait
   374	   `rpage.wait_for_selector("[data-review-empty]", timeout=WAIT_MS)` (queue emptied).
   375	10. Assert terminal: `api.get(server, f"/api/tickets/{mid}")["state"] == "done"`.

   377	### test_e31_refresh_restores_state(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   378	
   379	Satisfies SPEC line 310: on Day, Board, and Ticket **mid-flow (pending proposal,
   380	running claim)** a reload renders identical content. Guarantees: stateless render / "refresh
   381	restores identical state" (SPEC §10 line 191, `app.js:1-5`), board markers §10.3
   382	(`board_view` `tickets/views.py:245-246`, `screens-board.js:34-49`), pending-proposal
   383	parked at ceiling with `at_cap=propose` (§4.3, `resolution.py:119-139`), running claim
   384	(dispatch).
   385	
   386	**Mid-flow construction** — one ticket carries BOTH a pending gating-field proposal
   387	AND a running claim. This requires claiming *before* the proposal is filed (a
   388	gating-pending ticket is dispatch-ineligible, `eligibility.py:53`), and a ceiling that
   389	makes the result proposal PARK rather than advance ⇒ **ceiling `in_progress`**
   390	(target `needs_review` > ceiling ⇒ no auto-accept, but `state==ceiling==in_progress`
   391	with `at_cap=propose` ⇒ dispatch-eligible branch (b), `eligibility.py:59`).
   392	
   393	1. `mid = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E31_TITLE)["id"]`.
   394	2. `_grant_and_advance(server, api, cli, mid, "in_progress")` with E31_SUCCESS/APPROACH/
   395	   PLAN → `in_progress`, ceiling `in_progress`, `at_cap propose`, no claim, no pending.
   396	3. Day plan (a "pending plan tree"): `_set_now(api, server, NOW_0501)`;
   397	   `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`; assert `rep == {"planning_date":DAY_CUR,
   398	   "ran":True,"judgment":"ok","replan":None}`. Carryover is empty (no 2026-07-04
   399	   day-tickets) ⇒ **root-only proposed tree** (`fakes.py:59`, children=[]).
   400	4. `rep = _tick_dispatcher(api, server)`; assert `len(rep["spawned"])==1` and
   401	   `rep["spawned"][0]["ticket_id"]==mid` (only `mid` is eligible). `mid` now has a
   402	   running claim + run.
   403	5. Worker files the result (claimless): `r = cli(server,"propose","result",
   404	   "--body-file","-",ticket_id=mid,stdin=E31_RESULT)`; assert `r["state"]=="in_progress"`
   405	   and `r["fields"]["result"]["proposal"]["body"]==E31_RESULT` (PARKED, not advanced).
   406	   Now: `in_progress` + pending `result` proposal + active claim = the mid-flow state.
   407	
   408	Three surfaces. Define stable snapshots (content-bearing, timestamp-free) and assert
   409	`before == after` AND the exact values.
   410	
   411	**Ticket** (`page_t`), ready `f'section[data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id="{mid}"]'`,
   412	mid-flow selector `[data-field="result"] .proposal-card`:
   413	```python
   414	def _snap_ticket(p):
   415	    return {
   416	      "state": p.get_attribute('section[data-screen="ticket"]', "data-state"),
   417	      "meta":  p.inner_text('[data-field="result"] .proposal-card .proposal-meta'),
   418	      "body":  p.inner_text('[data-field="result"] .proposal-card .markdown-block'),
   419	      "claim": p.eval_on_selector_all('[data-marker="running-claim"]', "e=>e.length"),
   420	      "run":   p.eval_on_selector_all(
   421	                 '[data-run-history] [data-run-row][data-run-status="running"]', "e=>e.length"),
   422	    }
   423	```
   424	Expected: `state=="in_progress"`, `meta=="proposed by agent"` (`components.js:492`;
   425	proposer is `PLAN_ACTOR` default `agent`), `body==E31_RESULT`, `claim==1`
   426	(header marker `screens-ticket.js:129-131`), `run==1`.
   427	
   428	**Board** (`page_b`), ready `'section[data-screen="board"]'`, mid-flow selector
   429	`f'[data-column="in_progress"] [data-card][data-ticket-id="{mid}"]'`:
   430	```python
   431	def _snap_board(p):
   432	    card = f'[data-column="in_progress"] [data-card][data-ticket-id="{mid}"]'
   433	    return {
   434	      "title": p.inner_text(f'{card} .entity-row-title'),
   435	      "pend":  p.eval_on_selector_all(f'{card} [data-marker="pending-proposal"]', "e=>e.length"),
   436	      "claim": p.eval_on_selector_all(f'{card} [data-marker="running-claim"]', "e=>e.length"),
   437	    }
   438	```
   439	Expected: `title==E31_TITLE`, `pend==1`, `claim==1` (`board_view` sets
   440	`has_pending_proposal`+`has_running_claim`, markers `screens-board.js:39-40`).
   441	
   442	**Day** (`page_d`), ready `'.plan-tree [data-node="root"]'`, mid-flow selector same:
   443	```python
   444	def _snap_day(p):
   445	    return {
   446	      "focus":  p.inner_text('[data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus'),
   447	      "status": p.get_attribute('[data-node="root"]', "data-status"),
   448	      "brief":  p.inner_text('.day-main .markdown-block h1'),
   449	    }
   450	```
   451	Expected: `focus==FAKE_FOCUS`, `status=="proposed"`, `brief==FAKE_BRIEF_H1`.
   452	
   453	6. Open all three with `settled=True`. For each: wait its mid-flow selector, take
   454	   `before = _snap_*(page)`, then `_reload_settle(page, ready_selector)`, wait the
   455	   mid-flow selector again, take `after = _snap_*(page)`. Assert `before == after`
   456	   **and** `before == <expected dict>` (both, so "identical" isn't vacuously
   457	   satisfied by two identically-broken renders).

   459	### test_e32_sprint_live_status_and_loose(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   460	
   461	Satisfies SPEC line 311: `plan item set --status active` reflects on the Sprint screen
   462	in both contexts without reload; loose ticket appears in the loose section. Guarantees:
   463	agent `todo→active` (§3.2 line 41, `sprints/data.py:390-434`, `classify_agent_transition`),
   464	sprint-current view groups + loose tickets §5 (`sprints/views.py:195-230`), Sprint
   465	render (`screens-sprint.js`). **Requires the §0-finding-1 shell edit; without it every
   466	step below times out at `open_page`.** No `set-now` — baseline planning date 2026-07-04
   467	lies inside `[E32_START, E32_END]`, so `/api/sprint/current` resolves this sprint
   468	(`sprints/logic.current_sprint_id`).
   469	
   470	1. Create the current sprint (human-only; there is no `plan sprint create` verb —
   471	   `cli/main.py:443-459` has only `sprint show`): `s = api.human_post(server,
   472	   "/api/sprints", {"name":E32_SPRINT_NAME, "date_start":E32_START, "date_end":E32_END})`;
   473	   `sid = s["id"]` (`sprints/api.py:254-273`).
   474	2. Item in the sprint (defaults status `todo`): `iid = cli(server,"item","create",
   475	   "--title",E32_ITEM_TITLE,"--project",E32_ITEM_PROJECT,"--sprint",sid)["id"]`
   476	   (`cli/main.py:351-375` → `POST /api/items` with `sprint_id`).
   477	3. Loose ticket (sprint set, no parent item): `ltid = cli(server,"ticket","create",
   478	   "--title",E32_LOOSE_TITLE,"--sprint",sid)["id"]` (`--sprint`→`sprint_id`,
   479	   `sprint_item_id` stays NULL ⇒ loose, `sprints/views.py:220-225`).
   480	4. Open two Sprint contexts (state preceded ⇒ `settled=True`):
   481	   `ready = '[data-status-group="active"]'`;
   482	   `pa = open_page(context_factory(), server, "#/sprint", ready, settled=True)`;
   483	   `pb = open_page(context_factory(), server, "#/sprint", ready, settled=True)`.
   484	   The Items panel always renders all five status groups (`screens-sprint.js:133-136`),
   485	   so `ready` is present once the sprint view loads.
   486	5. Initial assertions (both pages):
   487	   - item in `todo`: `p.wait_for_selector(f'[data-status-group="todo"]
   488	     [data-item-id="{iid}"]', timeout=WAIT_MS)`.
   489	   - loose ticket present: `assert p.query_selector(f'[data-loose]
   490	     [data-ticket-id="{ltid}"]') is not None` (`screens-sprint.js:213-234`) — satisfies
   491	     the "loose ticket appears in the loose section" clause.
   492	6. Capture flush counters: `fa = pa.evaluate("window.__plannerDebug.flushes")`;
   493	   `fb = pb.evaluate("window.__plannerDebug.flushes")`.
   494	7. `cli(server, "item", "set", iid, "--status", "active")` →
   495	   `PATCH /api/items/{iid} {"status":"active"}`, `by_agent=True` (CLI sends
   496	   `X-Plan-Actor: agent`), agent `todo→active` permitted (`sprints/api.py:225-230`).
   497	8. Live move in BOTH contexts, no reload (WS flush → refetch → re-render):
   498	   for `p, f0` in `((pa, fa), (pb, fb))`:
   499	   - `p.wait_for_selector(f'[data-status-group="active"] [data-item-id="{iid}"]',
   500	     timeout=WAIT_MS)`.
   501	   - assert it left `todo`: `p.query_selector(f'[data-status-group="todo"]
   502	     [data-item-id="{iid}"]') is None`.
   503	   - assert a fresh flush drove it: `p.evaluate("window.__plannerDebug.flushes") > f0`.
   504	9. Cross-check canonical source: `api.get(server, "/api/sprint/current")` →
   505	   the item id appears under `groups["active"]`, not `groups["todo"]`; `ltid` in
   506	   `[t["id"] for t in loose_tickets]`.
   507	
   508	---
   509	
   510	## 6. Risks & race notes (each neutralized; wait-on-condition then assert)
   511	
   512	- **Replan is NOT synchronous (e29).** `POST …/plan/invalidate` only marks-invalidated
   513	  and *enqueues* (`days/api.py:191-195`); execution is in `process_pending_replan`,
   514	  reachable in test mode ONLY via `POST /api/test/tick-boundary` (`scheduler.py:265`;
   515	  no background loops, `server.py:92`). Neutralize: after every invalidate, issue one
   516	  `_tick_boundary` and assert its `replan.attempts[…].outcome == "stored"` BEFORE the
   517	  DOM wait. Issue the invalidate via `api.human_post` (blocks until commit+enqueue) so
   518	  the tick cannot drain an empty queue.
   519	- **e29 ordering hazard.** `replan_root` returns zero children (`fakes.py:70`), so
   520	  child-invalidate MUST precede root-invalidate — otherwise no child remains to
   521	  invalidate. Encoded in the step order.
   522	- **WS-flush timing (e28 accept-all, e30 run/flip, e32 status move).** Every observed
   523	  mutation is asserted only after `wait_for_selector`/`wait_for_function` on the
   524	  post-flush condition (rows present, `data-state`, status-group membership), never a
   525	  bare read; flush progress is additionally asserted via `window.__plannerDebug.flushes`
   526	  where "no reload" is the claim (e28, e32) — the flows_a pattern.
   527	- **`open_page` opens fresh pages; `page.reload()` does not (e31).** `_reload_settle`
   528	  re-applies ready-selector + `wsOpens>=1`, and each snapshot is taken only after its
   529	  mid-flow selector re-appears post-reload. The fixed test clock (never advanced past
   530	  05:01) keeps the 900s claim active, so `running-claim`/running-run markers persist
   531	  identically across the reload. Snapshots exclude timestamps (`run-history-times`,
   532	  `formatUnix`) — only statuses, notes, markers, bodies, and `data-state` are compared.
   533	- **Dispatcher claims exactly the intended ticket (e30, e31).** Each test's DB has a
   534	  single dispatch-eligible ticket at tick time, so `spawned` has length 1; asserted.
   535	  (In e31, ceiling `in_progress` + `at_cap propose` is what makes `mid` eligible at
   536	  `in_progress` via branch (b); after the result parks, it becomes ineligible but is
   537	  already claimed.)
   538	- **Claim-env unobtainable (e30) / shell gap (e32).** See §0. e30 uses the §7.6
   539	  claimless path and omits `plan run close`; e32 depends on the one-line `server.py`
   540	  shell edit. Both flagged for the integrator; the test bodies below assume the shell
   541	  edit is applied.
   542	- **`ran`/`judgment` report values.** First tick per date: `ran True`, `judgment "ok"`.
   543	  A second tick same date (e29 drains): `ran False`, `judgment "ok"` (idempotent guard,
   544	  `boundary.py:44`). Pinned in the report asserts.
   545	
   546	## 7. Fence-compliance checklist
   547	
   548	- Exactly one anchored test each: `test_e28_…`, `test_e29_…`, `test_e30_…`,
   549	  `test_e31_…`, `test_e32_…`. No other `test_`-prefixed names.
   550	- Helpers `_set_now`, `_tick_boundary`, `_tick_dispatcher`, `_wait_node_note`,
   551	  `_grant_and_advance`, `_reload_settle`, `_snap_ticket`, `_snap_board`, `_snap_day`
   552	  are all non-`test_`.
   553	- No `parametrize`, no `pytest.skip`/`xfail`/`.only`, no commented-out tests, no empty
   554	  bodies. Module docstring present (§3).
   555	- All asserted values are SPEC-exact literals (planning dates, fake-adapter strings,
   556	  node statuses, ticket states, report shapes), never weakened.
   557	```

    38	def _wait_enabled(page: Page, selector: str) -> None:
    39	    page.wait_for_function(
    40	        "sel => { const b = document.querySelector(sel); return !!b && !b.disabled; }",
    41	        arg=selector,
    42	        timeout=WAIT_MS,
    43	    )

    47	    page.wait_for_function(
    48	        "sel => document.querySelector(sel) !== null",
    49	        arg=selector,
    50	        timeout=WAIT_MS,
    51	    )

    28	    # to 400): the seam pins this exact body for T11's tests to compare whole.

    35	def _lazy(module_name: str, attr: str) -> Any | None:
    36	    # importlib (not a from-import) keeps the not-yet-existing T11 modules out of the
    37	    # static import graph; ImportError/AttributeError are the seam's named failures.
    38	    try:
    39	        module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    40	        return getattr(module, attr)
    41	    except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    42	        return None

     1	"""The boundary scheduler tick (§6.2) and the serialized latest-wins replan queue
     2	(§6.3, R5). One tick = run the idempotent boundary job for the current planning
     3	date, then drain the pending replan slot. The queue holds at most one pending
     4	request; a newer submission overwrites the older and invalidates any in-flight
     5	execution (generation check). Deterministic replan inputs are recomputed from the
     6	DB via boundary.py's reader helpers."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import sqlite3
    11	import threading
    12	from collections.abc import Callable
    13	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    14	from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
    15	from datetime import date, timedelta
    16	from functools import partial
    17	
    18	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryInputs
    19	from planner.core.adapters.registry import Adapters
    20	from planner.core.clock import Clock
    21	from planner.core.config import Config

    27	    _error_text,
    28	    _read_approval_candidates,
    29	    _read_overdue_candidates,
    30	    _read_yesterday_tickets,
    31	    run_boundary,
    32	)
    33	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanTree
    34	from planner.days.data import load_plan, store_plan
    35	from planner.days.logic.carryover import (
    36	    approvals_digest,

    38	    overdue_list,
    39	)
    40	from planner.days.logic.dates import planning_date

    42	from planner.days.logic.tree import as_proposed, tree_to_dict

    44	
    45	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    46	class _PendingReplan:
    47	    day_id: str
    48	    request: ReplanRequest
    49	    generation: int
    50	
    51	
    52	class _ReplanQueue:            # plain class, not a dataclass (holds a Lock)
    53	    def __init__(self) -> None:
    54	        self.lock = threading.Lock()
    55	        self.pending: _PendingReplan | None = None
    56	        self.generation = 0
    57	
    58	
    59	_QUEUE = _ReplanQueue()        # module singleton
    60	
    61	# A3: serializes the boundary read-guard-then-insert and single-flights the replan
    62	# consumer in-process. RLock so run_boundary_tick's nested process_pending_replan
    63	# re-enters. The generation check remains the backstop for supersession mid-flight.
    64	_TICK_MUTEX = threading.RLock()
    65	
    66	
    67	def submit_replan(day_id: str, request: ReplanRequest) -> None:
    68	    """Latest-wins enqueue: overwrite whatever was pending, bump the generation.
    69	    Never executes anything — T10's invalidate endpoints call this from the request
    70	    path; execution happens only in the consumer."""
    71	    with _QUEUE.lock:
    72	        _QUEUE.generation += 1
    73	        _QUEUE.pending = _PendingReplan(day_id, request, _QUEUE.generation)
    74	
    75	
    76	def reset_replan_queue() -> None:
    77	    """Test helper: clear the slot and reset the generation."""
    78	    with _TICK_MUTEX:
    79	        with _QUEUE.lock:
    80	            _QUEUE.pending = None
    81	            _QUEUE.generation = 0
    82	
    83	
    84	def _consume_if_current(generation: int) -> bool:
    85	    """Clear the slot iff it still holds the request with ``generation``."""
    86	    with _QUEUE.lock:
    87	        if _QUEUE.pending is not None and _QUEUE.pending.generation == generation:
    88	            _QUEUE.pending = None
    89	            return True
    90	        return False
    91	
    92	
    93	def _replan_inputs(conn: sqlite3.Connection, piso: str) -> BoundaryInputs:
    94	    """Recompute the deterministic §6.2 inputs for the day's planning date, reusing
    95	    boundary.py's private readers (RD-5)."""
    96	    yid = day_id(date.fromisoformat(piso) - timedelta(days=1))
    97	    carry = carryover_candidates(_read_yesterday_tickets(conn, yid))
    98	    overdue = overdue_list(*_read_overdue_candidates(conn), piso)
    99	    approvals = approvals_digest(*_read_approval_candidates(conn))
   100	    return BoundaryInputs(
   101	        planning_date=piso, carryover=carry, overdue=overdue, approvals_digest=approvals
   102	    )
   103	
   104	
   105	def _call_with_timeout[T](fn: Callable[[], T], timeout_s: int) -> T:
   106	    """Run ``fn`` in a worker thread; the CALLER owns the timeout. FuturesTimeout and
   107	    adapter raises both surface to the caller as exceptions."""
   108	    executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
   109	    try:
   110	        return executor.submit(fn).result(timeout=timeout_s)
   111	    finally:
   112	        executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
   113	
   114	
   115	def _execute_pending(
   116	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   117	    config: Config,
   118	    clock: Clock,
   119	    adapters: Adapters,
   120	    snapshot: _PendingReplan,
   121	) -> JsonDict:
   122	    """Execute one snapshot outside the queue lock, producing one attempt entry."""
   123	    now = clock.now_unix()
   124	    day = snapshot.day_id
   125	    piso = day.removeprefix("day_")
   126	    request = snapshot.request
   127	    scope: str
   128	    node: str | int
   129	    if isinstance(request, ReplanRoot):
   130	        scope, node = "root", "root"
   131	    else:
   132	        scope, node = "child", request.position
   133	
   134	    old_tree = load_plan(conn, day)
   135	
   136	    old_child: PlanNode | None = None
   137	    if isinstance(request, ReplanChild):
   138	        # Stale target (RD-7): no stored plan, or no child at the position by
   139	        # execution time. No adapter call, no event.
   140	        if old_tree is None:
   141	            _consume_if_current(snapshot.generation)
   142	            return {"day_id": day, "scope": scope, "node": node, "outcome": "stale_target"}
   143	        for child in old_tree.children:
   144	            if child.position == request.position:
   145	                old_child = child
   146	                break
   147	        if old_child is None:
   148	            _consume_if_current(snapshot.generation)
   149	            return {"day_id": day, "scope": scope, "node": node, "outcome": "stale_target"}
   150	
   151	    inputs = _replan_inputs(conn, piso)
   152	
   153	    # partial (not a lambda — ruff B023/E731); called per-branch so the generic
   154	    # _call_with_timeout binds a concrete return type, never a widened union.
   155	    adapter_result: PlanTree | PlanNode
   156	    try:
   157	        if isinstance(request, ReplanRoot):
   158	            adapter_result = _call_with_timeout(
   159	                partial(adapters.boundary.replan_root, day, inputs),
   160	                config.boundary_timeout_seconds,
   161	            )
   162	        else:
   163	            assert old_child is not None
   164	            adapter_result = _call_with_timeout(
   165	                partial(adapters.boundary.replan_child, day, old_child, inputs),
   166	                config.boundary_timeout_seconds,
   167	            )
   168	    except Exception as exc:
   169	        current = _consume_if_current(snapshot.generation)
   170	        if current:
   171	            append_event(
   172	                conn,
   173	                day,
   174	                EventKind.boundary_failed,
   175	                {"error": _error_text(exc, config.boundary_timeout_seconds)},
   176	                now,
   177	            )
   178	            outcome = "failed"
   179	        else:
   180	            # Superseded mid-flight: the generation check governs ALL writes (RD-6).
   181	            outcome = "discarded"
   182	        return {"day_id": day, "scope": scope, "node": node, "outcome": outcome}
   183	
   184	    current = _consume_if_current(snapshot.generation)
   185	    if not current:
   186	        return {"day_id": day, "scope": scope, "node": node, "outcome": "discarded"}
   187	
   188	    if isinstance(adapter_result, PlanTree):
   189	        new_tree = as_proposed(adapter_result)
   190	        store_plan(conn, day, new_tree, now)
   191	        append_event(
   192	            conn,
   193	            day,
   194	            EventKind.plan_replanned,
   195	            {
   196	                "old_tree": tree_to_dict(old_tree) if old_tree is not None else None,
   197	                "scope": "root",
   198	                "node": "root",
   199	            },
   200	            now,
   201	        )
   202	    else:
   203	        assert old_tree is not None
   204	        assert old_child is not None
   205	        spliced = [replace(child) for child in old_tree.children]
   206	        for index, child in enumerate(spliced):
   207	            if child.position == old_child.position:
   208	                spliced[index] = replace(
   209	                    adapter_result,
   210	                    status=NodeStatus.proposed,
   211	                    position=old_child.position,
   212	                )
   213	                break
   214	        store_plan(
   215	            conn, day, PlanTree(root=replace(old_tree.root), children=spliced), now
   216	        )
   217	        append_event(
   218	            conn,
   219	            day,
   220	            EventKind.plan_replanned,
   221	            {"old_tree": tree_to_dict(old_tree), "scope": "child", "node": old_child.position},
   222	            now,
   223	        )
   224	    return {"day_id": day, "scope": scope, "node": node, "outcome": "stored"}
   225	
   226	
   227	def process_pending_replan(
   228	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, config: Config, clock: Clock, adapters: Adapters
   229	) -> JsonDict | None:
   230	    """Single consumer. Drain the slot until empty (RD-20); a submission that arrives
   231	    during execution is consumed by the next iteration. Returns None when nothing was
   232	    pending."""
   233	    with _TICK_MUTEX:
   234	        attempts: list[JsonDict] = []
   235	        while True:
   236	            with _QUEUE.lock:
   237	                snapshot = _QUEUE.pending
   238	            if snapshot is None:
   239	                break
   240	            attempts.append(_execute_pending(conn, config, clock, adapters, snapshot))
   241	        if not attempts:
   242	            return None
   243	        return {"attempts": attempts}
   244	
   245	
   246	def run_boundary_tick(
   247	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
   248	    config: Config,
   249	    clock: Clock,
   250	    adapters: Adapters,
   251	) -> JsonDict:
   252	    """One boundary tick. Runs the idempotent boundary job for the snapshot planning
   253	    date (A6), then drains the replan queue. Returns the pinned four-key report."""
   254	    with _TICK_MUTEX:
   255	        piso = planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour).isoformat()
   256	        conn = conn_factory()
   257	        try:
   258	            existed = _boundary_ran(conn, piso)
   259	            run_boundary(conn, clock, config, adapters.boundary)
   260	            row = conn.execute(
   261	                "SELECT judgment FROM boundary_runs WHERE planning_date=?", (piso,)
   262	            ).fetchone()
   263	            judgment = str(row["judgment"]) if row is not None else None
   264	            ran = (not existed) and row is not None
   265	            replan = process_pending_replan(conn, config, clock, adapters)
   266	        finally:
   267	            conn.close()
   268	        return {
   269	            "planning_date": piso,
   270	            "ran": ran,
   271	            "judgment": judgment,
   272	            "replan": replan,
   273	        }

     1	"""The boundary job (§6.2). Runs once per planning date, guarded by the
     2	boundary_runs table. Deterministic pass (materialize the day, carryover, overdue,
     3	approvals, close yesterday) then one adapter judgment call wrapped in a
     4	caller-owned timeout. On failure/timeout the day survives with empty brief and no
     5	plan. Judgment is skipped entirely when the human already planned the day."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import sqlite3
    11	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    12	from concurrent.futures import TimeoutError as FuturesTimeout
    13	from datetime import timedelta
    14	from typing import Any
    15	
    16	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryAdapter, BoundaryInputs, BoundaryJudgment
    17	from planner.core.clock import Clock
    18	from planner.core.config import Config
    19	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    20	from planner.core.events import append_event
    21	from planner.core.ids import day_id
    22	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus
    23	from planner.days.data import load_plan, materialize_day, store_judgment
    24	from planner.days.logic.carryover import (
    25	    approvals_digest,
    26	    carryover_candidates,
    27	    day_ticket_counts,
    28	    overdue_list,
    29	)
    30	from planner.days.logic.dates import planning_date
    31	from planner.days.logic.tree import as_proposed, tree_to_dict
    32	
    33	
    34	def run_boundary(
    35	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, clock: Clock, config: Config, adapter: BoundaryAdapter
    36	) -> None:
    37	    """One boundary tick. Idempotent per planning date via the boundary_runs
    38	    guard, so the stage-4 scheduler may call it every tick — only the first tick
    39	    that advances the planning date does work (planning_date only becomes the new
    40	    date at/after boundary_hour, satisfying §6.2 'first tick at/after the boundary
    41	    hour')."""
    42	    pd = planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour)
    43	    piso = pd.isoformat()
    44	    if _boundary_ran(conn, piso):
    45	        return
    46	
    47	    now = clock.now_unix()
    48	    ndid = day_id(pd)
    49	    yid = day_id(pd - timedelta(days=1))
    50	
    51	    # Deterministic pass — always runs on a first tick.
    52	    materialize_day(conn, ndid, now)
    53	    yts = _read_yesterday_tickets(conn, yid)
    54	    carry = carryover_candidates(yts)
    55	    overdue_tickets, overdue_items = _read_overdue_candidates(conn)
    56	    overdue = overdue_list(overdue_tickets, overdue_items, piso)
    57	    approval_tickets, approval_items = _read_approval_candidates(conn)
    58	    approvals = approvals_digest(approval_tickets, approval_items)
    59	    done, not_done = day_ticket_counts(yts)
    60	    append_event(
    61	        conn,
    62	        yid,
    63	        EventKind.day_closed,
    64	        {"done_count": done, "not_done_count": not_done},
    65	        now,
    66	    )
    67	
    68	    # Skip check (§6.2 last paragraph): a human already planned this day.
    69	    if _human_planned(conn, ndid):
    70	        _record_boundary(conn, piso, now, "skipped")
    71	        return
    72	
    73	    # Judgment pass — one adapter call under a caller-owned timeout.
    74	    inputs = BoundaryInputs(
    75	        planning_date=piso, carryover=carry, overdue=overdue, approvals_digest=approvals
    76	    )
    77	    timeout_s = config.boundary_timeout_seconds
    78	    try:
    79	        judgment = _judgment_with_timeout(adapter, inputs, timeout_s)
    80	    except Exception as exc:  # timeout OR any adapter raise; day survives empty
    81	        append_event(
    82	            conn, ndid, EventKind.boundary_failed, {"error": _error_text(exc, timeout_s)}, now
    83	        )
    84	        _record_boundary(conn, piso, now, "failed")
    85	        return
    86	
    87	    proposed = as_proposed(judgment.plan_tree)  # §6.2 stored as a PROPOSED plan
    88	    store_judgment(conn, ndid, judgment.brief_markdown, proposed, now)
    89	    append_event(conn, ndid, EventKind.plan_proposed, {"tree": tree_to_dict(proposed)}, now)
    90	    _record_boundary(conn, piso, now, "ok")
    91	
    92	
    93	def _boundary_ran(conn: sqlite3.Connection, pd_iso: str) -> bool:
    94	    return conn.execute(
    95	        "SELECT 1 FROM boundary_runs WHERE planning_date = ?", (pd_iso,)
    96	    ).fetchone() is not None
    97

    99	def _record_boundary(conn: sqlite3.Connection, pd_iso: str, now_unix: int, judgment: str) -> None:
   100	    conn.execute(
   101	        "INSERT INTO boundary_runs (planning_date, ran_at, judgment) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   102	        (pd_iso, now_unix, judgment),
   103	    )

   106	def _read_yesterday_tickets(conn: sqlite3.Connection, yesterday_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   107	    rows = conn.execute(
   108	        "SELECT t.id AS id, t.title AS title, t.state AS state, t.priority AS priority "
   109	        "FROM day_tickets dt JOIN tickets t ON t.id = dt.ticket_id "
   110	        "WHERE dt.day_id = ? ORDER BY dt.position",
   111	        (yesterday_id,),

   114	        {"id": r["id"], "title": r["title"], "state": r["state"], "priority": r["priority"]}
   115	        for r in rows
   116	    ]

   120	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   121	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   122	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
   123	        {

   125	            "title": r["title"],
   126	            "state": r["state"],
   127	            "priority": r["priority"],
   128	            "deadline": r["deadline"],
   129	        }
   130	        for r in conn.execute(
   131	            "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline FROM tickets"
   132	        ).fetchall()
   133	    ]
   134	    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
   135	        {
   136	            "id": r["id"],
   137	            "title": r["title"],
   138	            "status": r["status"],
   139	            "priority": r["priority"],
   140	            "deadline": r["deadline"],
   141	        }
   142	        for r in conn.execute(
   143	            "SELECT id, title, status, priority, deadline FROM sprint_items"
   144	        ).fetchall()
   145	    ]
   146	    return tickets, items
   147

   149	def _read_approval_candidates(
   150	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   151	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   152	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
   153	        {
   154	            "id": r["id"],
   155	            "state": r["state"],
   156	            "fields": json.loads(r["fields"]),
   157	            "updated_at": r["updated_at"],
   158	        }
   159	        for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets").fetchall()
   160	    ]
   161	    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   162	    for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, status_proposal FROM sprint_items").fetchall():
   163	        raw = r["status_proposal"]
   164	        items.append(
   165	            {"id": r["id"], "status_proposal": json.loads(raw) if raw is not None else None}
   166	        )
   167	    return tickets, items
   168	
   169	
   170	def _human_planned(conn: sqlite3.Connection, new_day_id: str) -> bool:
   171	    """True iff a day-ticket exists on the new day OR the stored plan has the root
   172	    accepted or any child accepted (§6.2 'any day-ticket or accepted plan')."""
   173	    if conn.execute(
   174	        "SELECT 1 FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? LIMIT 1", (new_day_id,)
   175	    ).fetchone() is not None:
   176	        return True
   177	    tree = load_plan(conn, new_day_id)
   178	    if tree is None:
   179	        return False
   180	    if tree.root.status == NodeStatus.accepted:
   181	        return True
   182	    return any(child.status == NodeStatus.accepted for child in tree.children)
   183	
   184	
   185	def _judgment_with_timeout(
   186	    adapter: BoundaryAdapter, inputs: BoundaryInputs, timeout_s: int
   187	) -> BoundaryJudgment:
   188	    """Run adapter.judgment in a worker thread; the CALLER owns the timeout
   189	    (base.py). result(timeout=...) raises FuturesTimeout on timeout and re-raises
   190	    any adapter exception — both are caught by run_boundary. shutdown(wait=False)
   191	    so a hung adapter cannot block run_boundary past the configured timeout; the
   192	    abandoned worker thread is left to finish on its own."""
   193	    executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
   194	    try:
   195	        return executor.submit(adapter.judgment, inputs).result(timeout=timeout_s)
   196	    finally:
   197	        executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
   198	
   199	
   200	def _error_text(exc: Exception, timeout_s: int) -> str:
   201	    if isinstance(exc, FuturesTimeout):
   202	        return f"boundary judgment timed out after {timeout_s}s"
   203	    return str(exc) or exc.__class__.__name__

    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)

    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }

    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )

    93	def _load_plan_or_error(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str) -> PlanTree:

    95	    if plan is None:
    96	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "day has no plan", {"day_id": did})

   100	def _require_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> None:
   101	    if not any(child.position == position for child in tree.children):
   102	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no such plan node", {"node": position})

     1	"""In-memory fakes for the three adapters. Tests use these; they never touch the
     2	OS or the network. Each records its calls and behaves deterministically."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	from dataclasses import dataclass, field

    18	_FIRST_FAKE_PID = 90001
    19	
    20	
    21	@dataclass
    22	class FakeSpawnAdapter:
    23	    calls: list[SpawnRequest] = field(default_factory=list)
    24	    results: list[SpawnResult] = field(default_factory=list)  # scripted, popped FIFO
    25	    next_pid: int = _FIRST_FAKE_PID
    26	
    27	    def spawn(self, request: SpawnRequest) -> SpawnResult:
    28	        self.calls.append(request)
    29	        if self.results:
    30	            return self.results.pop(0)
    31	        pid = self.next_pid
    32	        self.next_pid += 1
    33	        return SpawnResult(ok=True, pid=pid)
    34

    60	        return BoundaryJudgment(
    61	            brief_markdown=f"# Brief for {inputs.planning_date}", plan_tree=tree
    62	        )
    63

    70	        return PlanTree(root=PlanRoot(focus="Fake replanned focus"), children=[])

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The dispatcher tick (§7.1). One synchronous tick: fail-safe dispatch_enabled
     2	re-read, machine-wide advisory flock, reclaim sweep, run-timeout enforcement,
     3	eligibility recompute, claim-then-spawn up to the concurrency cap. In test mode no
     4	code path ever signals a real pid (fake pids may collide with live processes). The
     5	lock is acquired once and held by the process; ``release_dispatcher_lock`` is the
     6	explicit release for loops.stop() and tests."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import fcntl
    11	import os
    12	import signal
    13	import sqlite3
    14	from collections.abc import Callable
    15	from pathlib import Path
    16	from typing import Final
    17	
    18	from planner.core.adapters.base import SpawnRequest, SpawnResult
    19	from planner.core.adapters.registry import Adapters
    20	from planner.core.clock import Clock
    21	from planner.core.config import HOST, Config, read_dispatch_enabled
    22	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    23	from planner.core.errors import PlannerError
    24	from planner.dispatch.contracts import RunStatus
    25	from planner.dispatch.data import claim, close_run, load_candidates, sweep_reclaims
    26	from planner.dispatch.logic import is_eligible, ordering_key
    27	
    28	ConnFactory = Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection]
    29	
    30	# One cached OS fd per lock path. The first successful acquisition caches the fd; the
    31	# process holds the flock from then on (released only on process exit or explicit
    32	# release).
    33	_LOCK_FDS: Final[dict[str, int]] = {}
    34	
    35	
    36	def _ensure_dispatcher_lock(lock_path: str) -> bool:
    37	    """Acquire (once) the machine-wide advisory lock; True when this process holds it."""
    38	    if lock_path in _LOCK_FDS:
    39	        return True
    40	    Path(lock_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    41	    fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o644)
    42	    try:
    43	        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    44	    except BlockingIOError:
    45	        os.close(fd)
    46	        return False
    47	    _LOCK_FDS[lock_path] = fd
    48	    return True
    49	
    50	
    51	def release_dispatcher_lock(lock_path: str) -> None:
    52	    """Explicit release used by loops.stop() and tests. Safe no-op when not held."""
    53	    fd = _LOCK_FDS.pop(lock_path, None)
    54	    if fd is not None:
    55	        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    56	        os.close(fd)
    57	
    58	
    59	def _pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    60	    """Real-mode liveness probe via signal 0."""
    61	    try:
    62	        os.kill(pid, 0)
    63	    except ProcessLookupError:
    64	        return False
    65	    except PermissionError:
    66	        return True
    67	    return True
    68	
    69	
    70	def _pid_alive_always(_pid: int) -> bool:
    71	    """Test-mode liveness probe: never signals, always reports alive."""
    72	    return True
    73	
    74	
    75	def _sigterm(pid: int) -> None:
    76	    """Real-mode kill: SIGTERM, guarded against an already-gone pid."""
    77	    try:
    78	        os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    79	    except ProcessLookupError:
    80	        pass
    81	
    82	
    83	def _kill_noop(_pid: int) -> None:
    84	    """Test-mode kill: never signals."""
    85	    return None
    86	
    87	
    88	def _enforce_run_timeouts(
    89	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    90	    now: int,
    91	    run_max_seconds: int,
    92	    failure_limit: int,
    93	    kill: Callable[[int], None],
    94	) -> list[str]:
    95	    """Close every running run that has exceeded ``run_max_seconds`` (fires at exactly
    96	    the boundary). A5: close FIRST (guarded by a PlannerError skip); only the run whose
    97	    close won the status CAS is then signalled and reported."""
    98	    rows = conn.execute(
    99	        "SELECT id, pid FROM runs WHERE status='running' AND started_at <= ?",
   100	        (now - run_max_seconds,),
   101	    ).fetchall()
   102	    closed: list[str] = []
   103	    for row in rows:
   104	        run_id = str(row["id"])
   105	        pid = int(row["pid"]) if row["pid"] is not None else None
   106	        try:
   107	            close_run(
   108	                conn,
   109	                run_id,
   110	                RunStatus.timed_out,
   111	                now,
   112	                failure_limit,
   113	                error=f"exceeded run_max_seconds ({run_max_seconds}s)",
   114	            )
   115	        except PlannerError:
   116	            continue
   117	        if pid is not None:
   118	            kill(pid)
   119	        closed.append(run_id)
   120	    return closed
   121	
   122	
   123	def run_tick(
   124	    conn_factory: ConnFactory, config: Config, clock: Clock, adapters: Adapters
   125	) -> JsonDict:
   126	    """One §7.1-ordered dispatcher tick. Returns the pinned five-key report."""
   127	    report: JsonDict = {
   128	        "skipped": None,
   129	        "reclaimed": [],
   130	        "timed_out": [],
   131	        "spawned": [],
   132	        "spawn_failed": [],
   133	    }
   134	
   135	    # (0) fail-safe dispatch flag — before the lock and before any connection.
   136	    if not read_dispatch_enabled():
   137	        report["skipped"] = "dispatch_disabled"
   138	        return report
   139	
   140	    # (0b) machine-wide advisory lock — a skipped tick opens no connection.
   141	    if not _ensure_dispatcher_lock(config.dispatcher_lock_path):
   142	        report["skipped"] = "lock_held"
   143	        return report
   144	
   145	    conn = conn_factory()
   146	    try:
   147	        now = clock.now_unix()
   148	
   149	        # (1) reclaim expired claims / dead pids.
   150	        pid_alive = _pid_alive_always if config.test_mode else _pid_alive
   151	        report["reclaimed"] = sweep_reclaims(conn, now, pid_alive, config.failure_limit)
   152	
   153	        # (2) enforce per-run max runtime.
   154	        kill = _kill_noop if config.test_mode else _sigterm
   155	        report["timed_out"] = _enforce_run_timeouts(
   156	            conn, now, config.run_max_seconds, config.failure_limit, kill
   157	        )
   158	
   159	        # (3) recompute eligibility, ordered.
   160	        candidates = load_candidates(conn, now)
   161	        eligible = sorted((c for c in candidates if is_eligible(c)), key=ordering_key)
   162	
   163	        # (4) claim-then-spawn up to the concurrency cap.
   164	        active = int(
   165	            conn.execute(
   166	                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE status='running'"
   167	            ).fetchone()["n"]
   168	        )
   169	        budget = max(config.max_runs - active, 0)
   170	        Path(config.logs_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   171	        for cand in eligible:
   172	            if budget == 0:
   173	                break
   174	            budget -= 1  # every claim attempt consumes one unit, win or lose (RD-2).
   175	            claimed = claim(conn, cand.ticket_id, now, config.claim_ttl_seconds)
   176	            if claimed is None:
   177	                continue
   178	            run_id, token = claimed
   179	            request = SpawnRequest(
   180	                ticket_id=cand.ticket_id,
   181	                run_id=run_id,
   182	                claim=token,
   183	                server_url=f"http://{HOST}:{config.port}",
   184	                log_path=str(Path(config.logs_dir) / f"{run_id}.log"),
   185	                hermes_bin=config.hermes_bin,
   186	                profile=config.hermes_profile,
   187	                skill=config.worker_skill,
   188	            )
   189	            try:
   190	                result = adapters.spawn.spawn(request)
   191	            except Exception as exc:  # adapter raise = spawn failure (RD-9).
   192	                result = SpawnResult(ok=False, error=str(exc))
   193	            if result.ok:
   194	                conn.execute(
   195	                    "UPDATE runs SET pid=? WHERE id=?", (result.pid, run_id)
   196	                )
   197	                report["spawned"].append(
   198	                    {"ticket_id": cand.ticket_id, "run_id": run_id, "pid": result.pid}
   199	                )
   200	            else:
   201	                close_run(
   202	                    conn,
   203	                    run_id,
   204	                    RunStatus.spawn_failed,
   205	                    now,
   206	                    config.failure_limit,
   207	                    error=result.error or "spawn failed",
   208	                )
   209	                report["spawn_failed"].append(
   210	                    {
   211	                        "ticket_id": cand.ticket_id,
   212	                        "run_id": run_id,
   213	                        "error": result.error or "spawn failed",
   214	                    }
   215	                )
   216	    finally:
   217	        conn.close()
   218	    return report

     1	"""Run routes (§9): heartbeat and close. Both require the claim headers — a request
     2	with no claim headers fails inside require_claim with stale_claim/missing_header.
     3	require_claim takes a ticket id, so each route resolves run -> ticket_id first via the
     4	run reader homed in tickets/views. close restricts the outcome to the agent-reportable

    12	from pydantic import BaseModel

    25	class CloseRunBody(BaseModel):

    29	
    30	@router.post("/runs/{run_id}/heartbeat")
    31	async def heartbeat(run_id: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
    32	    now = clk.now_unix()
    33	    run = tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id)
    34	    if run is None:
    35	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
    36	    require_claim(conn, ctx, run["ticket_id"], now)
    37	    with txn(conn):
    38	        new_expires = dispatch_data.heartbeat(conn, run_id, now, cfg.claim_ttl_seconds)
    39	    return {"run": tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id), "claim_expires": new_expires}
    40	
    41	
    42	@router.post("/runs/{run_id}/close")
    43	async def close_run(run_id: str, body: CloseRunBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
    44	                    clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
    45	    now = clk.now_unix()
    46	    run = tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id)
    47	    if run is None:
    48	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
    49	    require_claim(conn, ctx, run["ticket_id"], now)
    50	    status = parse_enum(RunStatus, body.outcome, "outcome")
    51	    if status not in AGENT_CLOSE_OUTCOMES:
    52	        raise PlannerError(
    53	            ErrorCode.validation, "outcome must be done or blocked", {"outcome": body.outcome}
    54	        )
    55	    with txn(conn):
    56	        dispatch_data.close_run(conn, run_id, status, now, cfg.failure_limit, summary=body.summary)
    57	    return {"run": tickets_views.get_run(conn, run_id)}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§7.2 dispatch eligibility over a pure DispatchCandidate, plus the gating-field
     2	pending check the data layer feeds it. Stdlib + contracts only; logic never touches
     3	the DB."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from typing import Final
     8	
     9	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    10	from planner.dispatch.contracts import DispatchCandidate
    11	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    12	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    13	    GATING_FIELD,
    14	    STATE_ORDER,
    15	    AtCap,
    16	    TicketState,
    17	)
    18	
    19	# Position of each state in the linear pipeline; drives the "<= ceiling" comparison.
    20	_ORDER_INDEX: Final[dict[TicketState, int]] = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(STATE_ORDER)}
    21	
    22	
    23	def gating_field_pending(state: TicketState, fields: JsonDict) -> bool:
    24	    """True iff the state's gating field carries a pending proposal in the parsed
    25	    tickets.fields JSON. States without a gating field (needs_review, done, dropped)
    26	    return False."""
    27	    name = GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    28	    if name is None:
    29	        return False
    30	    slot = fields.get(name.value)
    31	    if not isinstance(slot, dict):
    32	        return False
    33	    return slot.get("proposal") is not None
    34	
    35	
    36	def is_eligible(candidate: DispatchCandidate) -> bool:
    37	    """§7.2 (SPEC line 149): the dispatch-eligibility conjunction plus the two
    38	    advance branches. Checks run in the order below; the terminal-state guard also
    39	    guarantees ADVANCE_TARGET[state] below never KeyErrors (the four remaining states
    40	    are exactly its keys)."""
    41	    if candidate.state in (
    42	        TicketState.done,
    43	        TicketState.dropped,
    44	        TicketState.needs_review,
    45	    ):
    46	        return False
    47	    if candidate.is_blocked:            # §3.6 line 57
    48	        return False
    49	    if candidate.auto_blocked:          # §7.5
    50	        return False
    51	    if candidate.has_active_claim:
    52	        return False
    53	    if candidate.gating_pending:        # current gating field has a pending proposal
    54	        return False
    55	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET[candidate.state]
    56	    if _ORDER_INDEX[target] <= _ORDER_INDEX[candidate.ceiling]:
    57	        return True                     # branch (a): the agent can move it
    58	    # branch (b): at ceiling + propose eligible; at ceiling + stop and past-ceiling not.
    59	    return candidate.state == candidate.ceiling and candidate.at_cap is AtCap.propose

    65	async def db_conn(request: Request) -> AsyncIterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    66	    conn = cast(Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection], request.app.state.conn_factory)()
    67	    try:
    68	        yield conn
    69	    finally:
    70	        conn.close()
