    78	
    79	@contextmanager
    80	def txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    81	    """Wrap a NON-self-transacting writer (days/dispatch/links) so a mid-sequence
    82	    PlannerError rolls back cleanly. Never wrap a ticket/sprint writer — those open
    83	    their own BEGIN IMMEDIATE and would raise 'transaction within a transaction'."""
    84	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    85	    try:
    86	        yield
    87	    except BaseException:
    88	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    89	        raise
    90	    else:
    91	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    92	
    93	
    94	def parse_enum[E: StrEnum](enum_cls: type[E], raw: str, what: str) -> E:
    95	    try:
    96	        return enum_cls(raw)
    97	    except ValueError:
    98	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {what}", {what: raw}) from None

   149	def _parse_next_ceiling(raw: str | None) -> NextCeiling | None:
   150	    if raw is None:
   151	        return None
   152	    if raw == NO_FURTHER:
   153	        return NO_FURTHER
   154	    try:
   155	        return TicketState(raw)
   156	    except ValueError:
   157	        raise PlannerError(
   158	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": raw}
   159	        ) from None
   160

   165	    try:

   167	    except ValueError:

   175	               title_max_chars: int, now: int) -> Ticket:

   178	        ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   179	        prev = ticket.title
   180	        conn.execute(
   181	            "UPDATE tickets SET title = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (title, now, ticket_id)
   182	        )
   183	        append_event(
   184	            conn,
   185	            ticket_id,
   186	            EventKind.ticket_updated,
   187	            {"field": "title", "from": prev, "to": title},
   188	            now,
   189	        )
   190	        return tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)

   193	def _set_project(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, project: Project | None, *,
   194	                 now: int) -> Ticket:
   195	    with txn(conn):
   196	        ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   197	        if ticket.sprint_item_id is not None:
   198	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is derived when parented")
   199	        prev = ticket.project.value if ticket.project is not None else None
   200	        new_value = project.value if project is not None else None
   201	        conn.execute(
   202	            "UPDATE tickets SET project = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   203	            (new_value, now, ticket_id),
   204	        )
   205	        append_event(
   206	            conn,
   207	            ticket_id,
   208	            EventKind.ticket_updated,
   209	            {"field": "project", "from": prev, "to": new_value},
   210	            now,
   211	        )
   212	        return tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)

   263	@router.patch("/tickets/{ticket_id}")
   264	async def patch_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   265	                       cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   266	    recognized = ("title", "priority", "deadline", "project", "sprint_id")
   267	    for key in body:
   268	        if key not in recognized:
   269	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown ticket field", {"field": key})
   270	    if not body:
   271	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no ticket fields to update", {})
   272	    now = clk.now_unix()
   273	    if "title" in body:
   274	        _set_title(conn, ticket_id, body["title"], title_max_chars=cfg.title_max_chars, now=now)
   275	    if "priority" in body:
   276	        priority = parse_enum(Priority, body["priority"], "priority")
   277	        tickets_data.set_priority(conn, ticket_id, priority=priority, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   278	    if "deadline" in body:
   279	        tickets_data.set_deadline(conn, ticket_id, deadline=body["deadline"], actor=ctx.actor,
   280	                                  now=now)
   281	    if "project" in body:
   282	        project = parse_enum(Project, body["project"], "project") \
   283	            if body["project"] is not None else None
   284	        _set_project(conn, ticket_id, project, now=now)
   285	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   286	        tickets_data.set_sprint(conn, ticket_id, sprint_id=body["sprint_id"], actor=ctx.actor,
   287	                                now=now)
   288	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id), now)

   291	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/propose/{field}")

   304	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/accept/{field}")
   305	async def accept_field(ticket_id: str, field: str, body: AcceptBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   306	                       clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   307	    reject_agents(ctx)
   308	    field_enum = parse_enum(FieldName, field, "field")
   309	    now = clk.now_unix()
   310	    next_ceiling = _parse_next_ceiling(body.next_ceiling)
   311	    at_cap = _parse_grant_at_cap(body.at_cap)
   312	    ticket = tickets_data.accept_proposal(
   313	        conn,
   314	        ticket_id,
   315	        field=field_enum,
   316	        actor=ctx.actor,
   317	        now=now,
   318	        edited_body=body.edited_body,
   319	        next_ceiling=next_ceiling,
   320	        at_cap=at_cap,
   321	    )
   322	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   323	
   324	
   325	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/approve")
   326	async def approve_ticket(ticket_id: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   327	    reject_agents(ctx)
   328	    now = clk.now_unix()
   329	    ticket = tickets_data.approve_review(conn, ticket_id, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   330	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   331	
   332	
   333	@router.put("/tickets/{ticket_id}/notes/{field}")
   334	async def put_notes(ticket_id: str, field: str, body: NoteBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   335	                    clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   336	    field_enum = parse_enum(FieldName, field, "field")
   337	    now = clk.now_unix()
   338	    if ctx.is_claimed_agent:
   339	        require_claim(conn, ctx, ticket_id, now)
   340	    ticket = tickets_data.set_note(
   341	        conn, ticket_id, field=field_enum, note=body.note, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   342	    )
   343	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   344	
   345	
   346	@router.put("/tickets/{ticket_id}/recap")
   347	async def put_recap(ticket_id: str, body: RecapBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   348	                    clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   349	    now = clk.now_unix()
   350	    if ctx.is_claimed_agent:
   351	        require_claim(conn, ctx, ticket_id, now)
   352	    ticket = tickets_data.write_recap(conn, ticket_id, body=body.body, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   353	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   354	
   355	
   356	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/grant")
   357	async def grant_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: GrantBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   358	                       clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   359	    reject_agents(ctx)
   360	    now = clk.now_unix()
   361	    if body.ceiling is None or body.at_cap is None:
   362	        missing = [name for name, raw in (("ceiling", body.ceiling), ("at_cap", body.at_cap))
   363	                   if raw is None]
   364	        raise PlannerError(
   365	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "grant requires ceiling and at_cap", {"missing": missing}
   366	        )
   367	    try:
   368	        ceiling = TicketState(body.ceiling)
   369	    except ValueError:
   370	        raise PlannerError(
   371	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown ceiling", {"ceiling": body.ceiling}
   372	        ) from None
   373	    try:
   374	        at_cap = AtCap(body.at_cap)
   375	    except ValueError:
   376	        raise PlannerError(
   377	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": body.at_cap}
   378	        ) from None
   379	    ticket = tickets_data.change_grant(
   380	        conn, ticket_id, ceiling=ceiling, at_cap=at_cap, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   381	    )
   382	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   383	
   384	
   385	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/state")
   386	async def set_state(ticket_id: str, body: StateBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   387	                    clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   388	    reject_agents(ctx)
   389	    now = clk.now_unix()
   390	    to_state = parse_enum(TicketState, body.to, "state")
   391	    ticket = tickets_data.set_state(conn, ticket_id, new_state=to_state, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   392	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   393	
   394	
   395	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/drop")
   396	async def drop_ticket(ticket_id: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   397	    reject_agents(ctx)
   398	    now = clk.now_unix()
   399	    ticket = tickets_data.drop_ticket(conn, ticket_id, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   400	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)
   401	
   402	
   403	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/unblock")
   404	async def unblock_ticket(ticket_id: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   405	    reject_agents(ctx)
   406	    now = clk.now_unix()
   407	    with txn(conn):
   408	        dispatch_data.clear_auto_block(conn, ticket_id, now)
   409	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id), now)

   432	    now = clk.now_unix()
   433	    with txn(conn):
   434	        core_links.add_link(conn, body.from_id, body.to_id, kind, now)

   441	    now = clk.now_unix()
   442	    with txn(conn):
   443	        core_links.remove_link(conn, from_id, to_id, kind_enum, now)
   444	    return {"ok": True}

   453	async def queues(conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   454	    now = clk.now_unix()
   455	    today_iso = planning_date(clk.now(), cfg.boundary_hour).isoformat()
   456	    item_approval_rows = sprints_views.approval_item_rows(conn)

     1	"""The `plan` CLI (§8). A single entry point speaking HTTP to the server. The CLI
     2	is for agents and developer debugging; the human operates through the UI. No
     3	resolution verbs exist here (accept/approve/grant/unblock/day-plan are UI/API
     4	only).
     5	
     6	Every verb supports --json (machine output; exit codes 0 success, 1
     7	validation/domain error, 2 connection error). Long text arrives via
     8	`--body-file <path>` where `-` reads stdin — body text is never an inline
     9	argument. Every handler speaks HTTP through planner.cli.http, which owns all
    10	output and exit-code decisions; a handler only builds a route + body."""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import os
    15	import sqlite3
    16	import sys
    17	from collections.abc import Callable
    18	from pathlib import Path
    19	from typing import Any
    20	
    21	import click
    22	from click.core import ParameterSource
    23	
    24	from planner.cli import http
    25	
    26	_PRIORITIES = ["P0", "P1", "P2", "P3"]
    27	_FIELDS = ["success", "approach", "plan", "result"]
    28	_LINK_KINDS = ["belongs_to", "parent_child", "blocks", "relates"]
    29	_TICKET_ID_ENV = "PLAN_TICKET_ID"
    30	
    31	
    32	# --- body input, id resolution, and render helpers (amendment 6) ---------------
    33	
    34	
    35	def _read_source(spec: str, as_json: bool) -> str:
    36	    """`-` -> stdin; else read the file. Unreadable file -> validation exit 1."""
    37	    if spec == "-":
    38	        return sys.stdin.read()
    39	    try:
    40	        return Path(spec).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    41	    except OSError:
    42	        http.fail_validation(f"cannot read body file: {spec}", as_json)
    43	
    44	
    45	def _positional_is_stdin_marker(positional: str | None) -> bool:
    46	    """True only for a '-' the caller actually typed. click fills the positional from
    47	    PLAN_TICKET_ID too, and an env value of '-' is a bogus ticket id, not a stdin
    48	    request (amendment 6: body sources are explicit only)."""
    49	    if positional != "-":
    50	        return False
    51	    source = click.get_current_context().get_parameter_source("ticket_id")
    52	    return source == ParameterSource.COMMANDLINE
    53	
    54	
    55	def read_body(positional_ticket_id: str | None, body_file: str | None, as_json: bool) -> str:
    56	    """Required-body verbs. Body source is explicit only: --body-file PATH (or -), or a
    57	    trailing positional that is literally '-' (the stdin marker, not a ticket id).
    58	    Whitespace-only body -> validation exit 1. Returns the original unstripped text."""
    59	    if body_file is not None:
    60	        text = _read_source(body_file, as_json)
    61	    elif _positional_is_stdin_marker(positional_ticket_id):
    62	        text = sys.stdin.read()
    63	    else:
    64	        http.fail_validation("body required: pass '-' for stdin or --body-file PATH", as_json)
    65	    if not text.strip():
    66	        http.fail_validation("empty body", as_json)
    67	    return text
    68	
    69	
    70	def read_optional_body(body_file: str | None, as_json: bool) -> str | None:
    71	    """Optional-body verbs. Body comes only from --body-file (which may be '-');
    72	    absent flag -> None."""
    73	    if body_file is None:
    74	        return None
    75	    return _read_source(body_file, as_json)
    76	
    77	
    78	def resolve_ticket_id(positional: str | None, as_json: bool) -> str:
    79	    """Effective ticket id. A real positional wins; a None or '-' positional falls back
    80	    to PLAN_TICKET_ID (re-read here since click's envvar fallback does not fire when the
    81	    positional is the '-' stdin marker). Missing -> validation exit 1."""
    82	    if positional is not None and positional != "-":
    83	        return positional
    84	    env = os.environ.get(_TICKET_ID_ENV, "").strip()
    85	    if env and env != "-":  # '-' is the stdin marker, never a ticket id
    86	        return env
    87	    http.fail_validation("ticket id required: pass it or set PLAN_TICKET_ID", as_json)
    88	
    89	
    90	def _drop_none(d: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    91	    return {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v is not None}
    92	
    93	
    94	def _lines(rows: list[Any], fmt: Callable[[Any], str]) -> str:
    95	    return "\n".join(fmt(r) for r in rows) if rows else "(none)"
    96	
    97	
    98	def _seed_human(data: Any) -> str:
    99	    lines = [
   100	        f"seed: {data['sprints']} sprint(s), {data['sprint_items']} item(s), "
   101	        f"{data['deferred_items']} deferred, {data['tickets']} ticket(s), "
   102	        f"{data['ideas']} idea(s), {data['links']} link(s), "
   103	        f"{data['duplicates_skipped']} duplicate(s)"
   104	    ]
   105	    skipped = data["skipped"]
   106	    if skipped:
   107	        lines.append(f"skipped: {len(skipped)}")
   108	        for s in skipped:
   109	            lines.append(f"  - {s['source_file']} [{s['heading']}]: {s['reason']}")
   110	    return "\n".join(lines)
   111	
   112	
   113	def _require_run_id(as_json: bool) -> str:
   114	    rid = os.environ.get("PLAN_RUN_ID", "").strip()
   115	    if not rid:
   116	        http.fail_validation("run id required: set PLAN_RUN_ID", as_json)
   117	    return rid
   118	
   119	
   120	def json_option(func: Callable[..., Any]) -> Callable[..., Any]:
   121	    return click.option(
   122	        "--json",
   123	        "as_json",
   124	        is_flag=True,
   125	        default=False,
   126	        help="Emit machine-readable JSON (errors as JSON on stderr).",
   127	    )(func)
   128	
   129	
   130	@click.group()
   131	def main() -> None:
   132	    """plan — the planner CLI for agents and developer debugging."""
   133	
   134	
   135	# --- serve (the one handler that actually works in T01) ---
   136	
   137	
   138	@main.command("serve")
   139	@json_option
   140	def serve(as_json: bool) -> None:
   141	    """Run the server (foreground)."""
   142	    import os
   143	
   144	    import uvicorn
   145	
   146	    from planner.core.adapters.registry import build_adapters
   147	    from planner.core.clock import build_clock
   148	    from planner.core.config import HOST, load_config
   149	    from planner.core.db import connect, create_schema
   150	    from planner.core.server import create_app
   151	
   152	    config = load_config()
   153	    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(config.db_path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
   154	    os.makedirs(config.logs_dir, exist_ok=True)
   155	    with connect(config.db_path, config.db_busy_timeout_ms) as bootstrap:
   156	        create_schema(bootstrap)
   157	
   158	    clock = build_clock(config)
   159	    adapters = build_adapters(config)
   160	
   161	    def conn_factory() -> sqlite3.Connection:
   162	        return connect(config.db_path, config.db_busy_timeout_ms)
   163	
   164	    app = create_app(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
   165	    uvicorn.run(app, host=HOST, port=config.port)
   166

   168	# --- seed ---
   169	
   170	
   171	@main.command("seed")
   172	@click.option("--source", default=None, help="Path to a markdown planning directory.")
   173	@click.option("--demo", is_flag=True, default=False, help="Create the deterministic demo dataset.")
   174	@json_option
   175	def seed(source: str | None, demo: bool, as_json: bool) -> None:
   176	    """Import a markdown planning directory, or create a demo dataset (§12)."""
   177	    if (source is None) == (not demo):
   178	        http.fail_validation("provide exactly one of --source or --demo", as_json)
   179	    body: dict[str, Any]
   180	    if demo:
   181	        body = {"demo": True}
   182	    else:
   183	        assert source is not None
   184	        body = {"source_dir": os.path.abspath(source)}  # server resolves in ITS cwd
   185	    data = http.send("POST", "/api/seed", as_json=as_json, json_body=body)
   186	    http.emit(data, as_json, _seed_human(data))

   189	# --- top-level proposal/recap/note verbs ---

   192	@main.command("propose")
   193	@click.argument("field", type=click.Choice(_FIELDS))
   194	@click.argument("ticket_id", required=False, envvar=_TICKET_ID_ENV)
   195	@click.option("--body-file", default=None, help="Path to the body, or - for stdin.")
   196	@json_option
   197	def propose(field: str, ticket_id: str | None, body_file: str | None, as_json: bool) -> None:
   198	    """File or replace a proposal on a field (agent path)."""
   199	    body = read_body(ticket_id, body_file, as_json)
   200	    tid = resolve_ticket_id(ticket_id, as_json)
   201	    data = http.send(
   202	        "POST", f"/api/tickets/{tid}/propose/{field}", as_json=as_json, json_body={"body": body}
   203	    )
   204	    http.emit(data, as_json, f"proposed {field} on {data['id']}")
   205	
   206	
   207	@main.command("recap")
   208	@click.argument("ticket_id", required=False, envvar=_TICKET_ID_ENV)
   209	@click.option("--body-file", default=None, help="Path to the body, or - for stdin.")
   210	@json_option
   211	def recap(ticket_id: str | None, body_file: str | None, as_json: bool) -> None:
   212	    """Write a ticket recap (rejected before needs_approach)."""
   213	    body = read_body(ticket_id, body_file, as_json)
   214	    tid = resolve_ticket_id(ticket_id, as_json)
   215	    data = http.send("PUT", f"/api/tickets/{tid}/recap", as_json=as_json, json_body={"body": body})
   216	    http.emit(data, as_json, f"recap written on {data['id']}")

   221	@click.argument("ticket_id", required=False, envvar=_TICKET_ID_ENV)
   222	@click.option("--body-file", default=None, help="Path to the body, or - for stdin.")
   223	@json_option
   224	def note(field: str, ticket_id: str | None, body_file: str | None, as_json: bool) -> None:
   225	    """Write a field's notes slot (human or agent, any time)."""
   226	    body = read_body(ticket_id, body_file, as_json)

   236	
   237	@main.group("ticket")
   238	def ticket() -> None:
   239	    """Ticket verbs."""
   240	
   241	
   242	@ticket.command("create")
   243	@click.option("--title", required=True, help="Ticket title (<= 200 chars).")
   244	@click.option("--priority", type=click.Choice(_PRIORITIES), default=None)
   245	@click.option("--deadline", default=None, help="ISO date.")
   246	@click.option("--project", default=None, help="Vylo | Tribe | Learning | Other.")
   247	@click.option("--sprint", default=None, help="Sprint id (standalone tickets only).")
   248	@click.option("--item", default=None, help="Sprint item id to parent under.")
   249	@json_option
   250	def ticket_create(
   251	    title: str,
   252	    priority: str | None,
   253	    deadline: str | None,
   254	    project: str | None,
   255	    sprint: str | None,
   256	    item: str | None,
   257	    as_json: bool,
   258	) -> None:
   259	    """Create a ticket."""
   260	    body: dict[str, Any] = {"title": title}
   261	    if priority is not None:
   262	        body["priority"] = priority
   263	    if deadline is not None:
   264	        body["deadline"] = deadline
   265	    if project is not None:
   266	        body["project"] = project
   267	    if sprint is not None:
   268	        body["sprint_id"] = sprint       # --sprint -> sprint_id
   269	    if item is not None:
   270	        body["sprint_item_id"] = item    # --item   -> sprint_item_id
   271	    data = http.send("POST", "/api/tickets", as_json=as_json, json_body=body)
   272	    http.emit(data, as_json, f"{data['id']} {data['state']}")
   273	
   274	
   275	@ticket.command("show")
   276	@click.argument("ticket_id", required=False, envvar=_TICKET_ID_ENV)
   277	@json_option
   278	def ticket_show(ticket_id: str | None, as_json: bool) -> None:
   279	    """Show a ticket (defaults to $PLAN_TICKET_ID)."""
   280	    tid = resolve_ticket_id(ticket_id, as_json)
   281	    data = http.send("GET", f"/api/tickets/{tid}", as_json=as_json)
   282	    http.emit(data, as_json, f"{data['id']} {data['state']} {data['priority']} {data['title']}")

   285	@ticket.command("list")
   286	@click.option("--state", default=None)
   287	@click.option("--project", default=None)
   288	@click.option("--sprint", default=None)
   289	@json_option
   290	def ticket_list(state: str | None, project: str | None, sprint: str | None, as_json: bool) -> None:
   291	    """List tickets."""
   292	    params = _drop_none({"state": state, "project": project, "sprint_id": sprint})
   293	    data = http.send("GET", "/api/tickets", as_json=as_json, params=params)
   294	    http.emit(
   295	        data,
   296	        as_json,
   297	        _lines(data["tickets"], lambda t: f"{t['id']} {t['state']} {t['priority']} {t['title']}"),
   298	    )
   299	
   300	
   301	@ticket.command("set")
   302	@click.argument("ticket_id", required=False, envvar=_TICKET_ID_ENV)
   303	@click.option("--priority", type=click.Choice(_PRIORITIES), default=None)
   304	@click.option("--deadline", default=None, help="ISO date, or 'none' to clear.")
   305	@click.option("--day", default=None, help="ISO date or 'today' to assign to a day.")
   306	@click.option("--sprint", default=None, help="Sprint id, or 'none' to clear.")
   307	@json_option
   308	def ticket_set(
   309	    ticket_id: str | None,
   310	    priority: str | None,
   311	    deadline: str | None,
   312	    day: str | None,
   313	    sprint: str | None,
   314	    as_json: bool,
   315	) -> None:
   316	    """Set priority / deadline / day / sprint (no ceiling or at_cap — grants are human)."""
   317	    tid = resolve_ticket_id(ticket_id, as_json)
   318	    patch: dict[str, Any] = {}
   319	    if priority is not None:
   320	        patch["priority"] = priority
   321	    if deadline is not None:
   322	        patch["deadline"] = None if deadline == "none" else deadline
   323	    if sprint is not None:
   324	        patch["sprint_id"] = None if sprint == "none" else sprint
   325	    if not patch and day is None:
   326	        http.fail_validation("nothing to set: pass --priority/--deadline/--sprint/--day", as_json)
   327	    data: Any = None
   328	    if patch:
   329	        data = http.send("PATCH", f"/api/tickets/{tid}", as_json=as_json, json_body=patch)
   330	    if day is not None:
   331	        data = http.send(
   332	            "POST", f"/api/day/{day}/tickets", as_json=as_json, json_body={"ticket_id": tid}
   333	        )
   334	    # `data` is the LAST successful response: ticket_json if only PATCH, day view if --day.
   335	    human = (
   336	        f"day {data['id']}: {len(data['tickets'])} ticket(s)"
   337	        if "tickets" in data
   338	        else f"{data['id']} {data['state']}"
   339	    )
   340	    http.emit(data, as_json, human)
   341

   413	    if status is not None:

   415	    if blocked_by is not None:
   416	        body["blocked_by"] = [s for s in (x.strip() for x in blocked_by.split(",")) if s]
   417	    if not body:
   418	        http.fail_validation("nothing to set: pass --status/--blocked-by", as_json)
   419	    data = http.send("PATCH", f"/api/items/{item_id}", as_json=as_json, json_body=body)
   420	    http.emit(data, as_json, f"{data['id']} {data['status']}")

     1	"""Sprint-item, sprint, current-sprint view, and idea routes (§9). Thin HTTP shells
     2	over the stage-3 sprint writers and the sprint read views. Ideas are homed here
     3	because the Idea shape lives in this domain's contracts. Sprint writers take a
     4	`clock: Clock`, not `now: int` — do not mix them up.
     5	
     6	Two gap-fill writers (D1 ideas, D3 sprint dates) have no data-layer sibling yet;
     7	they are written writer-shaped (A1) so the integrator can relocate them into
     8	sprints/data.py as a pure cut-paste."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import sqlite3
    13	from datetime import date
    14	from typing import Any
    15	
    16	from fastapi import APIRouter
    17	from pydantic import BaseModel
    18	
    19	from planner.core.authctx import reject_agents
    20	from planner.core.clock import Clock
    21	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict, Priority, Project
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	from planner.core.events import append_event
    24	from planner.core.ids import ID_PREFIXES, new_id
    25	from planner.days.logic.dates import planning_date
    26	from planner.sprints import data as sprints_data
    27	from planner.sprints import views as sprints_views
    28	from planner.sprints.contracts import KICKOFF_FIELDS, REVIEW_FIELDS, ItemStatus
    29	from planner.sprints.logic import DateRange, find_overlap
    30	from planner.tickets.api import Cfg, Clk, Ctx, DbConn, parse_enum, txn
    31	
    32	router = APIRouter()
    33	
    34	_SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS = ("name",) + KICKOFF_FIELDS + REVIEW_FIELDS
    35	_ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS = ("title", "body", "priority", "deadline", "project", "current_state_note")

    42	    title: str = ""
    43	    project: str | None = None
    44	    body: str = ""
    45	    priority: str | None = None
    46	    deadline: str | None = None
    47	    current_state_note: str = ""

    54

    57	    name: str = ""

    59	    date_end: str = ""
    60	    limiting_factor: str = ""
    61	    primary_bet: str = ""

    77	# --- private gap-fill writers (D1/D3; A1: writer-shaped for relocation) ---------
    78	
    79	
    80	def _create_idea(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, body: str, project: Project | None,
    81	                 now: int) -> JsonDict:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "idea title is required", {})
    84	    idea_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["idea"])
    85	    with txn(conn):
    86	        conn.execute(
    87	            "INSERT INTO ideas (id, title, body, project, created_at, updated_at) "
    88	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
    89	            (idea_id, title, body, project.value if project is not None else None, now, now),
    90	        )
    91	        append_event(conn, idea_id, EventKind.idea_created, {"title": title, "source": "api"}, now)
    92	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM ideas WHERE id = ?", (idea_id,)).fetchone()
    93	    assert row is not None
    94	    return sprints_views.idea_json(row)
    95	
    96	
    97	def _set_sprint_dates(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, date_start: str | None,
    98	                      date_end: str | None, clock: Clock) -> None:
    99	    sprint = sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   100	    new_start = date_start if date_start is not None else sprint.date_start
   101	    new_end = date_end if date_end is not None else sprint.date_end
   102	    for label, value in (("date_start", new_start), ("date_end", new_end)):
   103	        try:
   104	            date.fromisoformat(value)
   105	        except ValueError as exc:
   106	            raise PlannerError(
   107	                ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {label}", {label: value}
   108	            ) from exc
   109	    if new_start > new_end:
   110	        raise PlannerError(
   111	            ErrorCode.validation,
   112	            "date_start must not be after date_end",
   113	            {"date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   114	        )
   115	    others = [
   116	        DateRange(id=str(r["id"]), date_start=str(r["date_start"]), date_end=str(r["date_end"]))
   117	        for r in conn.execute(
   118	            "SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints WHERE id != ?", (sprint_id,)
   119	        ).fetchall()
   120	    ]
   121	    conflict = find_overlap(new_start, new_end, others)
   122	    if conflict is not None:
   123	        raise PlannerError(
   124	            ErrorCode.sprint_overlap,
   125	            "sprint dates overlap",
   126	            {"conflict_id": conflict, "date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   127	        )
   128	    now = clock.now_unix()
   129	    with txn(conn):
   130	        conn.execute(
   131	            "UPDATE sprints SET date_start = ?, date_end = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   132	            (new_start, new_end, now, sprint_id),
   133	        )
   134	        if new_start != sprint.date_start:
   135	            append_event(
   136	                conn,
   137	                sprint_id,
   138	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   139	                {"field": "date_start", "from": sprint.date_start, "to": new_start},
   140	                now,
   141	            )
   142	        if new_end != sprint.date_end:
   143	            append_event(
   144	                conn,
   145	                sprint_id,
   146	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   147	                {"field": "date_end", "from": sprint.date_end, "to": new_end},
   148	                now,
   149	            )
   150	
   151	
   152	def _marshal_item_deadline(raw: object) -> None:
   153	    if raw is None:
   154	        return
   155	    if not isinstance(raw, str):
   156	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw})
   157	    try:
   158	        date.fromisoformat(raw)
   159	    except ValueError as exc:
   160	        raise PlannerError(
   161	            ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw}
   162	        ) from exc
   163	
   164	
   165	# --- item routes ---------------------------------------------------------------
   166	
   167	
   168	@router.post("/items")
   169	async def create_item(body: CreateItemBody, conn: DbConn, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   170	    if body.project is None:
   171	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is required")
   172	    project = parse_enum(Project, body.project, "project")
   173	    priority = parse_enum(Priority, body.priority, "priority") if body.priority is not None \
   174	        else Priority.P3
   175	    _marshal_item_deadline(body.deadline)
   176	    item = sprints_data.create_item(
   177	        conn,
   178	        title=body.title,
   179	        project=project,
   180	        body=body.body,
   181	        priority=priority,
   182	        deadline=body.deadline,
   183	        current_state_note=body.current_state_note,
   184	        sprint_id=body.sprint_id,
   185	        clock=clk,
   186	    )
   187	    return {**sprints_views.item_json(item), "blockers_cleared": False}
   188	
   189	
   190	@router.get("/items")
   191	async def list_items(conn: DbConn, status: str | None = None, project: str | None = None,
   192	                     sprint_id: str | None = None) -> JsonDict:
   193	    status_enum = parse_enum(ItemStatus, status, "status") if status is not None else None
   194	    project_enum = parse_enum(Project, project, "project") if project is not None else None
   195	    return {
   196	        "items": sprints_views.list_items(
   197	            conn, status=status_enum, project=project_enum, sprint_id_filter=sprint_id
   198	        )
   199	    }
   200	
   201	
   202	@router.get("/items/{item_id}")
   203	async def get_item(item_id: str, conn: DbConn) -> JsonDict:
   204	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   205	
   206	
   207	@router.patch("/items/{item_id}")
   208	async def patch_item(item_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   209	                     clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   210	    recognized = set(_ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS) | {"sprint_id", "status", "blocked_by"}
   211	    for key in body:
   212	        if key not in recognized:
   213	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown item field", {"field": key})
   214	    if not body:
   215	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no item fields to update", {})
   216	    if "blocked_by" in body and "status" not in body:
   217	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "blocked_by requires status", {})
   218	    for field in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   219	        if field in body:
   220	            if field == "deadline":
   221	                _marshal_item_deadline(body["deadline"])
   222	            sprints_data.update_item_field(conn, item_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   223	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   224	        sprints_data.assign_item_sprint(conn, item_id, body["sprint_id"], clock=clk)
   225	    if "status" in body:
   226	        to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body["status"], "status")
   227	        sprints_data.transition_item_status(
   228	            conn, item_id, to_status, clock=clk, by_agent=not ctx.is_human,
   229	            blocked_by=body.get("blocked_by"),
   230	        )
   231	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   232

   236	                              clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   237	    to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body.to, "status")

   239	        conn, item_id, to_status, note=body.note, proposed_by=ctx.actor, clock=clk

   288	    recognized = set(_SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS) | {"date_start", "date_end"}
   289	    for key in body:
   290	        if key not in recognized:
   291	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown sprint field", {"field": key})
   292	    if not body:
   293	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no sprint fields to update", {})
   294	    for field in _SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS:
   295	        if field in body:
   296	            sprints_data.update_sprint_field(conn, sprint_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   297	    if "date_start" in body or "date_end" in body:
   298	        _set_sprint_dates(
   299	            conn, sprint_id, date_start=body.get("date_start"), date_end=body.get("date_end"),
   300	            clock=clk,
   301	        )
   302	    return sprints_views.sprint_json(sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sprint_id))

    91	def item_rollup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    92	    rollup: dict[str, int] = {s.value: 0 for s in TicketState}
    93	    rows = conn.execute(
    94	        "SELECT state, COUNT(*) AS n FROM tickets WHERE sprint_item_id = ? GROUP BY state",
    95	        (item_id,),
    96	    ).fetchall()
    97	    for r in rows:
    98	        rollup[str(r["state"])] = int(r["n"])
    99	    return rollup

   102	def _item_row_key(row: sqlite3.Row) -> tuple[int, int, str]:
   103	    return (_prio_rank(str(row["priority"])), int(row["created_at"]), str(row["id"]))

   106	def list_items(
   107	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   108	    *,
   109	    status: ItemStatus | None,
   110	    project: Project | None,
   111	    sprint_id_filter: str | None,
   112	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   113	    clauses: list[str] = []
   114	    params: list[str] = []
   115	    if status is not None:
   116	        clauses.append("status = ?")
   117	        params.append(status.value)
   118	    if project is not None:
   119	        clauses.append("project = ?")
   120	        params.append(project.value)
   121	    if sprint_id_filter is not None:
   122	        if sprint_id_filter == "null":
   123	            clauses.append("sprint_id IS NULL")
   124	        else:
   125	            clauses.append("sprint_id = ?")
   126	            params.append(sprint_id_filter)
   127	    where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
   128	    rows = conn.execute(
   129	        "SELECT id, priority, created_at FROM sprint_items" + where, tuple(params)
   130	    ).fetchall()
   131	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   132	    for row in sorted(rows, key=_item_row_key):
   133	        read = sprints_data.read_item(conn, str(row["id"]))
   134	        result.append({**item_json(read.item), "blockers_cleared": read.blockers_cleared})
   135	    return result

   193	
   194	
   195	def sprint_current_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, today_iso: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   196	    ranges = [
   197	        DateRange(id=str(r["id"]), date_start=str(r["date_start"]), date_end=str(r["date_end"]))
   198	        for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints").fetchall()
   199	    ]
   200	    sid = current_sprint_id(today_iso, ranges)
   201	    if sid is None:
   202	        return {
   203	            "sprint": None,
   204	            "groups": {s.value: [] for s in ItemStatus},
   205	            "loose_tickets": [],
   206	        }
   207	    item_rows = conn.execute(
   208	        "SELECT id, priority, created_at FROM sprint_items WHERE sprint_id = ?", (sid,)
   209	    ).fetchall()
   210	    groups: dict[str, list[JsonDict]] = {s.value: [] for s in ItemStatus}
   211	    for row in sorted(item_rows, key=_item_row_key):
   212	        read = sprints_data.read_item(conn, str(row["id"]))
   213	        groups[read.item.status.value].append(
   214	            {
   215	                **item_json(read.item),
   216	                "blockers_cleared": read.blockers_cleared,
   217	                "rollup": item_rollup(conn, str(row["id"])),
   218	            }
   219	        )
   220	    loose_rows = conn.execute(
   221	        "SELECT id FROM tickets WHERE sprint_id = ? AND sprint_item_id IS NULL "
   222	        "ORDER BY created_at, id",
   223	        (sid,),
   224	    ).fetchall()
   225	    loose = [ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, str(r["id"])), now) for r in loose_rows]
   226	    return {
   227	        "sprint": sprint_json(sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sid)),
   228	        "groups": groups,
   229	        "loose_tickets": loose,
   230	    }

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/planner/cli/http.py | sed -n '1,120p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The CLI's single HTTP seam. Owns URL construction, header assembly, request
     2	execution, and — the whole point — every stdout/stderr/exit-code decision. A
     3	handler in main.py only builds a route + body, calls `send`, then `emit` (or a
     4	client-side `fail_validation`). This module imports stdlib + httpx only; error
     5	envelopes are plain dict literals, never a planner type."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import os
    11	import sys
    12	from typing import Any, NoReturn
    13	
    14	import httpx
    15	
    16	_DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8767"
    17	_TIMEOUT = 30.0
    18	
    19	EXIT_OK = 0
    20	EXIT_ERROR = 1
    21	EXIT_CONNECTION = 2
    22	
    23	
    24	def _base_url() -> str:
    25	    raw = os.environ.get("PLAN_SERVER_URL", "").strip()
    26	    return raw or _DEFAULT_BASE_URL
    27	
    28	
    29	def _url(path: str) -> str:
    30	    return _base_url().rstrip("/") + path
    31	
    32	
    33	def _headers() -> dict[str, str]:
    34	    # X-Plan-Actor is always sent (default "agent"); run/claim only when set & non-empty.
    35	    headers = {"X-Plan-Actor": os.environ.get("PLAN_ACTOR", "").strip() or "agent"}
    36	    run_id = os.environ.get("PLAN_RUN_ID", "").strip()
    37	    if run_id:
    38	        headers["X-Plan-Run-Id"] = run_id
    39	    claim = os.environ.get("PLAN_CLAIM", "").strip()
    40	    if claim:
    41	        headers["X-Plan-Claim"] = claim
    42	    return headers
    43	
    44	
    45	def send(
    46	    method: str,
    47	    path: str,
    48	    *,
    49	    as_json: bool,
    50	    json_body: Any | None = None,
    51	    params: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    52	) -> Any:
    53	    """Execute one request and apply the failure half of the exit contract. Transport
    54	    failure -> stderr + exit 2. Non-2xx (or a 2xx body that still carries an "error"
    55	    key) -> stderr + exit 1. On 2xx: return the parsed JSON body to the caller."""
    56	    try:
    57	        resp = httpx.request(
    58	            method, _url(path), json=json_body, params=params, headers=_headers(),
    59	            timeout=_TIMEOUT,
    60	        )
    61	    except httpx.TransportError as exc:
    62	        _fail_connection(exc, as_json)
    63	    try:
    64	        data: Any = resp.json()
    65	    except ValueError:
    66	        data = None
    67	    if resp.is_success:
    68	        if isinstance(data, dict) and "error" in data:
    69	            _fail_response(resp, data, as_json)  # A1: unconditional error-envelope contract
    70	        return data
    71	    _fail_response(resp, data, as_json)
    72	
    73	
    74	def emit(data: Any, as_json: bool, human: str) -> NoReturn:
    75	    """Success path. --json: raw JSON to stdout. Else: the terse human line. exit 0."""
    76	    if as_json:
    77	        print(json.dumps(data))
    78	    else:
    79	        print(human)
    80	    sys.exit(EXIT_OK)
    81	
    82	
    83	def fail_validation(
    84	    message: str, as_json: bool, detail: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    85	) -> NoReturn:
    86	    """Client-side validation error, rendered in the same style as a server error. exit 1."""
    87	    payload = {"error": {"code": "validation", "message": message, "detail": detail or {}}}
    88	    if as_json:
    89	        print(json.dumps(payload), file=sys.stderr)
    90	    else:
    91	        print(f"error: validation: {message}", file=sys.stderr)
    92	    sys.exit(EXIT_ERROR)
    93	
    94	
    95	def _fail_connection(exc: httpx.TransportError, as_json: bool) -> NoReturn:
    96	    payload = {"error": {"code": "connection", "message": str(exc), "detail": {}}}
    97	    if as_json:
    98	        print(json.dumps(payload), file=sys.stderr)
    99	    else:
   100	        print(f"error: connection: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
   101	    sys.exit(EXIT_CONNECTION)
   102	
   103	
   104	def _fail_response(resp: httpx.Response, data: Any, as_json: bool) -> NoReturn:
   105	    if isinstance(data, dict) and "error" in data:
   106	        payload: dict[str, Any] = data
   107	    else:
   108	        # Non-envelope non-2xx (422 pydantic, 404 routing, 500 HTML). Never swallow:
   109	        # wrap so exit is 1 and stderr still carries a parseable {"error":...}.
   110	        payload = {
   111	            "error": {
   112	                "code": "http_error",
   113	                "message": f"HTTP {resp.status_code}",
   114	                "detail": data if data is not None else resp.text,
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	    if as_json:
   118	        print(json.dumps(payload), file=sys.stderr)
   119	    else:
   120	        err = payload["error"]

     5	live here only. Column names equal the contract dataclass field names one-for-one."""

    41	
    42	class ItemRead(NamedTuple):
    43	    item: SprintItem
    44	    blockers_cleared: bool
    45	
    46	
    47	_ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    48	    {"title", "body", "priority", "deadline", "project", "current_state_note"}
    49	)
    50	_SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(KICKOFF_FIELDS + REVIEW_FIELDS + ("name",))
    51	
    52	
    53	@contextmanager
    54	def _tx(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    55	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    56	    try:
    57	        yield
    58	    except BaseException:
    59	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    60	        raise
    61	    else:
    62	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    63	
    64	
    65	def _row_to_sprint(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Sprint:
    66	    return Sprint(
    67	        id=row["id"],
    68	        name=row["name"],
    69	        date_start=row["date_start"],
    70	        date_end=row["date_end"],
    71	        limiting_factor=row["limiting_factor"],
    72	        primary_bet=row["primary_bet"],
    73	        supports=row["supports"],
    74	        premortem=row["premortem"],
    75	        weekly_addenda=[Addendum(**a) for a in json.loads(row["weekly_addenda"])],
    76	        kickoff_frozen_at=row["kickoff_frozen_at"],
    77	        outcomes=row["outcomes"],
    78	        solo_reflection=row["solo_reflection"],
    79	        joint_discussion=row["joint_discussion"],
    80	        updates_to_thinking=row["updates_to_thinking"],
    81	        carry_forward=row["carry_forward"],
    82	        review_frozen_at=row["review_frozen_at"],
    83	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    84	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    85	    )
    86	
    87	
    88	def _proposal_to_json(p: ItemStatusProposal) -> str:
    89	    return json.dumps(
    90	        {
    91	            "to_status": p.to_status.value,
    92	            "note": p.note,
    93	            "proposed_by": p.proposed_by,
    94	            "created_at": p.created_at,
    95	        }
    96	    )
    97	
    98	
    99	def _proposal_from_json(raw: str | None) -> ItemStatusProposal | None:
   100	    if raw is None:
   101	        return None
   102	    data = json.loads(raw)
   103	    return ItemStatusProposal(
   104	        to_status=ItemStatus(data["to_status"]),
   105	        note=data["note"],
   106	        proposed_by=data["proposed_by"],
   107	        created_at=data["created_at"],
   108	    )
   109	
   110	
   111	def _row_to_item(row: sqlite3.Row) -> SprintItem:
   112	    return SprintItem(
   113	        id=row["id"],
   114	        title=row["title"],
   115	        body=row["body"],
   116	        status=ItemStatus(row["status"]),
   117	        priority=Priority(row["priority"]),
   118	        deadline=row["deadline"],
   119	        project=Project(row["project"]),
   120	        current_state_note=row["current_state_note"],
   121	        sprint_id=row["sprint_id"],
   122	        blocked_by=json.loads(row["blocked_by"]),
   123	        status_proposal=_proposal_from_json(row["status_proposal"]),
   124	        created_at=row["created_at"],
   125	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
   126	    )
   127	
   128	
   129	def _load_sprint(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str) -> Sprint:
   130	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM sprints WHERE id = ?", (sprint_id,)).fetchone()
   131	    if row is None:
   132	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint not found", {"id": sprint_id})
   133	    return _row_to_sprint(row)
   134	
   135	
   136	def _load_item(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str) -> SprintItem:
   137	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (item_id,)).fetchone()
   138	    if row is None:
   139	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint item not found", {"id": item_id})
   140	    return _row_to_item(row)
   141	
   142	
   143	# --- sprint writers -------------------------------------------------------------
   144	
   145	
   146	def create_sprint(
   147	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   148	    *,
   149	    name: str,
   150	    date_start: str,
   151	    date_end: str,
   152	    limiting_factor: str = "",
   153	    primary_bet: str = "",
   154	    supports: str = "",
   155	    premortem: str = "",
   156	    clock: Clock,
   157	) -> Sprint:
   158	    if not name:
   159	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "sprint name is required", {})
   160	    if date_start > date_end:
   161	        raise PlannerError(
   162	            ErrorCode.validation,
   163	            "date_start must not be after date_end",
   164	            {"date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end},
   165	        )
   166	    now = clock.now_unix()
   167	    sprint_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint"])
   168	    with _tx(conn):
   169	        existing = [
   170	            DateRange(id=r["id"], date_start=r["date_start"], date_end=r["date_end"])
   171	            for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints")
   172	        ]
   173	        conflict = find_overlap(date_start, date_end, existing)
   174	        if conflict is not None:
   175	            raise PlannerError(
   176	                ErrorCode.sprint_overlap,
   177	                "sprint dates overlap",
   178	                {"conflict_id": conflict, "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end},
   179	            )
   180	        conn.execute(
   181	            "INSERT INTO sprints ("
   182	            "id, name, date_start, date_end, limiting_factor, primary_bet, supports, "
   183	            "premortem, weekly_addenda, kickoff_frozen_at, outcomes, solo_reflection, "
   184	            "joint_discussion, updates_to_thinking, carry_forward, review_frozen_at, "
   185	            "created_at, updated_at) "
   186	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, '[]', NULL, '', '', '', '', '', NULL, ?, ?)",
   187	            (
   188	                sprint_id,
   189	                name,
   190	                date_start,
   191	                date_end,
   192	                limiting_factor,
   193	                primary_bet,
   194	                supports,
   195	                premortem,
   196	                now,
   197	                now,
   198	            ),
   199	        )
   200	        append_event(
   201	            conn,
   202	            sprint_id,
   203	            EventKind.sprint_created,
   204	            {"name": name, "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end},
   205	            now,
   206	        )
   207	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   208	
   209	
   210	def update_sprint_field(
   211	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, field: str, value: str, *, clock: Clock
   212	) -> Sprint:
   213	    if field not in _SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS:
   214	        raise PlannerError(
   215	            ErrorCode.validation,
   216	            "field is not an editable sprint text field",
   217	            {"field": field},
   218	        )
   219	    sprint = _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   220	    if not field_write_admissible(field, sprint.kickoff_frozen_at, sprint.review_frozen_at):
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.frozen_write,
   223	            "field is frozen",
   224	            {"field": field, "group": frozen_group(field)},
   225	        )
   226	    now = clock.now_unix()
   227	    prev = getattr(sprint, field)
   228	    with _tx(conn):
   229	        conn.execute(
   230	            f"UPDATE sprints SET {field} = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   231	            (value, now, sprint_id),
   232	        )
   233	        append_event(
   234	            conn,
   235	            sprint_id,
   236	            EventKind.sprint_updated,
   237	            {"field": field, "from": prev, "to": value},
   238	            now,
   239	        )
   240	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   241	
   242	
   243	def freeze_kickoff(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, clock: Clock) -> Sprint:
   244	    sprint = _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   245	    if sprint.kickoff_frozen_at is not None:
   246	        return sprint
   247	    now = clock.now_unix()
   248	    with _tx(conn):
   249	        # Latch inside the transaction: only the write that flips NULL emits the event.
   250	        cursor = conn.execute(
   251	            "UPDATE sprints SET kickoff_frozen_at = ?, updated_at = ? "
   252	            "WHERE id = ? AND kickoff_frozen_at IS NULL",
   253	            (now, now, sprint_id),
   254	        )
   255	        if cursor.rowcount == 1:
   256	            append_event(conn, sprint_id, EventKind.kickoff_frozen, {"frozen_at": now}, now)
   257	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   258	
   259	
   260	def freeze_review(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, clock: Clock) -> Sprint:
   261	    sprint = _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   262	    if sprint.review_frozen_at is not None:
   263	        return sprint
   264	    now = clock.now_unix()
   265	    with _tx(conn):
   266	        cursor = conn.execute(
   267	            "UPDATE sprints SET review_frozen_at = ?, updated_at = ? "
   268	            "WHERE id = ? AND review_frozen_at IS NULL",
   269	            (now, now, sprint_id),
   270	        )
   271	        if cursor.rowcount == 1:
   272	            append_event(conn, sprint_id, EventKind.review_frozen, {"frozen_at": now}, now)
   273	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   274	
   275	
   276	def add_addendum(
   277	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, date: str, text: str, clock: Clock
   278	) -> Sprint:
   279	    if not date:
   280	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "addendum date is required", {})
   281	    if not text:
   282	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "addendum text is required", {})
   283	    sprint = _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   284	    updated = sprint.weekly_addenda + [Addendum(date=date, text=text)]
   285	    now = clock.now_unix()
   286	    with _tx(conn):
   287	        conn.execute(
   288	            "UPDATE sprints SET weekly_addenda = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   289	            (
   290	                json.dumps([{"date": a.date, "text": a.text} for a in updated]),
   291	                now,
   292	                sprint_id,
   293	            ),
   294	        )
   295	        append_event(
   296	            conn, sprint_id, EventKind.addendum_added, {"date": date, "text": text}, now
   297	        )
   298	    return _load_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   299	
   300	
   301	# --- sprint-item writers --------------------------------------------------------

   303	
   304	def create_item(
   305	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   306	    *,
   307	    title: str,
   308	    project: Project,
   309	    body: str = "",
   310	    priority: Priority = Priority.P3,
   311	    deadline: str | None = None,
   312	    current_state_note: str = "",
   313	    sprint_id: str | None = None,
   314	    clock: Clock,
   315	) -> SprintItem:
   316	    if not title:
   317	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "item title is required", {})
   318	    item_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint_item"])
   319	    now = clock.now_unix()
   320	    with _tx(conn):
   321	        conn.execute(
   322	            "INSERT INTO sprint_items ("
   323	            "id, title, body, status, priority, deadline, project, current_state_note, "
   324	            "sprint_id, blocked_by, status_proposal, created_at, updated_at) "
   325	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, '[]', NULL, ?, ?)",
   326	            (
   327	                item_id,
   328	                title,
   329	                body,
   330	                ItemStatus.todo.value,
   331	                priority.value,
   332	                deadline,
   333	                project.value,
   334	                current_state_note,
   335	                sprint_id,
   336	                now,
   337	                now,
   338	            ),
   339	        )
   340	        append_event(
   341	            conn,
   342	            item_id,
   343	            EventKind.sprint_item_created,
   344	            {"title": title, "project": project.value, "sprint_id": sprint_id},
   345	            now,
   346	        )
   347	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)
   348	
   349	
   350	def update_item_field(
   351	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str, field: str, value: str | None, *, clock: Clock
   352	) -> SprintItem:
   353	    if field not in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   354	        raise PlannerError(
   355	            ErrorCode.validation, "field is not an editable item field", {"field": field}
   356	        )
   357	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   358	    prev = getattr(item, field)
   359	    stored: str | None = value
   360	    if field == "priority":
   361	        if value is None:
   362	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid priority", {"value": value})
   363	        try:
   364	            stored = Priority(value).value
   365	        except ValueError as exc:
   366	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid priority", {"value": value}) from exc
   367	    elif field == "project":
   368	        if value is None:
   369	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid project", {"value": value})
   370	        try:
   371	            stored = Project(value).value
   372	        except ValueError as exc:
   373	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid project", {"value": value}) from exc
   374	    now = clock.now_unix()
   375	    with _tx(conn):
   376	        conn.execute(
   377	            f"UPDATE sprint_items SET {field} = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   378	            (stored, now, item_id),
   379	        )
   380	        append_event(
   381	            conn,
   382	            item_id,
   383	            EventKind.item_updated,
   384	            {"field": field, "from": prev, "to": stored},
   385	            now,
   386	        )
   387	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)
   388	
   389	
   390	def transition_item_status(
   391	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   392	    item_id: str,
   393	    to_status: ItemStatus,
   394	    *,
   395	    clock: Clock,
   396	    by_agent: bool = True,
   397	    blocked_by: Sequence[str] | None = None,
   398	) -> SprintItem:
   399	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   400	    from_status = item.status
   401	    intended: list[str] = list(blocked_by or []) if to_status is ItemStatus.blocked else []
   402	    verdict = (
   403	        classify_agent_transition(from_status, to_status, intended)
   404	        if by_agent
   405	        else classify_human_transition(from_status, to_status, intended)
   406	    )
   407	    if verdict is TransitionVerdict.forbidden:
   408	        raise PlannerError(
   409	            ErrorCode.item_transition_forbidden,
   410	            "transition not permitted",
   411	            {"from": from_status.value, "to": to_status.value},
   412	        )
   413	    if verdict is TransitionVerdict.missing_blockers:
   414	        raise PlannerError(
   415	            ErrorCode.validation,
   416	            "blocked requires a non-empty blocked_by",
   417	            {"to": "blocked"},
   418	        )
   419	    new_blocked_by = intended if to_status is ItemStatus.blocked else []
   420	    cause = "agent" if by_agent else "human"
   421	    now = clock.now_unix()
   422	    with _tx(conn):
   423	        conn.execute(
   424	            "UPDATE sprint_items SET status = ?, blocked_by = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   425	            (to_status.value, json.dumps(new_blocked_by), now, item_id),
   426	        )
   427	        append_event(
   428	            conn,
   429	            item_id,
   430	            EventKind.item_status_changed,
   431	            {"from": from_status.value, "to": to_status.value, "cause": cause},
   432	            now,
   433	        )
   434	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)
   435	
   436	
   437	def propose_item_status(
   438	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   439	    item_id: str,
   440	    to_status: ItemStatus,
   441	    *,
   442	    note: str | None,
   443	    proposed_by: str,
   444	    clock: Clock,
   445	) -> SprintItem:
   446	    if to_status not in PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES:
   447	        raise PlannerError(
   448	            ErrorCode.validation,
   449	            "only done/deferred_next_sprint are proposable",
   450	            {"to": to_status.value},
   451	        )
   452	    item = _load_item(conn, item_id)
   453	    if item.status in PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES:
   454	        raise PlannerError(
   455	            ErrorCode.validation,
   456	            "item status is terminal; no further proposals",
   457	            {"status": item.status.value},
   458	        )
   459	    now = clock.now_unix()
   460	    with _tx(conn):
   461	        existing = item.status_proposal
   462	        if existing is not None:
   463	            append_event(
   464	                conn,
   465	                item_id,
   466	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   467	                {
   468	                    "field": "status",
   469	                    "replaced_body": {
   470	                        "to_status": existing.to_status.value,
   471	                        "note": existing.note,
   472	                        "proposed_by": existing.proposed_by,
   473	                        "created_at": existing.created_at,
   474	                    },
   475	                },
   476	                now,
   477	            )
   478	        proposal = ItemStatusProposal(
   479	            to_status=to_status, note=note, proposed_by=proposed_by, created_at=now
   480	        )
   481	        conn.execute(
   482	            "UPDATE sprint_items SET status_proposal = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   483	            (_proposal_to_json(proposal), now, item_id),
   484	        )
   485	        append_event(
   486	            conn,
   487	            item_id,
   488	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   489	            {
   490	                "field": "status",
   491	                "body": {"to_status": to_status.value, "note": note},
   492	                "proposed_by": proposed_by,
   493	            },
   494	            now,
   495	        )
   496	    return _load_item(conn, item_id)
   497	
   498	
   499	def accept_item_status(
   500	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str, *, clock: Clock, resolved_by: str = "human"

     1	/* Screen 1: Day (#/day, home). Stateless render over /api/day/today +
     2	 * /api/queues + /api/chat/<day>/status. Every action calls fetchJson and relies
     3	 * on the WS flush -> route() -> full re-render with fresh fetches; no optimistic
     4	 * UI. Registers the real "day" screen over app.js's placeholder (overwrite-wins).
     5	 * Classic script: IIFE + "use strict", var/function style, createElement only. */
     6	(function () {
     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = window.Planner;
     9	  var c = Planner.components;
    10	
    11	  function el(tag, className, text) {
    12	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    13	    if (className) {
    14	      node.className = className;
    15	    }
    16	    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) {
    17	      node.textContent = text;
    18	    }
    19	    return node;
    20	  }

    22	  function render(root) {
    23	    root.setAttribute("data-screen", "day");
    24	    Promise.all([
    25	      Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/day/today"),
    26	      Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/queues")
    27	    ]).then(function (results) {
    28	      var day = results[0];
    29	      var queues = results[1];
    30	      // The chat entity id is day.id (day_YYYY-MM-DD) verbatim; a status-fetch
    31	      // failure renders the offline notice rather than blanking the screen.
    32	      return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + day.id + "/status").then(
    33	        function (st) {
    34	          build(root, day, queues, st.available);
    35	        },
    36	        function () {
    37	          build(root, day, queues, false);
    38	        }
    39	      );
    40	    }).then(null, function (err) {
    41	      root.replaceChildren(c.errorLine(err));
    42	    });
    43	  }

    72	  function dayRow(t, date, container) {
    73	    var row = el("div", "day-ticket-row");
    74	    row.setAttribute("data-ticket-id", t.id);
    75	    var chips = [c.chip("priority", t.priority), c.chip("state", t.state)];

    93	      }

    96	        function () {},   // A3: stay disabled on success; the flush re-render replaces the row

    98	          remove.disabled = false;
    99	          container.appendChild(c.errorLine(err));
   100	        }
   101	      );
   102	    });

   104	    return row;
   105	  }
   106	
   107	  function build(root, day, queues, available) {
   108	    // Mutating day routes use the server's canonical date, not the browser clock.
   109	    var date = day.id.slice(4);
   110	    var grid = el("div", "day-grid");
   111	
   112	    var main = el("div", "day-main");
   113	    main.appendChild(c.panel("Brief", c.markdownBlock(day.brief)));
   114	    main.appendChild(c.panel("Plan", day.plan ? c.planTree(day.plan, planHandlers(date)) : c.quietLine("(no plan)")));
   115	
   116	    var tickets = day.tickets || [];
   117	    var todayPanel = c.panel("Today", tickets.length ? [] : c.quietLine("(none)"));
   118	    var todayBody = todayPanel.querySelector(".panel-body");
   119	    tickets.forEach(function (t) {
   120	      todayBody.appendChild(dayRow(t, date, todayBody));
   121	    });
   122	    main.appendChild(todayPanel);
   123	    grid.appendChild(main);
   124	
   125	    var side = el("div", "day-side");
   126	    var reviewLink = el("a", "review-entry");
   127	    reviewLink.setAttribute("data-review-entry", "");
   128	    reviewLink.setAttribute("href", "#/review");
   129	    reviewLink.appendChild(el("span", null, "Review"));
   130	    var n = (queues.approvals || []).length;
   131	    var count = el("span", n === 0 ? "review-entry-count zero" : "review-entry-count", String(n));
   132	    count.setAttribute("data-pending-count", String(n));
   133	    reviewLink.appendChild(count);
   134	    side.appendChild(reviewLink);
   135	    side.appendChild(c.panel("Chat", c.chatPanel(day.id, { available: available })));
   136	    grid.appendChild(side);
   137	
   138	    root.replaceChildren(grid);
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  Planner.registerScreen("day", render);
   142	})();

   489	  // requireGrant, Accept is unfireable until the picker yields both halves.
   490	  function proposalCard(opts) {
   491	    var card = make("div", "proposal-card");
   492	    card.appendChild(make("div", "proposal-meta", "proposed by " + opts.proposal.proposed_by));
   493	    card.appendChild(markdownBlock(opts.proposal.body));
   494	    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input proposal-edit");
   495	    textarea.setAttribute("data-edit", "");
   496	    textarea.rows = 8;
   497	    textarea.value = opts.proposal.body;
   498	    card.appendChild(textarea);
   499	
   500	    var picker = null;
   501	    var inFlight = false;
   502	    var resolved = false;
   503	    var actions = make("div", "proposal-card-actions");
   504	    var accept = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
   505	    accept.type = "button";
   506	    accept.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
   507	
   508	    function sync() {
   509	      accept.disabled = inFlight || resolved ||
   510	        (opts.requireGrant && (picker === null || picker.getGrant() === null));
   511	    }
   512	
   513	    if (opts.requireGrant) {
   514	      picker = grantPairPicker(opts.newState, sync);
   515	      card.appendChild(picker);
   516	    }
   517	
   518	    accept.addEventListener("click", function () {
   519	      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");
   520	      if (prior) {
   521	        actions.removeChild(prior);
   522	      }
   523	      var payload = {};
   524	      if (textarea.value !== opts.proposal.body) {
   525	        payload.edited_body = textarea.value;
   526	      }
   527	      if (opts.requireGrant) {
   528	        var grant = picker.getGrant();
   529	        payload.next_ceiling = grant.next_ceiling;
   530	        payload.at_cap = grant.at_cap;
   531	      }
   532	      inFlight = true;
   533	      sync();
   534	      Promise.resolve(opts.onAccept(payload)).then(
   535	        function () {
   536	          resolved = true;   // A3: stay disabled after a successful accept
   537	          inFlight = false;
   538	          sync();
   539	        },
   540	        function (err) {
   541	          inFlight = false;
   542	          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
   543	          sync();
   544	        }
   545	      );
   546	    });
   547	
   548	    actions.appendChild(accept);
   549	    card.appendChild(actions);
   550	    sync();   // Accept starts disabled when a grant is required (nothing picked)
   551	    return card;
   552	  }

   604	      });

   619	  // --- D11 item 10: chat panel -----------------------------------------------
   620	  // entityId is the SERVER-provided chat id (day.id / ticket.id), never built
   621	  // client-side. Rebuilds its message list from the module-scope transcript, which
   622	  // the WS flush does not touch — that is the survival mechanism.
   623	  function chatPanel(entityId, opts) {
   624	    var panelEl = make("div", "chat-panel");
   625	    panelEl.setAttribute("data-chat-panel", "");
   626	    var messages = make("div", "chat-messages");
   627	    messages.setAttribute("data-chat-messages", "");
   628	    panelEl.appendChild(messages);
   629	
   630	    function paint() {
   631	      messages.replaceChildren();
   632	      (chatTranscripts[entityId] || []).forEach(function (msg) {
   633	        if (msg.who === "you") {
   634	          var you = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--you", msg.text);
   635	          you.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "you");
   636	          messages.appendChild(you);
   637	        } else {
   638	          var reply = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--planner");
   639	          reply.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "planner");
   640	          reply.appendChild(markdownBlock(msg.text));
   641	          messages.appendChild(reply);
   642	        }
   643	      });
   644	    }
   645	    paint();
   646	
   647	    if (!opts.available) {
   648	      var offline = make("div", "chat-offline", "gateway offline");
   649	      offline.setAttribute("data-chat-offline", "");
   650	      panelEl.appendChild(offline);
   651	      return panelEl;   // §11 notice; no input source rendered when offline
   652	    }
   653	
   654	    var source;
   655	    var ctx = {
   656	      initialText: chatDrafts[entityId] || "",
   657	      onInput: function (text) {
   658	        chatDrafts[entityId] = text;
   659	      },
   660	      submit: function (text) {
   661	        if (!chatTranscripts[entityId]) {
   662	          chatTranscripts[entityId] = [];
   663	        }
   664	        chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "you", text: text });
   665	        delete chatDrafts[entityId];
   666	        paint();
   667	        return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + entityId + "/send", {
   668	          method: "POST",
   669	          body: { text: text }
   670	        }).then(
   671	          function (res) {
   672	            chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "planner", text: res.reply_text });
   673	            if (panelEl.isConnected) {   // a flush may have replaced the panel mid-flight
   674	              paint();
   675	            }
   676	            return res;
   677	          },
   678	          function (err) {
   679	            var prior = panelEl.querySelector(".error-line");
   680	            if (prior) {
   681	              panelEl.removeChild(prior);
   682	            }
   683	            panelEl.insertBefore(errorLine(err), source);
   684	            return Promise.reject(err);
   685	          }
   686	        );
   687	      }
   688	    };
   689	    source = Planner.chatInput.sources[Planner.chatInput.active](ctx);
   690	    panelEl.appendChild(source);
   691	    return panelEl;
   692	  }

   776	  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   777	  // T16 additions (D11 items 11, 12, 14, 15) — State control, Grant control,
   778	  // Event log, Run history. Additive: nothing above this line is touched. These
   779	  // reuse the T15 grant-pair picker and the shared bindMutating discipline; the
   780	  // server re-validates every write, so the pickers only choose what to OFFER.
   781	  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   782	
   783	  function stateLabel(s) {
   784	    return String(s).replace(/_/g, " ");
   785	  }
   786	
   787	  function formatUnix(seconds) {
   788	    if (seconds === null || seconds === undefined) {
   789	      return "";
   790	    }
   791	    return new Date(Number(seconds) * 1000).toLocaleString();
   792	  }
   793	
   794	  // The exact event-row summary (payload keys confirmed against resolution.py /
   795	  // api.py writers). Unlisted kinds get an empty summary; kind + time still show.
   796	  function eventSummary(kind, payload) {
   797	    payload = payload || {};
   798	    if (kind === "state_changed") {
   799	      return String(payload.from) + " → " + String(payload.to);
   800	    }
   801	    if (kind === "proposal_accepted") {
   802	      return String(payload.field) + " · " + String(payload.resolved_by);
   803	    }
   804	    if (kind === "ticket_updated") {
   805	      return String(payload.field);
   806	    }
   807	    return "";
   808	  }
   809	
   810	  // D11 #11: state control — human jump / drop / (conditional) unblock. No
   811	  // re-render on success; the WS flush handles it (bindMutating stays disabled).
   812	  function stateControl(opts) {
   813	    var wrap = make("div", "state-control");
   814	    wrap.setAttribute("data-state-control", "");
   815	    wrap.appendChild(make("span", "state-control-current", stateLabel(opts.state)));
   816	
   817	    var select = make("select", "state-control-select form-control");
