   819	    STATE_ORDER.forEach(function (s) {
   820	      var option = make("option", null, stateLabel(s));
   821	      option.value = s;
   822	      select.appendChild(option);
   823	    });
   824	    select.value = opts.state;
   825	    wrap.appendChild(select);
   826	
   827	    var errorHost = make("div", "state-control-error");
   828	
   829	    var jump = make("button", "button", "Jump");
   830	    jump.type = "button";
   831	    jump.setAttribute("data-state-jump", "");
   832	    bindMutating(jump, errorHost, function () {
   833	      return opts.onJump(select.value);
   834	    });
   835	    wrap.appendChild(jump);
   836	
   837	    var drop = make("button", "button", "Drop");
   838	    drop.type = "button";
   839	    drop.setAttribute("data-drop", "");
   840	    bindMutating(drop, errorHost, function () {
   841	      return opts.onDrop();
   842	    });
   843	    wrap.appendChild(drop);
   844	
   845	    if (opts.autoBlocked) {
   846	      var unblock = make("button", "button", "Unblock");
   847	      unblock.type = "button";
   848	      unblock.setAttribute("data-unblock", "");
   849	      bindMutating(unblock, errorHost, function () {
   850	        return opts.onUnblock();
   851	      });
   852	      wrap.appendChild(unblock);
   853	    }
   854	
   855	    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
   856	    return wrap;
   857	  }
   858	
   859	  // D11 #12: grant control — plain ceiling/at-cap pickers via the T15 grant-pair
   860	  // picker (no dial). Save is disabled until BOTH picker halves are chosen. "No
   861	  // further" (next_ceiling === "none") means the ceiling becomes exactly the
   862	  // current state (resolve_grant, machine.py:87-89).
   863	  function grantControl(opts) {
   864	    var wrap = make("div", "grant-control");
   865	    wrap.setAttribute("data-grant-control", "");
   866	
   867	    // Raw values (not stateLabel'd) so the persisted grant is asserted verbatim.
   868	    var current = make(
   869	      "div", "grant-control-current",
   870	      "ceiling: " + opts.ceiling + " · at-cap: " + opts.atCap
   871	    );
   872	    current.setAttribute("data-grant-current", "");
   873	    wrap.appendChild(current);
   874	
   875	    var errorHost = make("div", "grant-control-error");
   876	
   877	    var save = make("button", "button button--primary", "Save");
   878	    save.type = "button";
   879	    save.setAttribute("data-grant-save", "");
   880	    save.disabled = true;
   881	
   882	    var picker = grantPairPicker(opts.state, function () {
   883	      save.disabled = picker.getGrant() === null;
   884	    });
   885	    wrap.appendChild(picker);
   886	
   887	    bindMutating(save, errorHost, function () {
   888	      var grant = picker.getGrant();
   889	      var ceiling = grant.next_ceiling === "none" ? opts.state : grant.next_ceiling;
   890	      return opts.onSave(ceiling, grant.at_cap);
   891	    });
   892	    wrap.appendChild(save);
   893	    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
   894	    return wrap;
   895	  }
   896	
   897	  // D11 #14: event log — events as served (ascending id), rendered in order.
   898	  function eventLog(events) {
   899	    var wrap = make("div", "event-log");
   900	    wrap.setAttribute("data-event-log", "");
   901	    if (!events || !events.length) {
   902	      wrap.appendChild(quietLine("(no events)"));
   903	      return wrap;
   904	    }
   905	    events.forEach(function (ev) {
   906	      var row = make("div", "event-log-row");
   907	      row.setAttribute("data-event-row", "");
   908	      row.setAttribute("data-event-kind", ev.kind);
   909	      row.appendChild(make("span", "event-log-kind", ev.kind));
   910	      row.appendChild(make("span", "event-log-summary", eventSummary(ev.kind, ev.payload)));
   911	      row.appendChild(make("span", "event-log-time", formatUnix(ev.created_at)));
   912	      wrap.appendChild(row);
   913	    });
   914	    return wrap;
   915	  }
   916	
   917	  // D11 #15: run history — runs as served (newest first), rendered in order.
   918	  function runHistory(runs) {
   919	    var wrap = make("div", "run-history");
   920	    wrap.setAttribute("data-run-history", "");
   921	    if (!runs || !runs.length) {
   922	      wrap.appendChild(quietLine("(no runs)"));
   923	      return wrap;
   924	    }
   925	    runs.forEach(function (run) {
   926	      var row = make("div", "run-history-row");
   927	      row.setAttribute("data-run-row", "");
   928	      row.setAttribute("data-run-status", run.status);
   929	      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-status", run.status));
   930	      var times = formatUnix(run.started_at) +
   931	        (run.ended_at ? " – " + formatUnix(run.ended_at) : "");
   932	      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-times", times));
   933	      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-detail", run.summary || run.error || ""));
   934	      wrap.appendChild(row);
   935	    });
   936	    return wrap;
   937	  }

    23	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    24	    if (className) {
    25	      node.className = className;

    35	  function marker(v) {

    41	  // Plain-text fetch for copy-text: fetchJson would JSON.parse the text body and
    42	  // reject. The reject shape only needs .code/.message for errorLine.
    43	  function fetchText(path) {
    44	    return fetch(path).then(function (r) {
    45	      if (!r.ok) {
    46	        var err = new Error("HTTP " + r.status);
    47	        err.code = "http_error";
    48	        return Promise.reject(err);
    49	      }
    50	      return r.text();
    51	    });
    52	  }

    54	  function copyToClipboard(text) {
    55	    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    56	      return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    57	    }
    58	    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    59	      var ta = el("textarea");
    60	      ta.value = text;
    61	      ta.setAttribute("readonly", "");
    62	      ta.style.position = "fixed";
    63	      ta.style.opacity = "0";
    64	      document.body.appendChild(ta);
    65	      ta.select();
    66	      var copied = false;
    67	      try {
    68	        copied = document.execCommand("copy");
    69	      } catch (e) {
    70	        copied = false;
    71	      }
    72	      document.body.removeChild(ta);
    73	      if (copied) {
    74	        resolve();
    75	      } else {
    76	        reject(new Error("copy failed"));
    77	      }
    78	    });
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  // In-flight discipline for the screen's own buttons (links/day/sprint/copy): clear
    82	  // any prior error, disable, run. On rejection re-enable and surface the error;
    83	  // returns the promise so copy can flash after a successful clipboard write. For a
    84	  // WS-mutating action the caller does NOT re-enable — the flush re-render replaces
    85	  // the DOM.
    86	  function submit(button, errorHost, fn) {
    87	    var prior = errorHost.querySelector(".error-line");
    88	    if (prior) {
    89	      errorHost.removeChild(prior);
    90	    }
    91	    button.disabled = true;
    92	    return Promise.resolve(fn()).then(
    93	      function (res) {
    94	        return res;
    95	      },
    96	      function (err) {
    97	        button.disabled = false;
    98	        errorHost.prepend(C.errorLine(err));
    99	        return Promise.reject(err);
   100	      }
   101	    );
   102	  }

   104	  function selectEl(options, selectedValue) {
   105	    var select = el("select", "form-control");
   106	    options.forEach(function (opt) {
   107	      var option = el("option", null, opt.label);
   108	      option.value = opt.value;
   109	      select.appendChild(option);
   110	    });
   111	    if (selectedValue !== undefined && selectedValue !== null) {
   112	      select.value = selectedValue;
   113	    }
   114	    return select;
   115	  }

   387	  function sprintPanel(id, detail, sprints) {
   388	    var errorHost = el("div", "ticket-sprint-error");
   389	    var wrap = el("div", "ticket-sprint");
   390	    wrap.setAttribute("data-sprint-assign", "");
   391	
   392	    if (detail.sprint_item_id !== null && detail.sprint_item_id !== undefined) {
   393	      // Parented: sprint is derived through the item; the server rejects set_sprint.
   394	      wrap.appendChild(el("div", "ticket-sprint-derived",
   395	        "sprint: " + (detail.effective_sprint_id || "(none)")));
   396	      return C.panel("Sprint", [wrap]);
   397	    }
   398	
   399	    var options = [{ value: "", label: "(none)" }];
   400	    sprints.forEach(function (s) {
   401	      options.push({ value: s.id, label: s.name });
   402	    });
   403	    var select = selectEl(options, detail.sprint_id || "");
   404	    select.setAttribute("data-sprint-select", "");
   405	
   406	    var save = el("button", "button", "Save");
   407	    save.type = "button";
   408	    save.setAttribute("data-sprint-save", "");
   409	    save.addEventListener("click", function () {
   410	      submit(save, errorHost, function () {
   411	        return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id, {
   412	          method: "PATCH",
   413	          body: { sprint_id: select.value || null }
   414	        });
   415	      });
   416	    });
   417	
   418	    wrap.appendChild(select);
   419	    wrap.appendChild(save);
   420	    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
   421	    return C.panel("Sprint", [wrap]);
   422	  }

   424	  // --- assembly --------------------------------------------------------------
   425	
   426	  function build(root, id, detail, evs, runs, sprintsRes, statusRes) {
   427	    var section = el("section", "ticket-screen");
   428	    section.setAttribute("data-screen", "ticket");
   429	    section.setAttribute("data-ticket-id", id);
   430	    section.setAttribute("data-state", detail.state);
   431	
   432	    section.appendChild(headerPanel(detail));
   433	    FIELD_NAMES.forEach(function (name) {
   434	      section.appendChild(fieldPanel(id, detail, name));
   435	    });
   436	    section.appendChild(recapPanel(id, detail));
   437	    section.appendChild(statePanel(id, detail));
   438	    section.appendChild(grantPanel(id, detail));
   439	    section.appendChild(copyPanel(id));
   440	    section.appendChild(linksPanel(id, detail));
   441	    section.appendChild(dayPanel(id, detail));
   442	    section.appendChild(sprintPanel(id, detail, sprintsRes.sprints || []));
   443	    section.appendChild(C.panel("Runs", [C.runHistory(runs.runs)]));
   444	    section.appendChild(C.panel("Events", [C.eventLog(evs.events)]));
   445	
   446	    var chatWrap = el("div", "ticket-chat");
   447	    chatWrap.setAttribute("data-chat", "");
   448	    chatWrap.appendChild(C.chatPanel(id, { available: statusRes.available }));
   449	    section.appendChild(C.panel("Chat", [chatWrap]));
   450	
   451	    root.appendChild(section);
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function render(root, params) {
   455	    var id = params.id;
   456	    Promise.all([
   457	      api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id),
   458	      api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/events"),
   459	      api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/runs"),
   460	      api.fetchJson("/api/sprints"),
   461	      api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + id + "/status")
   462	    ]).then(
   463	      function (results) {
   464	        build(root, id, results[0], results[1], results[2], results[3], results[4]);
   465	      },
   466	      function (err) {
   467	        root.appendChild(C.errorLine(err));
   468	      }
   469	    );
   470	  }
   471	
   472	  Planner.registerScreen("ticket", render);
   473	})();

    11	
    12	  // The one transient set (amendment A2): human skips + auto-skips. Reload resets
    13	  // it; the wrap rule clears it when every entry is skipped.
    14	  var skipped = {};
    15	
    16	  function key(entry) {
    17	    return entry.entity_id + ":" + entry.kind;
    18	  }
    19	
    20	  function render(root) {
    21	    root.setAttribute("data-screen", "review");
    22	    root.classList.add("review-screen");
    23	    Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/queues").then(function (q) {
    24	      var entries = q.approvals || [];   // oldest-first from the server (views.py:310)
    25	      if (!entries.length) {
    26	        showEmpty(root);
    27	        return;
    28	      }
    29	      var live = entries.filter(function (e) {
    30	        return !skipped[key(e)];
    31	      });
    32	      if (!live.length) {
    33	        // Wrap rule: skipping the last entry starts over at the oldest.
    34	        skipped = {};
    35	        live = entries;
    36	      }
    37	      show(root, live);
    38	    }, function (err) {
    39	      root.replaceChildren(c.errorLine(err));
    40	    });
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  function showEmpty(root) {
    44	    var empty = c.quietLine("nothing waiting");
    45	    empty.setAttribute("data-review-empty", "");
    46	    root.replaceChildren(empty);
    47	  }
    48	
    49	  function isStale(entry, detail) {
    50	    var kind = entry.kind;
    51	    if (kind === "review") {
    52	      return detail.state !== "needs_review";
    53	    }
    54	    if (kind === "status") {
    55	      return !detail.status_proposal;
    56	    }
    57	    // gating kinds: proposal must still be pending on the current gating field.
    58	    if (!detail.fields || !detail.fields[kind] || !detail.fields[kind].proposal) {
    59	      return true;
    60	    }
    61	    return c.gatingField(detail.state) !== kind;
    62	  }
    63	
    64	  function detailError(root, entry, err) {
    65	    // A2: detail-fetch rejection. Do NOT hide the entry and do NOT auto-refetch.
    66	    // Surface an inline error; the human decides to move on via Skip.
    67	    var wrap = document.createElement("div");
    68	    wrap.appendChild(c.errorLine(err));
    69	    wrap.appendChild(c.quietLine(entry.title));
    70	    var footer = document.createElement("div");
    71	    footer.className = "review-card-actions";
    72	    var skip = document.createElement("button");
    73	    skip.type = "button";
    74	    skip.className = "button";
    75	    skip.setAttribute("data-skip", "");
    76	    skip.textContent = "Skip";
    77	    skip.addEventListener("click", function () {
    78	      skipped[key(entry)] = true;
    79	      render(root);
    80	    });
    81	    footer.appendChild(skip);
    82	    if (entry.entity_type === "ticket") {
    83	      var open = document.createElement("a");
    84	      open.setAttribute("data-open-ticket", "");
    85	      open.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + entry.entity_id);
    86	      open.textContent = "open ticket";
    87	      footer.appendChild(open);
    88	    }
    89	    wrap.appendChild(footer);
    90	    root.replaceChildren(wrap);
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  function show(root, live) {
    94	    if (!live.length) {
    95	      showEmpty(root);
    96	      return;
    97	    }
    98	    var entry = live[0];
    99	    var path = entry.entity_type === "ticket" ? "/api/tickets/" : "/api/items/";
   100	    Planner.api.fetchJson(path + entry.entity_id).then(function (detail) {
   101	      if (isStale(entry, detail)) {
   102	        // A2 staleness guard: mark it skipped and advance WITHIN this fetched
   103	        // list — never a dead end while later entries may be live, never a
   104	        // render loop (the list strictly shrinks; the next fresh queue fetch
   105	        // drops truly-resolved entries anyway).
   106	        skipped[key(entry)] = true;
   107	        show(root, live.slice(1));
   108	        return;
   109	      }
   110	      root.replaceChildren(c.reviewCard({
   111	        entry: entry,
   112	        detail: detail,
   113	        onSkip: function () {
   114	          skipped[key(entry)] = true;
   115	          render(root);
   116	        },
   117	        onAccept: function (payload) {
   118	          return Planner.api.fetchJson(
   119	            "/api/tickets/" + entry.entity_id + "/accept/" + entry.kind,
   120	            { method: "POST", body: payload }
   121	          );
   122	        },
   123	        onApprove: function () {
   124	          return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + entry.entity_id + "/approve", {
   125	            method: "POST",
   126	            body: {}
   127	          });
   128	        },
   129	        onAcceptStatus: function () {
   130	          return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/items/" + entry.entity_id + "/accept-status", {
   131	            method: "POST",
   132	            body: {}
   133	          });
   134	        }
   135	      }));
   136	    }, function (err) {
   137	      detailError(root, entry, err);
   138	    });
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  Planner.registerScreen("review", render);
   142	})();

    20	  function appendChildren(parent, children) {

    22	      return;
    23	    }
    24	    if (Array.isArray(children)) {

    26	        if (child) {
    27	          parent.appendChild(child);
    28	        }
    29	      });
    30	    } else {
    31	      parent.appendChild(children);
    32	    }
    33	  }

   102	      return make("div", "quiet-line", "(none)");

   168	      }
   169	      span.textContent = String(value);
   170	    } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(CHIP_MARKERS, variant)) {
   171	      span.classList.add("chip--" + variant);

   175	    }

   177	  }

   160	    var chips = [
   161	      comp.chip("priority", item.priority),
   162	      comp.chip("project", item.project)
   163	    ];
   164	    if (item.deadline) {
   165	      chips.push(comp.chip("deadline", item.deadline));
   166	    }
   167	    if (item.blockers_cleared) {
   168	      chips.push(comp.chip("blockers-cleared"));
   169	    }
   170	    if (item.status_proposal) {
   171	      chips.push(comp.chip("pending-proposal"));
   172	    }
   173	    return chips;
   174	  }
   175	
   176	  function rollupText(rollup) {
   177	    var total = 0;
   178	    STATE_ORDER.forEach(function (st) {
   179	      total += rollup[st] || 0;
   180	    });
   181	    var parts = STATE_ORDER.filter(function (st) {
   182	      return rollup[st] > 0;
   183	    }).map(function (st) {
   184	      return st.replace(/_/g, " ") + " " + rollup[st];
   185	    });
   186	    var text = total === 1 ? "1 ticket" : total + " tickets";
   187	    if (parts.length) {
   188	      text += " · " + parts.join(" · ");
   189	    }
   190	    return text;
   191	  }

   202	    rollup.setAttribute("data-rollup", "");
   203	    wrap.appendChild(rollup);

   205	  }

     1	/* Hash router, screen registry, and boot. Owns the shell instance. The router
     2	 * exposes exactly Planner.registerScreen(name, render) — screens navigate via
     3	 * anchors and read params; no navigate(), no route events, no current-route
     4	 * getter. Every render derives from location.hash plus fresh fetches, so refresh
     5	 * restores state by construction. Classic script: attaches Planner.registerScreen. */
     6	(function () {
     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
     9	  var config = Planner.config;
    10	
    11	  var registry = {};
    12	  var shell = null;
    13	  var lastHash = null;
    14	
    15	  Planner.registerScreen = function (name, render) {
    16	    registry[name] = { render: render };
    17	  };
    18	
    19	  function quietLine(text) {
    20	    var div = document.createElement("div");
    21	    div.className = "quiet-line";
    22	    div.textContent = text;
    23	    return div;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  // Placeholders — T15–T17 scripts, appended after this file, re-register the real
    27	  // screens over these before DOMContentLoaded fires (overwrite-wins).
    28	  ["day", "review", "board", "ticket", "sprint", "backlog"].forEach(function (name) {
    29	    Planner.registerScreen(name, function (root) {
    30	      root.appendChild(quietLine("not built yet"));
    31	    });
    32	  });
    33	
    34	  function route() {
    35	    var hash = location.hash;
    36	    if (hash === "" || hash === "#") {
    37	      location.replace(config.ROUTES.day);
    38	      return;
    39	    }
    40	    var segments = hash.slice(1).split("/").filter(Boolean);
    41	    var name = segments[0];
    42	    var params = {};
    43	    if (name === "ticket") {
    44	      params.id = segments[1];
    45	    }
    46	    var known = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, name);
    47	    // Exact route shapes only: #/<screen> or #/ticket/<id> — no trailing extras.
    48	    var badShape = name === "ticket"
    49	      ? segments.length !== 2 || !params.id
    50	      : segments.length !== 1;
    51	    if (!known || badShape) {
    52	      shell.setActiveNav(null);
    53	      shell.content.replaceChildren(quietLine("no such screen"));
    54	      return;
    55	    }
    56	    shell.setActiveNav(name);
    57	    var entrance = hash !== lastHash;
    58	    lastHash = hash;
    59	    var screenDiv = document.createElement("div");
    60	    screenDiv.className = entrance ? "screen screen-enter" : "screen";
    61	    shell.content.replaceChildren(screenDiv);
    62	    registry[name].render(screenDiv, params);
    63	  }
    64	
    65	  function refreshBadge() {
    66	    Planner.api.fetchJson(config.API.QUEUES).then(
    67	      function (res) {
    68	        shell.setReviewBadge((res.approvals || []).length);
    69	      },
    70	      function () {
    71	        console.debug("[planner] queues fetch failed; badge unchanged");
    72	      }
    73	    );
    74	  }
    75	
    76	  function onFlush() {
    77	    refreshBadge();
    78	    route();
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  function boot() {
    82	    shell = Planner.components.appShell();
    83	    document.getElementById("app").appendChild(shell);
    84	
    85	    Planner.api.fetchJson(config.API.META).then(
    86	      function (meta) {
    87	        return meta.ui_debounce_ms;
    88	      },
    89	      function () {
    90	        console.debug("[planner] meta fetch failed; using default debounce");
    91	        return config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    92	      }
    93	    ).then(function (debounceMs) {
    94	      Planner.bus.onInvalidate(onFlush);
    95	      Planner.bus.start(debounceMs);
    96	      window.addEventListener("hashchange", route);
    97	      refreshBadge();
    98	      route();
    99	    });
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
   103	    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
   104	  } else {
   105	    boot();
   106	  }
   107	})();

     1	"""The dispatch data layer over sqlite3 — claims (§7.3), heartbeat, reclaim, run
     2	close + circuit breaker (§7.5), and candidate assembly (§7.2 inputs). Config values
     3	(ttl_seconds, failure_limit) are always parameters; this module never reads config or
     4	the clock — every function takes now: int.
     5	
     6	Single-winner guarantees come from two CAS statements: the claim CAS on
     7	tickets.claim_lock and the finalize CAS on runs.status, each one atomic UPDATE.
     8	Follow-up statements run only on the CAS winner's path, so they cannot double-run."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import json
    13	import sqlite3
    14	from collections.abc import Callable
    15	from typing import Final, NamedTuple
    16	
    17	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict, Priority
    18	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    19	from planner.core.events import append_event
    20	from planner.core.ids import new_claim, new_id
    21	from planner.core.links import blocked_target_ids
    22	from planner.dispatch.contracts import (
    23	    AGENT_CLOSE_OUTCOMES,
    24	    FAILURE_STATUSES,
    25	    DispatchCandidate,
    26	    RunStatus,
    27	)
    28	from planner.dispatch.logic import (
    29	    expiry_at,
    30	    gating_field_pending,
    31	    has_active_claim,
    32	    is_expired,
    33	    next_breaker_state,
    34	)
    35	from planner.tickets.contracts import AtCap, TicketState
    36	
    37	# The statuses close_run accepts: agent outcomes plus failures (D20). running and
    38	# reclaimed are rejected — reclaimed is reachable only through sweep_reclaims.
    39	_CLOSABLE: Final[frozenset[RunStatus]] = AGENT_CLOSE_OUTCOMES | FAILURE_STATUSES
    40	
    41	
    42	def claim(
    43	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    44	    ticket_id: str,
    45	    now: int,
    46	    ttl_seconds: int,
    47	    pid: int | None = None,
    48	) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    49	    """§7.3 claim: the CAS is the sole arbiter against double-claim. Returns
    50	    (run_id, claim_token) on the win, or None when lost (rowcount 0 — indistinguishable
    51	    from an unknown ticket id, D13). No eligibility re-check, no updated_at bump (D7)."""
    52	    token = new_claim()
    53	    expires = expiry_at(now, ttl_seconds)
    54	    cursor = conn.execute(
    55	        "UPDATE tickets SET claim_lock=?, claim_expires=? WHERE id=? AND claim_lock IS NULL",
    56	        (token, expires, ticket_id),
    57	    )
    58	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
    59	        return None
    60	    run_id = new_id("run")
    61	    conn.execute(
    62	        "INSERT INTO runs (id, ticket_id, status, started_at, ended_at, summary, error, pid) "
    63	        "VALUES (?, ?, 'running', ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?)",
    64	        (run_id, ticket_id, now, pid),
    65	    )
    66	    append_event(conn, ticket_id, EventKind.run_started, {"run_id": run_id, "pid": pid}, now)
    67	    return run_id, token
    68	
    69	
    70	def heartbeat(conn: sqlite3.Connection, run_id: str, now: int, ttl_seconds: int) -> int:
    71	    """Extend the lease by exactly one TTL from now (D2), returning the new expiry.
    72	    A missing run is not_found; a non-running run or an expired/cleared claim is
    73	    stale_claim (D3) — an expired lease is never revived by a late heartbeat."""
    74	    row = conn.execute("SELECT ticket_id, status FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
    75	    if row is None:
    76	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
    77	    if str(row["status"]) != RunStatus.running.value:
    78	        raise PlannerError(
    79	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
    80	            "run is not running",
    81	            {"run_id": run_id, "run_status": str(row["status"])},
    82	        )
    83	    ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
    84	    ticket = conn.execute(
    85	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
    86	    ).fetchone()
    87	    claim_lock = str(ticket["claim_lock"]) if ticket["claim_lock"] is not None else None
    88	    claim_expires = int(ticket["claim_expires"]) if ticket["claim_expires"] is not None else None
    89	    if not has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now):
    90	        raise PlannerError(
    91	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
    92	            "claim expired or cleared",
    93	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "claim_expires": claim_expires},
    94	        )
    95	    new_expires = expiry_at(now, ttl_seconds)
    96	    conn.execute("UPDATE tickets SET claim_expires=? WHERE id=?", (new_expires, ticket_id))
    97	    append_event(
    98	        conn,
    99	        ticket_id,
   100	        EventKind.claim_heartbeat,
   101	        {"run_id": run_id, "claim_expires": new_expires},
   102	        now,
   103	    )
   104	    return new_expires
   105	
   106	
   107	class _Finalized(NamedTuple):
   108	    ticket_id: str
   109	    consecutive_failures: int
   110	    newly_auto_blocked: bool
   111	
   112	
   113	def _finalize_run(
   114	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   115	    run_id: str,
   116	    status: RunStatus,
   117	    now: int,
   118	    summary: str | None,
   119	    error: str | None,
   120	    failure_limit: int,
   121	) -> _Finalized | None:
   122	    """The single door for runs.status + breaker writes (D5). The runs-UPDATE is a
   123	    CAS guarded by status='running', so a close racing a reclaim has exactly one
   124	    winner; the loser gets rowcount 0 and None back. Returns None on a missing run or
   125	    a lost CAS — the caller decides what that means."""
   126	    row = conn.execute("SELECT ticket_id FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
   127	    if row is None:
   128	        return None
   129	    ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
   130	    cursor = conn.execute(
   131	        "UPDATE runs SET status=?, ended_at=?, summary=?, error=? WHERE id=? AND status='running'",
   132	        (status.value, now, summary, error, run_id),
   133	    )
   134	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   135	        return None
   136	    trow = conn.execute(
   137	        "SELECT consecutive_failures, auto_blocked FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   138	    ).fetchone()
   139	    current = int(trow["consecutive_failures"])
   140	    was_blocked = bool(trow["auto_blocked"])
   141	    new_failures, tripped = next_breaker_state(current, status, failure_limit)
   142	    newly = tripped and not was_blocked
   143	    # auto_blocked is sticky: once set it is never written back to 0 here (only
   144	    # clear_auto_block does that).
   145	    flag = 1 if (was_blocked or tripped) else 0
   146	    # Clear the lease and apply the breaker in one statement (no updated_at, D7). The
   147	    # unconditional lock clear is safe: a newer claim needs claim_lock IS NULL, which
   148	    # needs this run finalized first — the CAS above established we are that finalizer.
   149	    conn.execute(
   150	        "UPDATE tickets SET claim_lock=NULL, claim_expires=NULL, "
   151	        "consecutive_failures=?, auto_blocked=? WHERE id=?",
   152	        (new_failures, flag, ticket_id),
   153	    )
   154	    return _Finalized(ticket_id, new_failures, newly)
   155	
   156	
   157	def close_run(
   158	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   159	    run_id: str,
   160	    status: RunStatus,
   161	    now: int,
   162	    failure_limit: int,
   163	    summary: str | None = None,
   164	    error: str | None = None,
   165	) -> None:
   166	    """Close a run with an agent outcome or failure status (§7.5). A1: an expired
   167	    claim can never write — the close is rejected (stale_claim) before finalizing,
   168	    leaving the run for sweep_reclaims. Emits run_closed, then auto_blocked only on the
   169	    breaker's 0->1 trip."""
   170	    if status not in _CLOSABLE:
   171	        raise PlannerError(
   172	            ErrorCode.validation, "not a closeable run status", {"status": status.value}
   173	        )
   174	    row = conn.execute("SELECT status, ticket_id FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
   175	    if row is None:
   176	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "run not found", {"run_id": run_id})
   177	    current_status = str(row["status"])
   178	    if current_status != RunStatus.running.value:
   179	        raise PlannerError(
   180	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
   181	            "run is not running",
   182	            {"run_id": run_id, "run_status": current_status},
   183	        )
   184	    ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
   185	    ticket = conn.execute(
   186	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   187	    ).fetchone()
   188	    claim_lock = str(ticket["claim_lock"]) if ticket["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   189	    claim_expires = int(ticket["claim_expires"]) if ticket["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   190	    if not has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now):
   191	        raise PlannerError(
   192	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
   193	            "claim expired or cleared",
   194	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "claim_expires": claim_expires},
   195	        )
   196	    fin = _finalize_run(conn, run_id, status, now, summary, error, failure_limit)
   197	    if fin is None:
   198	        raise PlannerError(
   199	            ErrorCode.stale_claim,
   200	            "run is not running",
   201	            {"run_id": run_id, "run_status": current_status},
   202	        )
   203	    append_event(
   204	        conn,
   205	        fin.ticket_id,
   206	        EventKind.run_closed,
   207	        {"run_id": run_id, "status": status.value, "summary": summary},
   208	        now,
   209	    )
   210	    if fin.newly_auto_blocked:
   211	        append_event(
   212	            conn,
   213	            fin.ticket_id,
   214	            EventKind.auto_blocked,
   215	            {"consecutive_failures": fin.consecutive_failures},
   216	            now,
   217	        )
   218	
   219	
   220	def sweep_reclaims(
   221	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   222	    now: int,
   223	    pid_alive: Callable[[int], bool],
   224	    failure_limit: int,
   225	) -> list[str]:
   226	    """Stage-4 tick primitive: reclaim every running run whose claim expired (reason
   227	    'expired', checked first — D14) or whose pid is dead (reason 'dead_pid'). Returns
   228	    the reclaimed run ids. reclaimed leaves the breaker unchanged (D6); a run whose
   229	    finalize CAS was lost to a legitimate close is skipped silently."""
   230	    rows = conn.execute(
   231	        "SELECT r.id AS run_id, r.pid, r.ticket_id, t.claim_expires "
   232	        "FROM runs r JOIN tickets t ON t.id = r.ticket_id "
   233	        "WHERE r.status = 'running' AND t.claim_lock IS NOT NULL"
   234	    ).fetchall()
   235	    reclaimed: list[str] = []
   236	    for row in rows:
   237	        run_id = str(row["run_id"])
   238	        ticket_id = str(row["ticket_id"])
   239	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   240	        pid = int(row["pid"]) if row["pid"] is not None else None
   241	        if is_expired(claim_expires, now):
   242	            reason = "expired"
   243	        elif pid is not None and not pid_alive(pid):
   244	            reason = "dead_pid"
   245	        else:
   246	            continue
   247	        fin = _finalize_run(conn, run_id, RunStatus.reclaimed, now, None, None, failure_limit)
   248	        if fin is None:
   249	            continue
   250	        append_event(
   251	            conn,
   252	            ticket_id,
   253	            EventKind.claim_reclaimed,
   254	            {"run_id": run_id, "reason": reason},
   255	            now,
   256	        )
   257	        reclaimed.append(run_id)
   258	    return reclaimed
   259

   121	def ticket_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   122	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   123	    detail = ticket_json(ticket, now)
   124	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   125	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   126	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   127	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   128	    ).fetchall()
   129	    day_rows = conn.execute(
   130	        "SELECT day_id FROM day_tickets WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY day_id ASC", (ticket_id,)
   131	    ).fetchall()
   132	    total = int(
   133	        conn.execute(
   134	            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ?", (ticket_id,)
   135	        ).fetchone()["n"]
   136	    )
   137	    running = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT 1 FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? AND status = 'running' LIMIT 1", (ticket_id,)
   139	    ).fetchone()
   140	    latest = conn.execute(
   141	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
   142	        (ticket_id,),
   143	    ).fetchone()
   144	    detail.update(
   145	        {
   146	            "blocked": core_links.is_blocked(conn, ticket_id),
   147	            "effective_sprint_id": tickets_data.get_effective_sprint_id(conn, ticket_id),
   148	            "links": [
   149	                {"from_id": str(r["from_id"]), "to_id": str(r["to_id"]), "kind": str(r["kind"])}
   150	                for r in link_rows
   151	            ],
   152	            "day_ids": [str(r["day_id"]) for r in day_rows],
   153	            "run_summary": {
   154	                "total": total,
   155	                "running": running is not None,
   156	                "latest": run_json(latest) if latest is not None else None,
   157	            },
   158	        }
   159	    )
   160	    return detail

   212	        f"\n"
   213	        f"result:\n{show(fields.result.value)}\n"
   214	        f"\n"
   215	        f"recap:\n{show(ticket.recap)}\n"
   216	        f"\n"
   217	        f"links:\n{links_block}\n"
   218	    )

   223	
   224	def board_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   225	    rows = conn.execute(
   226	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, fields, claim_lock, "
   227	        "claim_expires, created_at FROM tickets WHERE state != 'dropped'"
   228	    ).fetchall()
   229	    by_state: dict[str, list[tuple[tuple[int, int, str, int], JsonDict]]] = {
   230	        s.value: [] for s in STATE_ORDER
   231	    }
   232	    for row in rows:
   233	        state = str(row["state"])
   234	        priority = str(row["priority"])
   235	        deadline = str(row["deadline"]) if row["deadline"] is not None else None
   236	        fields = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   237	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   238	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   239	        card: JsonDict = {
   240	            "id": str(row["id"]),
   241	            "title": str(row["title"]),
   242	            "priority": priority,
   243	            "deadline": deadline,
   244	            "project": str(row["project"]) if row["project"] is not None else None,
   245	            "has_pending_proposal": gating_field_pending(TicketState(state), fields),
   246	            "has_running_claim": has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   247	        }
   248	        sort_key = (
   249	            _prio_rank(priority),
   250	            0 if deadline is not None else 1,
   251	            deadline or "",
   252	            int(row["created_at"]),
   253	        )
   254	        by_state[state].append((sort_key, card))
   255	    columns: list[JsonDict] = []
   256	    for s in STATE_ORDER:
   257	        cards = [card for _, card in sorted(by_state[s.value], key=lambda item: item[0])]
   258	        columns.append({"state": s.value, "cards": cards})
   259	    return {"columns": columns}

   265	def _entity_type(entity_id: str) -> str:
   266	    return "ticket" if entity_id.split("_", 1)[0] == "t" else "item"

   268	
   269	def _approvals(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict]) -> list[JsonDict]:
   270	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   271	        "SELECT id, title, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets "
   272	        "WHERE state NOT IN ('done','dropped') ORDER BY id"
   273	    ).fetchall()
   274	    ticket_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   275	    ticket_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   276	    ticket_updated: dict[str, int] = {}
   277	    for r in ticket_rows:
   278	        tid = str(r["id"])
   279	        ticket_digest.append(
   280	            {
   281	                "id": tid,
   282	                "state": str(r["state"]),
   283	                "fields": json.loads(str(r["fields"])),
   284	                "updated_at": int(r["updated_at"]),
   285	            }
   286	        )
   287	        ticket_title[tid] = str(r["title"])
   288	        ticket_updated[tid] = int(r["updated_at"])
   289	    item_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   290	    item_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   291	    for r in item_approval_rows:
   292	        iid = str(r["id"])
   293	        item_digest.append({"id": iid, "status_proposal": r["status_proposal"]})
   294	        item_title[iid] = str(r["title"])
   295	    digest = approvals_digest(ticket_digest, item_digest)
   296	    # A5: review entries use the last state_changed->needs_review event time, not the
   297	    # updated_at proxy; the boundary digest keeps its documented proxy.
   298	    for entry in digest:
   299	        if entry["kind"] == "review":
   300	            tid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   301	            row = conn.execute(
   302	                "SELECT created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? AND kind = 'state_changed' "
   303	                "AND json_extract(payload, '$.to') = 'needs_review' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
   304	                (tid,),
   305	            ).fetchone()
   306	            entry["waiting_since"] = (
   307	                int(row["created_at"]) if row is not None
   308	                else ticket_updated.get(tid, entry["waiting_since"])
   309	            )
   310	    digest.sort(key=lambda e: e["waiting_since"])
   311	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   312	    for entry in digest:
   313	        eid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   314	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   315	        title = ticket_title.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_title.get(eid)
   316	        result.append(
   317	            {
   318	                "entity_id": eid,
   319	                "entity_type": etype,
   320	                "kind": entry["kind"],
   321	                "title": title,
   322	                "waiting_since": entry["waiting_since"],
   323	            }
   324	        )
   325	    return result
   326

   328	def _pickup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> list[JsonDict]:
   329	    candidates = dispatch_data.load_candidates(conn, now)
   330	    eligible = [c for c in candidates if is_eligible(c)]
   331	    eligible.sort(key=ordering_key)
   332	    if not eligible:
   333	        return []
   334	    ids = [c.ticket_id for c in eligible]
   335	    placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in ids)
   336	    rows = conn.execute(
   337	        f"SELECT id, title FROM tickets WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", tuple(ids)
   338	    ).fetchall()
   339	    title_by_id = {str(r["id"]): str(r["title"]) for r in rows}
   340	    return [
   341	        {
   342	            "ticket_id": c.ticket_id,
   343	            "title": title_by_id.get(c.ticket_id, ""),
   344	            "state": c.state.value,
   345	            "priority": c.priority.value,
   346	            "deadline": c.deadline,
   347	        }
   348	        for c in eligible
   349	    ]

     1	"""The only module that writes ticket rows. State, ceiling/at_cap, and fields
     2	value mutations happen in exactly one function (_apply_decision); every public
     3	writer is one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction. Plain field writers (note, recap,
     4	priority, deadline, sprint) do their own single-column UPDATE and never touch
     5	state/ceiling/at_cap/fields.value. sqlite3, events and ids live here only; the
     6	clock arrives as now (unix seconds) and the title limit as an argument."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import sqlite3
    11	from collections.abc import Iterator
    12	from contextlib import contextmanager
    13	
    14	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, Priority, Project
    15	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    16	from planner.core.events import append_event
    17	from planner.core.ids import ID_PREFIXES, new_id
    18	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    19	    AtCap,
    20	    FieldName,
    21	    FieldSlot,
    22	    NextCeiling,
    23	    Ticket,

    28	from planner.tickets.logic.decisions import Decision

    30	
    31	@contextmanager
    32	def _txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    33	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    34	    try:
    35	        yield
    36	    except BaseException:
    37	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    38	        raise
    39	    else:
    40	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    41	
    42	
    43	def _row_to_ticket(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Ticket:
    44	    project_raw = row["project"]
    45	    return Ticket(
    46	        id=row["id"],
    47	        title=row["title"],
    48	        state=TicketState(row["state"]),
    49	        priority=Priority(row["priority"]),
    50	        deadline=row["deadline"],
    51	        project=Project(project_raw) if project_raw is not None else None,
    52	        sprint_item_id=row["sprint_item_id"],
    53	        sprint_id=row["sprint_id"],
    54	        recap=row["recap"],
    55	        ceiling=TicketState(row["ceiling"]),
    56	        at_cap=AtCap(row["at_cap"]),
    57	        auto_blocked=bool(row["auto_blocked"]),
    58	        consecutive_failures=row["consecutive_failures"],
    59	        chat_session_key=row["chat_session_key"],
    60	        alias=row["alias"],
    61	        fields=fields_codec.fields_from_json(row["fields"]),
    62	        claim_lock=row["claim_lock"],
    63	        claim_expires=row["claim_expires"],
    64	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    65	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    66	    )
    67	
    68	
    69	def _load_ticket(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> Ticket:
    70	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM tickets WHERE id = ?", (ticket_id,)).fetchone()
    71	    if row is None:
    72	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
    73	    return _row_to_ticket(row)
    74	
    75	
    76	def _apply_decision(
    77	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket: Ticket, decision: Decision, now: int
    78	) -> Ticket:
    79	    new_fields = decision.new_fields if decision.new_fields is not None else ticket.fields
    80	    new_state = decision.new_state if decision.new_state is not None else ticket.state
    81	    new_ceiling = decision.new_ceiling if decision.new_ceiling is not None else ticket.ceiling
    82	    new_at_cap = decision.new_at_cap if decision.new_at_cap is not None else ticket.at_cap
    83	    conn.execute(
    84	        "UPDATE tickets SET fields = ?, state = ?, ceiling = ?, at_cap = ?, updated_at = ? "
    85	        "WHERE id = ?",
    86	        (
    87	            fields_codec.fields_to_json(new_fields),
    88	            new_state.value,
    89	            new_ceiling.value,
    90	            new_at_cap.value,
    91	            now,
    92	            ticket.id,
    93	        ),
    94	    )
    95	    for spec in decision.events:
    96	        append_event(conn, ticket.id, spec.kind, spec.payload, now)
    97	    return _load_ticket(conn, ticket.id)
    98	
    99	
   100	def create_ticket(
   101	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   102	    *,
   103	    title: str,
   104	    actor: str,
   105	    now: int,
   106	    title_max_chars: int,
   107	    project: Project | None = None,
   108	    priority: Priority = Priority.P3,
   109	    deadline: str | None = None,
   110	    sprint_id: str | None = None,
   111	    sprint_item_id: str | None = None,
   112	) -> Ticket:
   113	    admission.validate_title(title, title_max_chars)
   114	    admission.validate_deadline(deadline)
   115	    ticket_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["ticket"])
   116	    empty_fields = fields_codec.fields_to_json(TicketFields())
   117	    with _txn(conn):
   118	        if sprint_item_id is not None:
   119	            if conn.execute(
   120	                "SELECT 1 FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (sprint_item_id,)
   121	            ).fetchone() is None:
   122	                raise PlannerError(
   123	                    ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint item not found", {"sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id}
   124	                )
   125	            if sprint_id is not None:
   126	                raise PlannerError(
   127	                    ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
   128	                    "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
   129	                    {"sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
   130	                )
   131	            if project is not None:
   132	                raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is derived when parented")
   133	        elif sprint_id is not None:
   134	            exists = conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM sprints WHERE id = ?", (sprint_id,)).fetchone()
   135	            if exists is None:
   136	                raise PlannerError(
   137	                    ErrorCode.not_found, "sprint not found", {"sprint_id": sprint_id}
   138	                )
   139	        conn.execute(
   140	            "INSERT INTO tickets (id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, sprint_item_id, "
   141	            "sprint_id, recap, ceiling, at_cap, auto_blocked, consecutive_failures, "
   142	            "chat_session_key, alias, fields, claim_lock, claim_expires, created_at, updated_at) "
   143	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, '', ?, ?, 0, 0, NULL, NULL, ?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)",
   144	            (
   145	                ticket_id,
   146	                title,
   147	                TicketState.needs_success.value,
   148	                priority.value,
   149	                deadline,
   150	                project.value if project is not None else None,
   151	                sprint_item_id,
   152	                sprint_id,
   153	                TicketState.needs_success.value,
   154	                AtCap.propose.value,
   155	                empty_fields,
   156	                now,
   157	                now,
   158	            ),
   159	        )
   160	        append_event(conn, ticket_id, EventKind.ticket_created, {}, now)
   161	        return _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   162	
   163	
   164	def read_ticket(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> Ticket:
   165	    return _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   166	
   167	
   168	def get_effective_sprint_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str | None:
   169	    ticket = _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   170	    if ticket.sprint_item_id is None:
   171	        return ticket.sprint_id
   172	    row = conn.execute(
   173	        "SELECT sprint_id FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (ticket.sprint_item_id,)
   174	    ).fetchone()
   175	    if row is None:
   176	        return None
   177	    sprint_id: str | None = row["sprint_id"]
   178	    return sprint_id
   179

   181	def file_proposal(
   182	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, *, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   183	) -> Ticket:

   246	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,

   249	    field: FieldName,

   251	    actor: str,

   258	        new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)

   260	            "UPDATE tickets SET fields = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",

   262	        )
   263	        append_event(conn, ticket_id, EventKind.note_updated, {"field": field.value}, now)
   264	        return _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)

   323	) -> Ticket:

     1	"""§4.4 resolution semantics as pure Decisions. _accept_gating_proposal is the
     2	sole constructor of any Decision that traverses the gating-acceptance edges 1-5
     3	(auto and human alike); _state_change is the sole constructor of state_changed
     4	EventSpecs. Together with data._apply_decision (the sole appender) this is the
     5	"one canonical writer per transition" guarantee (§4.4.6)."""

    26	CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "auto_accept"
    27	CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "human_accept"
    28	CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE: Final[str] = "review_approve"
    29	CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP: Final[str] = "human_state_jump"
    30	CAUSE_DROP: Final[str] = "drop"
    31	CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT: Final[str] = "onward_grant"
    32	CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT: Final[str] = "human_grant"

    37	def _state_change(old: TicketState, new: TicketState, cause: str) -> EventSpec:
    38	    return EventSpec(EventKind.state_changed, {"from": old.value, "to": new.value, "cause": cause})
    39	
    40	
    41	def _accept_gating_proposal(
    42	    ticket: Ticket,
    43	    field: FieldName,
    44	    stored_body: str,
    45	    resolved_by: str,
    46	    edited: bool,
    47	    grant: GrantPair | None,
    48	    cause: str,
    49	    superseded_body: str | None = None,
    50	) -> Decision:
    51	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
    52	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
    53	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
    54	    new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
    55	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
    56	    if superseded_body is not None:
    57	        events.append(
    58	            EventSpec(
    59	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
    60	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
    61	            )
    62	        )
    63	    events.append(
    64	        EventSpec(
    65	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
    66	            {
    67	                "field": field.value,
    68	                "body": stored_body,
    69	                "resolved_by": resolved_by,
    70	                "edited": edited,
    71	            },
    72	        )
    73	    )
    74	    events.append(_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, cause))
    75	    new_ceiling: TicketState | None = None
    76	    new_at_cap: AtCap | None = None
    77	    if grant is not None:
    78	        ceiling = grant.next_ceiling
    79	        assert isinstance(ceiling, TicketState)  # resolve_grant always yields a concrete state
    80	        new_ceiling = ceiling
    81	        new_at_cap = grant.at_cap
    82	        events.append(
    83	            EventSpec(
    84	                EventKind.grant_changed,
    85	                {
    86	                    "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    87	                    "at_cap": grant.at_cap.value,
    88	                    "cause": CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT,
    89	                },
    90	            )
    91	        )
    92	    return Decision(
    93	        events=tuple(events),
    94	        new_fields=new_fields,
    95	        new_state=new_state,
    96	        new_ceiling=new_ceiling,
    97	        new_at_cap=new_at_cap,
    98	    )
    99	
   100	
   101	def decide_file_proposal(
   102	    ticket: Ticket, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   103	) -> Decision:
   104	    admission.validate_body(body, "proposal body")
   105	    admission.check_agent_proposal(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, ticket.at_cap, field)
   106	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   107	    superseded_body = slot.proposal.body if slot.proposal is not None else None
   108	    if machine.auto_accept_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, field) is not None:
   109	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   110	            ticket,
   111	            field,
   112	            stored_body=body,
   113	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_AUTO,
   114	            edited=False,
   115	            grant=None,
   116	            cause=CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT,
   117	            superseded_body=superseded_body,
   118	        )
   119	    new_slot = FieldSlot(
   120	        value=slot.value,
   121	        proposal=Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=actor, created_at=now),
   122	        notes=slot.notes,
   123	    )
   124	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   125	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
   126	    if superseded_body is not None:
   127	        events.append(
   128	            EventSpec(
   129	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   130	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
   131	            )
   132	        )
   133	    events.append(
   134	        EventSpec(
   135	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   136	            {"field": field.value, "body": body, "proposed_by": actor},
   137	        )
   138	    )
   139	    return Decision(events=tuple(events), new_fields=new_fields)
   140	
   141	
   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")
   216	    if ticket.state is TicketState.done:
   217	        raise PlannerError(
   218	            ErrorCode.validation, "done tickets cannot be dropped", {"state": "done"}
   219	        )
   220	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.validation, "ticket is already dropped", {"state": "dropped"}
   223	        )
   224	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.dropped, CAUSE_DROP),)
   225	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.dropped)
   226	
   227	
   228	def decide_grant_change(
   229	    ticket: Ticket, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, actor: str
   230	) -> Decision:
   231	    admission.require_human(actor, "change_grant")
   232	    machine.validate_ceiling(ceiling)
   233	    events = (
   234	        EventSpec(
   235	            EventKind.grant_changed,
   236	            {"ceiling": ceiling.value, "at_cap": at_cap.value, "cause": CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT},
   237	        ),
   238	    )
   239	    return Decision(events=events, new_ceiling=ceiling, new_at_cap=at_cap)

    13	
    14	HUMAN_ACTOR: Final[str] = "human"
    15	
    16	
    17	def is_human(actor: str) -> bool:
    18	    return actor == HUMAN_ACTOR
    19	
    20	
    21	def require_human(actor: str, action: str) -> None:
    22	    if actor != HUMAN_ACTOR:
    23	        raise PlannerError(
    24	            ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
    25	            f"{action} is a human-only action",
    26	            {"action": action, "actor": actor},
    27	        )

    29	
    30	def check_agent_proposal(
    31	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, field: FieldName
    32	) -> None:
    33	    if machine.is_terminal(state):
    34	        raise PlannerError(
    35	            ErrorCode.validation, "no proposals on a terminal ticket", {"state": state.value}
    36	        )
    37	    gating = machine.gating_field(state)
    38	    if not machine.at_or_beyond_ceiling(state, ceiling):
    39	        return
    40	    if at_cap is AtCap.stop:
    41	        raise PlannerError(
    42	            ErrorCode.at_cap_stop,
    43	            "ticket is at its ceiling with at_cap=stop",
    44	            {
    45	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    46	                "state": state.value,
    47	                "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    48	                "at_cap": "stop",
    49	            },
    50	        )
    51	    if field is not gating:
    52	        raise PlannerError(
    53	            ErrorCode.validation,
    54	            "at the ceiling agents may propose only the current gating field",
    55	            {
    56	                "field": field.value,
    57	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    58	                "state": state.value,
    59	            },
    60	        )
    61	
    62	
    63	def check_recap_writable(state: TicketState) -> None:
    64	    if state is TicketState.needs_success or state is TicketState.dropped:
    65	        raise PlannerError(
    66	            ErrorCode.recap_too_early,
    67	            "recap is writable only past needs_success",
    68	            {"state": state.value},
    69	        )
    70	
    71	
    72	def check_sprint_assignable(ticket_id: str, sprint_item_id: str | None) -> None:
    73	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
    74	        raise PlannerError(
    75	            ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
    76	            "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
    77	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
    78	        )
    79	
    80	
    81	def validate_title(title: str, max_chars: int) -> None:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "title must be non-empty")
    84	    if len(title) > max_chars:
    85	        raise PlannerError(
    86	            ErrorCode.title_too_long,
    87	            f"title exceeds {max_chars} characters",
    88	            {"length": len(title), "max": max_chars},
    89	        )
    90	
    91	
    92	def validate_deadline(deadline: str | None) -> None:
    93	    if deadline is None:

    95	    try:

    97	    except ValueError as exc:
    98	        raise PlannerError(

   100	        ) from exc

     1	"""State-machine math for §4.2/§4.3/§4.4: linear order indexing, gating and
     2	advance tables, the auto-accept condition, ceiling comparison, and onward-grant
     3	resolution. Pure: contracts/errors + fields_codec only."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
     8	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
     9	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    10	    GATING_FIELD,
    11	    NO_FURTHER,
    12	    STATE_ORDER,
    13	    AtCap,
    14	    FieldName,
    15	    GrantPair,
    16	    NextCeiling,
    17	    TicketFields,
    18	    TicketState,
    19	)
    20	from planner.tickets.logic import fields_codec
    21	
    22	
    23	def state_index(state: TicketState) -> int:
    24	    try:
    25	        return STATE_ORDER.index(state)
    26	    except ValueError as exc:
    27	        raise PlannerError(
    28	            ErrorCode.validation, "state outside the linear order", {"state": state.value}
    29	        ) from exc
    30	
    31	
    32	def is_terminal(state: TicketState) -> bool:
    33	    return state in (TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped)
    34	
    35	
    36	def gating_field(state: TicketState) -> FieldName | None:
    37	    return GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    38	
    39	
    40	def advance_target(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> TicketState:
    41	    if state is TicketState.in_progress and ceiling is TicketState.done:
    42	        return TicketState.done
    43	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET.get(state)
    44	    if target is None:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.validation, "state has no advance target", {"state": state.value}
    47	        )
    48	    return target
    49	
    50	
    51	def auto_accept_target(
    52	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, field: FieldName
    53	) -> TicketState | None:
    54	    if is_terminal(state):
    55	        return None
    56	    if field is not gating_field(state):
    57	        return None
    58	    target = advance_target(state, ceiling)
    59	    if state_index(target) > state_index(ceiling):
    60	        return None
    61	    return target
    62	
    63	
    64	def at_or_beyond_ceiling(state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState) -> bool:
    65	    return state_index(state) >= state_index(ceiling)
    66	
    67	
    68	def validate_ceiling(ceiling: TicketState) -> None:
    69	    if ceiling not in STATE_ORDER:
    70	        raise PlannerError(
    71	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "ceiling must be a linear state", {"ceiling": ceiling.value}
    72	        )
    73	
    74	
    75	def resolve_grant(
    76	    new_state: TicketState, next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None, at_cap: AtCap | None
    77	) -> GrantPair:
    78	    if next_ceiling is None or at_cap is None:
    79	        missing: list[str] = []
    80	        if next_ceiling is None:
    81	            missing.append("next_ceiling")
    82	        if at_cap is None:
    83	            missing.append("at_cap")
    84	        raise PlannerError(
    85	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "accept requires the onward grant pair", {"missing": missing}
    86	        )
    87	    if next_ceiling == NO_FURTHER:
    88	        # the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state
    89	        return GrantPair(next_ceiling=new_state, at_cap=at_cap)
    90	    if not isinstance(next_ceiling, TicketState):
    91	        raise PlannerError(
    92	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": str(next_ceiling)}
    93	        )
    94	    if next_ceiling not in STATE_ORDER or state_index(next_ceiling) < state_index(new_state):
    95	        raise PlannerError(
    96	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid,
    97	            "next_ceiling must be at or beyond the new state",
    98	            {"next_ceiling": next_ceiling.value, "new_state": new_state.value},
    99	        )
   100	    return GrantPair(next_ceiling=next_ceiling, at_cap=at_cap)
   101	
   102	
   103	def has_pending_gating_proposal(state: TicketState, fields: TicketFields) -> bool:
   104	    field = gating_field(state)
   105	    if field is None:
   106	        return False
   107	    return fields_codec.get_slot(fields, field).proposal is not None

     1	"""Ticket domain shapes: the state machine order, the four fields, the grant pair,
     2	and the ticket row. Stdlib only; Priority/Project are imported from core (shared
     3	vocabulary), never redeclared."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     8	from enum import StrEnum
     9	from typing import Final, Literal
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import Priority, Project
    12	
    13	
    14	class TicketState(StrEnum):        # §4.1, exact order
    15	    needs_success = "needs_success"
    16	    needs_approach = "needs_approach"
    17	    needs_plan = "needs_plan"
    18	    in_progress = "in_progress"
    19	    needs_review = "needs_review"
    20	    done = "done"
    21	    dropped = "dropped"            # terminal, human-only, outside the linear order
    22	
    23	
    24	# Linear pipeline order (dropped excluded). Index comparison implements "<= ceiling".
    25	# ceiling and next_ceiling values are restricted to members of this tuple (never dropped).
    26	STATE_ORDER: Final[tuple[TicketState, ...]] = (
    27	    TicketState.needs_success, TicketState.needs_approach, TicketState.needs_plan,
    28	    TicketState.in_progress, TicketState.needs_review, TicketState.done,
    29	)
    30	
    31	
    32	class FieldName(StrEnum):          # §4.2 — the exactly-four field keys
    33	    success = "success"
    34	    approach = "approach"
    35	    plan = "plan"
    36	    result = "result"
    37	
    38	
    39	class AtCap(StrEnum):              # §4.3
    40	    stop = "stop"
    41	    propose = "propose"
    42	
    43	
    44	# §4.2 table — gating field per pre-terminal state. needs_review has none (human approve).
    45	GATING_FIELD: Final[dict[TicketState, FieldName]] = {
    46	    TicketState.needs_success: FieldName.success,
    47	    TicketState.needs_approach: FieldName.approach,
    48	    TicketState.needs_plan: FieldName.plan,
    49	    TicketState.in_progress: FieldName.result,
    50	}
    51	
    52	# §4.2 table — accepted proposal advances to. in_progress -> needs_review is the
    53	# default route; the ceiling=done special case (§4.4.5) is resolution-engine logic,
    54	# not a second table entry.
    55	ADVANCE_TARGET: Final[dict[TicketState, TicketState]] = {
    56	    TicketState.needs_success: TicketState.needs_approach,
    57	    TicketState.needs_approach: TicketState.needs_plan,
    58	    TicketState.needs_plan: TicketState.in_progress,
    59	    TicketState.in_progress: TicketState.needs_review,
    60	}
    61	
    62	
    63	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    64	class Proposal:                    # §4.2 proposal slot
    65	    body: str
    66	    proposed_by: str               # actor string: "agent", run id context, or PLAN_ACTOR
    67	    created_at: int
    68	
    69	
    70	@dataclass
    71	class FieldSlot:                   # §4.2 — one of the four field objects
    72	    value: str | None = None       # canonical; resolution engine is the only writer
    73	    proposal: Proposal | None = None
    74	    notes: str | None = None       # free guidance; result.notes = review notes (§4.2)
    75	
    76	
    77	@dataclass
    78	class TicketFields:                # tickets.fields JSON column, exactly four keys
    79	    success: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    80	    approach: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    81	    plan: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    82	    result: FieldSlot = field(default_factory=FieldSlot)
    83	
    84	
    85	# --- the grant pair (§4.4.7) ---
    86	NO_FURTHER: Final = "none"                     # wire sentinel: ceiling = the newly entered state
    87	NextCeiling = TicketState | Literal["none"]    # valid TicketState values are STATE_ORDER members
    88	
    89	
    90	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    91	class GrantPair:                   # required on every human accept/edit-accept
    92	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling
    93	    at_cap: AtCap
    94	
    95	
    96	@dataclass
    97	class Ticket:                      # §3.3 — column names match exactly
    98	    id: str
    99	    title: str                     # <= title_max_chars (200), every write path
   100	    state: TicketState
   101	    priority: Priority             # default P3
   102	    deadline: str | None           # ISO date
   103	    project: Project | None        # NULL when parented (derived)
   104	    sprint_item_id: str | None
   105	    sprint_id: str | None          # writable only when sprint_item_id IS NULL
   106	    recap: str                     # writable only past needs_success
   107	    ceiling: TicketState           # default needs_success (R2); restricted to STATE_ORDER
   108	    at_cap: AtCap                  # default propose (R2)

   110	    consecutive_failures: int      # §7.5

   113	    fields: TicketFields
   114	    claim_lock: str | None         # §7.3 claim token; NULL = unclaimed

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Sprint-range overlap (§3.1) and current-sprint selection (§6.1). Inclusive ISO
     2	date intervals compared lexicographically. Pure: a minimal DateRange projection in,
     3	ids/bools out."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from collections.abc import Iterable
     8	from typing import NamedTuple
     9	
    10	
    11	class DateRange(NamedTuple):
    12	    id: str
    13	    date_start: str            # ISO date, inclusive
    14	    date_end: str              # ISO date, inclusive
    15	
    16	
    17	def ranges_overlap(a_start: str, a_end: str, b_start: str, b_end: str) -> bool:
    18	    return a_start <= b_end and b_start <= a_end
    19	
    20	
    21	def find_overlap(
    22	    date_start: str, date_end: str, existing: Iterable[DateRange]
    23	) -> str | None:
    24	    for r in existing:
    25	        if ranges_overlap(date_start, date_end, r.date_start, r.date_end):
    26	            return r.id
    27	    return None
    28	
    29	
    30	def current_sprint_id(
    31	    planning_date: str, sprints: Iterable[DateRange]
    32	) -> str | None:
    33	    for r in sprints:
    34	        if r.date_start <= planning_date <= r.date_end:
    35	            return r.id
    36	    return None
