 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§7.6 request context and write validation. This is the server-side dependency
     2	that T10/T13 route bodies consume: it reads the X-Plan-* headers, classifies each
     3	request as a claimed agent, a plain agent, or the human, and validates a presented
     4	claim against a ticket's active lease. Lease liveness is decided only by
     5	dispatch.logic.claims — never re-derived here.
     6	
     7	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py
     8	family), not a logic or data module."""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import sqlite3
    13	from dataclasses import dataclass
    14	from typing import Final, NoReturn
    15	
    16	from fastapi import Request
    17	
    18	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    19	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    20	from planner.dispatch.logic.claims import has_active_claim, is_expired
    21	
    22	X_PLAN_RUN_ID: Final = "X-Plan-Run-Id"
    23	X_PLAN_CLAIM: Final = "X-Plan-Claim"
    24	X_PLAN_ACTOR: Final = "X-Plan-Actor"
    25	_DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR: Final = "agent"    # §7.6: PLAN_ACTOR default "agent"
    26	_HUMAN_ACTOR: Final = "human"
    27	
    28	
    29	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30	class RequestContext:
    31	    actor: str
    32	    run_id: str | None
    33	    claim: str | None
    34	    is_claimed_agent: bool
    35	    is_human: bool
    36	
    37	
    38	def _normalize(raw: str | None) -> str | None:
    39	    """strip(); an empty or whitespace-only header is an absent header."""
    40	    if raw is None:
    41	        return None
    42	    stripped = raw.strip()
    43	    return stripped or None
    44	
    45	
    46	def _classify(run_id: str | None, claim: str | None, actor: str | None) -> RequestContext:
    47	    """Pure classification over the §1.1 truth table; inputs are pre-normalized."""
    48	    if run_id is not None or claim is not None:
    49	        # Any claim header present (whole or half) is a claimed-agent attempt.
    50	        return RequestContext(
    51	            actor=actor if actor is not None else _DEFAULT_AGENT_ACTOR,
    52	            run_id=run_id,
    53	            claim=claim,
    54	            is_claimed_agent=True,
    55	            is_human=False,
    56	        )
    57	    if actor is not None:
    58	        # Non-dispatched agent context (planner-main chat agent): proposer from PLAN_ACTOR.
    59	        return RequestContext(
    60	            actor=actor,
    61	            run_id=None,
    62	            claim=None,
    63	            is_claimed_agent=False,
    64	            is_human=False,
    65	        )
    66	    # The human: UI requests carry no agent headers.
    67	    return RequestContext(
    68	        actor=_HUMAN_ACTOR,
    69	        run_id=None,
    70	        claim=None,
    71	        is_claimed_agent=False,
    72	        is_human=True,
    73	    )
    74	
    75	
    76	def request_context(request: Request) -> RequestContext:
    77	    """FastAPI dependency: reads the three headers, normalizes, classifies. No DB, no clock."""
    78	    headers = request.headers
    79	    return _classify(
    80	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_RUN_ID)),
    81	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_CLAIM)),
    82	        _normalize(headers.get(X_PLAN_ACTOR)),
    83	    )
    84	
    85	
    86	def _reject_stale(
    87	    reason: str,
    88	    message: str,
    89	    ctx: RequestContext,
    90	    ticket_id: str,
    91	    active_claim_present: bool,
    92	    stored_lock: str | None,
    93	    extra: JsonDict | None = None,
    94	) -> NoReturn:
    95	    # Presented values are echoed back — except a presented token equal to the stored
    96	    # claim_lock (the expired/run_mismatch/missing-half paths reach here with a correct
    97	    # token): verbatim it would put the live stored token into a detail that may be
    98	    # logged or displayed, so it is redacted.
    99	    presented_claim = ctx.claim
   100	    if presented_claim is not None and presented_claim == stored_lock:
   101	        presented_claim = "(redacted)"
   102	    detail: JsonDict = {
   103	        "reason": reason,
   104	        "ticket_id": ticket_id,
   105	        "presented_run_id": ctx.run_id,
   106	        "presented_claim": presented_claim,
   107	        "active_claim_present": active_claim_present,
   108	    }
   109	    if extra is not None:
   110	        detail.update(extra)
   111	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.stale_claim, message, detail)
   112	
   113	
   114	def require_claim(
   115	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, ctx: RequestContext, ticket_id: str, now: int
   116	) -> None:
   117	    """§7.6 write validation. Checks run in the fixed order (ticket exists → active
   118	    claim → unexpired → token → run id); the first failure raises stale_claim naming
   119	    which check failed. The stored token is never echoed. now is unix seconds."""
   120	    row = conn.execute(
   121	        "SELECT claim_lock, claim_expires FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
   122	    ).fetchone()
   123	    if row is None:
   124	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "ticket not found", {"ticket_id": ticket_id})
   125	    claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   126	    claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   127	    active = has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now)
   128	
   129	    if ctx.run_id is None or ctx.claim is None:
   130	        missing = [
   131	            name
   132	            for name, value in ((X_PLAN_RUN_ID, ctx.run_id), (X_PLAN_CLAIM, ctx.claim))
   133	            if value is None
   134	        ]
   135	        _reject_stale(
   136	            "missing_header",
   137	            "claim headers incomplete",
   138	            ctx,
   139	            ticket_id,
   140	            active,
   141	            claim_lock,
   142	            {"missing": missing},
   143	        )
   144	    if claim_lock is None:
   145	        _reject_stale(
   146	            "none_active", "ticket has no active claim", ctx, ticket_id, active, claim_lock
   147	        )
   148	    if is_expired(claim_expires, now):
   149	        _reject_stale(
   150	            "expired",
   151	            "claim expired",
   152	            ctx,
   153	            ticket_id,
   154	            active,
   155	            claim_lock,
   156	            {"claim_expires": claim_expires},
   157	        )
   158	    if ctx.claim != claim_lock:
   159	        _reject_stale(
   160	            "foreign",
   161	            "claim token does not match the ticket's active claim",
   162	            ctx,
   163	            ticket_id,
   164	            active,
   165	            claim_lock,
   166	        )
   167	    run_row = conn.execute(
   168	        "SELECT id FROM runs WHERE ticket_id=? AND status='running' "
   169	        "ORDER BY started_at DESC LIMIT 1",
   170	        (ticket_id,),
   171	    ).fetchone()
   172	    if run_row is None or str(run_row["id"]) != ctx.run_id:
   173	        _reject_stale(
   174	            "run_mismatch",
   175	            "run id does not match the ticket's running run",
   176	            ctx,
   177	            ticket_id,
   178	            active,
   179	            claim_lock,
   180	        )

exec

     4	dataclass field names one-for-one."""

    51	);

    55	  title                TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(title) <= 200),
    56	  state                TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'needs_success'
    57	                       CHECK (state IN ('needs_success','needs_approach','needs_plan',
    58	                                        'in_progress','needs_review','done','dropped')),
    59	  priority             TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'P3' CHECK (priority IN ('P0','P1','P2','P3')),
    60	  deadline             TEXT,
    61	  project              TEXT CHECK (project IN ('Vylo','Tribe','Learning','Other')),  -- NULL when parented
    62	  sprint_item_id       TEXT REFERENCES sprint_items(id),
    63	  sprint_id            TEXT REFERENCES sprints(id),  -- writable only when sprint_item_id IS NULL
    64	  recap                TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    65	  ceiling              TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'needs_success'
    66	                       CHECK (ceiling IN ('needs_success','needs_approach','needs_plan',
    67	                                          'in_progress','needs_review','done')),
    68	  at_cap               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'propose' CHECK (at_cap IN ('stop','propose')),
    69	  auto_blocked         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    70	  consecutive_failures INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- §7.5
    71	  chat_session_key     TEXT,
    72	  alias                TEXT,                         -- migration "Ticket ID:" (§12)
    73	  fields               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{"success":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null},"approach":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null},"plan":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null},"result":{"value":null,"proposal":null,"notes":null}}',
    74	  claim_lock           TEXT,                         -- §7.3 claim token; NULL = unclaimed
    75	  claim_expires        INTEGER,
    76	  created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL,
    77	  updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL
    78	);
    79	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tickets_alias ON tickets(alias) WHERE alias IS NOT NULL;
    80	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tickets_state ON tickets(state);
    81	
    82	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS days (
    83	  id               TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                 -- day_YYYY-MM-DD (planning date, §3.4)
    84	  brief            TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    85	  notes            TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    86	  plan             TEXT,                             -- JSON PlanTree | NULL
    87	  chat_session_key TEXT,
    88	  created_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
    89	  updated_at       INTEGER NOT NULL
    90	);
    91	
    92	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS day_tickets (
    93	  day_id    TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES days(id),
    94	  ticket_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tickets(id),
    95	  position  INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (position >= 0),  -- contiguous from 0, writer-enforced
    96	  PRIMARY KEY (day_id, ticket_id)
    97	);
    98	
    99	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ideas (
   100	  id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- idea_<slug>
   101	  title      TEXT NOT NULL,
   102	  body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
   103	  project    TEXT CHECK (project IN ('Vylo','Tribe','Learning','Other')),  -- nullable (§3.5)
   104	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   105	  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
   106	);
   107	
   108	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS links (                   -- §3.6, exactly the spec's three columns
   109	  from_id TEXT NOT NULL,
   110	  to_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
   111	  kind    TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('belongs_to','parent_child','blocks','relates')),
   112	  PRIMARY KEY (from_id, to_id, kind),
   113	  CHECK (from_id <> to_id)
   114	);
   115	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_links_one_belongs_to
   116	  ON links(from_id) WHERE kind = 'belongs_to';       -- at most one belongs_to per ticket
   117	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_links_to ON links(to_id, kind);
   118	
   119	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (                  -- §3, append-only
   120	  id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
   121	  entity_id  TEXT NOT NULL,
   122	  kind       TEXT NOT NULL,
   123	  payload    TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',             -- JSON
   124	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
   125	);
   126	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_entity ON events(entity_id, id);
   127	
   128	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (                    -- §7.3
   129	  id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- run_<slug>
   130	  ticket_id  TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tickets(id),
   131	  status     TEXT NOT NULL
   132	             CHECK (status IN ('running','done','blocked','crashed','timed_out','reclaimed','spawn_failed')),
   133	  started_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   134	  ended_at   INTEGER,
   135	  summary    TEXT,
   136	  error      TEXT,
   137	  pid        INTEGER
   138	);
   139	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_ticket ON runs(ticket_id, started_at);
   140	
   141	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS boundary_runs (           -- §6.2: never twice per date
   142	  planning_date TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                    -- ISO date
   143	  ran_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
   144	  judgment      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (judgment IN ('ok','skipped','failed'))
   145	);
   146	"""
   147	
   148	
   149	def connect(db_path: str, busy_timeout_ms: int = 5000) -> sqlite3.Connection:
   150	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, isolation_level=None)
   151	    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
   152	    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
   153	    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
   154	    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA busy_timeout={int(busy_timeout_ms)}")
   155	    return conn
   156	
   157	
   158	def create_schema(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
   159	    conn.executescript(DDL)
   160	    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version={SCHEMA_VERSION}")

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Item status-transition permissions (§3.2, §4.4.7). Pure: returns a verdict enum;
     2	never raises, never writes. The data layer maps each verdict to an error or a write."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	from collections.abc import Sequence
     7	from enum import StrEnum
     8	
     9	from planner.sprints.contracts import (
    10	    AGENT_ITEM_TRANSITIONS,
    11	    PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES,
    12	    ItemStatus,
    13	)
    14	
    15	
    16	class TransitionVerdict(StrEnum):
    17	    allowed = "allowed"
    18	    forbidden = "forbidden"               # not an agent/human edge, or into a proposal-only status
    19	    missing_blockers = "missing_blockers"  # -> blocked with an empty blocked_by list
    20	
    21	
    22	def classify_agent_transition(
    23	    from_status: ItemStatus,
    24	    to_status: ItemStatus,
    25	    blocked_by: Sequence[str],
    26	) -> TransitionVerdict:
    27	    if to_status in PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES:
    28	        return TransitionVerdict.forbidden
    29	    if (from_status, to_status) not in AGENT_ITEM_TRANSITIONS:
    30	        return TransitionVerdict.forbidden
    31	    if to_status is ItemStatus.blocked and not blocked_by:
    32	        return TransitionVerdict.missing_blockers
    33	    return TransitionVerdict.allowed
    34	
    35	
    36	def classify_human_transition(
    37	    from_status: ItemStatus,
    38	    to_status: ItemStatus,
    39	    blocked_by: Sequence[str],
    40	) -> TransitionVerdict:
    41	    if to_status in PROPOSAL_ONLY_STATUSES:
    42	        return TransitionVerdict.forbidden
    43	    # done is terminal: entered only via accepted proposal (§4.4.7), no exit edge.
    44	    # deferred_next_sprint stays human-resumable, or deferral would be a dead end.
    45	    if from_status is ItemStatus.done:
    46	        return TransitionVerdict.forbidden
    47	    if from_status == to_status:
    48	        return TransitionVerdict.forbidden
    49	    if to_status is ItemStatus.blocked and not blocked_by:
    50	        return TransitionVerdict.missing_blockers
    51	    return TransitionVerdict.allowed

     1	"""The day data layer. Materializes a day on first read/write (no "missing day"
     2	state), owns the ordered day-ticket list (contiguous positions from 0), plan JSON
     3	storage, brief/notes writes, and applies the logic layer's plan-tree effects.
     4	Every state transition here appends exactly one canonical event. No FastAPI/
     5	pydantic; times come in as unix-second ints from the caller's clock."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import sqlite3
    11	
    12	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    13	from planner.core.events import append_event
    14	from planner.days.contracts import Day, DayTicket, PlanTree
    15	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    16	    AddTicketToDay,
    17	    Effect,
    18	    EmitEvent,
    19	    ReplanChild,
    20	    ReplanRequest,
    21	    ReplanRoot,
    22	)
    23	from planner.days.logic.tree import tree_from_dict, tree_to_dict
    24	
    25	
    26	def materialize_day(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
    27	    """§3.4: create the day row if absent (brief='', notes='', plan=NULL,
    28	    chat_session_key=NULL, created_at=updated_at=now_unix) and append a
    29	    day_created event. Idempotent: a present day → no write, no event."""
    30	    if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM days WHERE id = ?", (day_id,)).fetchone() is not None:
    31	        return
    32	    conn.execute(
    33	        "INSERT INTO days (id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at) "
    34	        "VALUES (?, '', '', NULL, NULL, ?, ?)",
    35	        (day_id, now_unix, now_unix),
    36	    )
    37	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_created, {}, now_unix)
    38	
    39	
    40	def read_day(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, now_unix: int) -> Day:
    41	    """§3.4 'reading a nonexistent day materializes it empty'. Materializes, then
    42	    SELECTs the row and builds a Day (plan parsed via tree_from_dict when set)."""
    43	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
    44	    row = conn.execute(
    45	        "SELECT id, brief, notes, plan, chat_session_key, created_at, updated_at "
    46	        "FROM days WHERE id = ?",
    47	        (day_id,),
    48	    ).fetchone()
    49	    assert row is not None
    50	    plan_json = row["plan"]
    51	    return Day(
    52	        id=row["id"],
    53	        brief=row["brief"],
    54	        notes=row["notes"],
    55	        plan=tree_from_dict(json.loads(plan_json)) if plan_json is not None else None,
    56	        chat_session_key=row["chat_session_key"],
    57	        created_at=row["created_at"],
    58	        updated_at=row["updated_at"],
    59	    )
    60	
    61	
    62	def list_day_tickets(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str) -> list[DayTicket]:
    63	    """day_tickets for the day, ordered by position (contiguous from 0)."""
    64	    rows = conn.execute(
    65	        "SELECT day_id, ticket_id, position FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? ORDER BY position",
    66	        (day_id,),
    67	    ).fetchall()
    68	    return [
    69	        DayTicket(day_id=row["day_id"], ticket_id=row["ticket_id"], position=row["position"])
    70	        for row in rows
    71	    ]
    72	
    73	
    74	def load_plan(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str) -> PlanTree | None:
    75	    """Parse days.plan JSON → PlanTree, or None (absent day or NULL plan)."""
    76	    row = conn.execute("SELECT plan FROM days WHERE id = ?", (day_id,)).fetchone()
    77	    if row is None or row["plan"] is None:
    78	        return None
    79	    return tree_from_dict(json.loads(row["plan"]))
    80	
    81	
    82	def store_plan(
    83	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, tree: PlanTree | None, now_unix: int
    84	) -> None:
    85	    """Materialize, then write days.plan = tree_to_dict(tree) JSON (or NULL) and
    86	    bump updated_at. No event here (callers emit the specific plan_* event)."""
    87	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
    88	    plan_json = json.dumps(tree_to_dict(tree)) if tree is not None else None
    89	    conn.execute(
    90	        "UPDATE days SET plan = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
    91	        (plan_json, now_unix, day_id),
    92	    )
    93	
    94	
    95	def store_judgment(
    96	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, brief: str, tree: PlanTree, now_unix: int
    97	) -> None:
    98	    """Boundary success path: write days.brief + days.plan (serialized) +
    99	    updated_at in one UPDATE. No day_updated event (that kind is reserved for
   100	    manual edits); the boundary emits plan_proposed separately."""
   101	    conn.execute(
   102	        "UPDATE days SET brief = ?, plan = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   103	        (brief, json.dumps(tree_to_dict(tree)), now_unix, day_id),
   104	    )
   105	
   106	
   107	def add_day_ticket(
   108	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, ticket_id: str, now_unix: int, cause: str = "manual"
   109	) -> bool:
   110	    """§3.4: append at end with the next contiguous position (= current count).
   111	    Idempotent: if (day_id, ticket_id) already present → return False, no event.
   112	    On add → INSERT, append day_ticket_added {ticket_id, position, cause}, bump
   113	    updated_at, return True. Materializes the day first (write path)."""
   114	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   115	    present = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT 1 FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   117	        (day_id, ticket_id),
   118	    ).fetchone()
   119	    if present is not None:
   120	        return False
   121	    position = conn.execute(
   122	        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ?", (day_id,)
   123	    ).fetchone()["n"]
   124	    conn.execute(
   125	        "INSERT INTO day_tickets (day_id, ticket_id, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   126	        (day_id, ticket_id, position),
   127	    )
   128	    append_event(
   129	        conn,
   130	        day_id,
   131	        EventKind.day_ticket_added,
   132	        {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "position": position, "cause": cause},
   133	        now_unix,
   134	    )
   135	    conn.execute("UPDATE days SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_unix, day_id))
   136	    return True
   137	
   138	
   139	def remove_day_ticket(
   140	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, ticket_id: str, now_unix: int
   141	) -> None:
   142	    """§3.4: delete the association only (ticket state untouched). If nothing was
   143	    deleted (rowcount 0) → no-op, no event, no re-pack. Else re-pack remaining
   144	    positions to 0..n-1 in existing position order, append day_ticket_removed
   145	    {ticket_id}, bump updated_at."""
   146	    cursor = conn.execute(
   147	        "DELETE FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   148	        (day_id, ticket_id),
   149	    )
   150	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   151	        return
   152	    survivors = conn.execute(
   153	        "SELECT ticket_id FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? ORDER BY position",
   154	        (day_id,),
   155	    ).fetchall()
   156	    for new_position, row in enumerate(survivors):
   157	        conn.execute(
   158	            "UPDATE day_tickets SET position = ? WHERE day_id = ? AND ticket_id = ?",
   159	            (new_position, day_id, row["ticket_id"]),
   160	        )
   161	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_ticket_removed, {"ticket_id": ticket_id}, now_unix)
   162	    conn.execute("UPDATE days SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_unix, day_id))
   163	
   164	
   165	def set_brief(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, brief: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
   166	    """Manual brief edit: materialize, UPDATE brief + updated_at, append
   167	    day_updated {field: "brief"}."""
   168	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   169	    conn.execute(
   170	        "UPDATE days SET brief = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (brief, now_unix, day_id)
   171	    )
   172	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_updated, {"field": "brief"}, now_unix)
   173	
   174	
   175	def set_notes(conn: sqlite3.Connection, day_id: str, notes: str, now_unix: int) -> None:
   176	    """Manual notes edit: materialize, UPDATE notes + updated_at, append
   177	    day_updated {field: "notes"}."""
   178	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   179	    conn.execute(
   180	        "UPDATE days SET notes = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (notes, now_unix, day_id)
   181	    )
   182	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_updated, {"field": "notes"}, now_unix)
   183	
   184	
   185	def apply_plan_effects(
   186	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   187	    day_id: str,
   188	    new_tree: PlanTree | None,
   189	    effects: list[Effect],
   190	    now_unix: int,
   191	) -> list[ReplanRequest]:
   192	    """Persist the transform result and apply its effects. (1) store_plan writes
   193	    days.plan (or NULL for reject-all). (2) For each effect in order:
   194	    AddTicketToDay → add_day_ticket(cause="plan_accept") (idempotent);
   195	    EmitEvent → append_event; ReplanRoot/ReplanChild → collected. (3) Return the
   196	    collected replan requests for the stage-4 runtime to serialize + execute (R5).
   197	    At stage 3 no adapter is called here."""
   198	    store_plan(conn, day_id, new_tree, now_unix)
   199	    replans: list[ReplanRequest] = []
   200	    for effect in effects:
   201	        if isinstance(effect, AddTicketToDay):
   202	            add_day_ticket(conn, day_id, effect.ticket_id, now_unix, cause="plan_accept")
   203	        elif isinstance(effect, EmitEvent):
   204	            append_event(conn, day_id, effect.kind, effect.payload, now_unix)
   205	        elif isinstance(effect, (ReplanRoot, ReplanChild)):
   206	            replans.append(effect)
   207	    return replans

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The boundary deterministic pass (§6.2) as pure functions over plain projected
     2	row data. Produce the ``BoundaryInputs`` digest lists: carryover, overdue,
     3	approvals — plus the yesterday done/not-done split for ``day_closed``. Zero DB
     4	access; the boundary layer does the SQL and hands rows in. Stdlib + planner
     5	contract modules only."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from planner.sprints.contracts import ItemStatus
    12	from planner.tickets.contracts import GATING_FIELD, TicketState
    13	
    14	# §6.2.2 — carryover excludes tickets that are already terminal.
    15	_CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE = {TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped}
    16	# Overdue excludes items that are done or already deferred to next sprint.
    17	_ITEM_CLOSED = {ItemStatus.done, ItemStatus.deferred_next_sprint}
    18	
    19	
    20	def carryover_candidates(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    21	    """§6.2 step 2: yesterday's day-tickets whose ticket state is NOT done/dropped.
    22	
    23	    Input rows carry {id, title, state, priority} (ticket joined to its day-ticket,
    24	    in day-ticket position order). Output digest keeps the same four keys. StrEnum
    25	    members compare equal to their string values, so plain state strings work.
    26	    """
    27	    return [
    28	        {
    29	            "id": row["id"],
    30	            "title": row["title"],
    31	            "state": row["state"],
    32	            "priority": row["priority"],
    33	        }
    34	        for row in yesterday_tickets
    35	        if row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE
    36	    ]
    37	
    38	
    39	def day_ticket_counts(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    40	    """§6.2 step 4: (done_count, not_done_count) over yesterday's day-tickets.
    41	
    42	    done_count = rows with state == done; not_done_count = the rest (dropped and
    43	    every unfinished state). The two sum to len(rows).
    44	    """
    45	    done_count = sum(1 for row in yesterday_tickets if row["state"] == TicketState.done)
    46	    return done_count, len(yesterday_tickets) - done_count
    47	
    48	
    49	def overdue_list(
    50	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]], today_iso: str
    51	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    52	    """§6.2 step 3 (overdue). A ticket is overdue iff deadline is not None and
    53	    deadline < today_iso (strict; due-today is NOT overdue) and state not in
    54	    {done, dropped}. An item is overdue iff deadline < today_iso and status not
    55	    in {done, deferred_next_sprint}. Uniform digest {id, title, state, priority}
    56	    (for items, state = the item status). ISO date strings compare lexically.
    57	    Tickets first, then items."""
    58	    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59	    for row in tickets:
    60	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    61	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE:
    62	            result.append(
    63	                {
    64	                    "id": row["id"],
    65	                    "title": row["title"],
    66	                    "state": row["state"],
    67	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    68	                }
    69	            )
    70	    for row in items:
    71	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    72	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["status"] not in _ITEM_CLOSED:
    73	            result.append(
    74	                {
    75	                    "id": row["id"],
    76	                    "title": row["title"],
    77	                    "state": row["status"],
    78	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    79	                }
    80	            )
    81	    return result
    82	
    83	
    84	def approvals_digest(
    85	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]]
    86	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    87	    """§6.2 step 3 / §4.5 approval-queue digest. Three sources, oldest-pending first:
    88	
    89	    * a ticket with a non-null proposal on its current gating field
    90	      (GATING_FIELD[state]); kind = the gating field name (e.g. "plan"),
    91	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"];
    92	    * a ticket in ``needs_review`` (§4.5); kind = "review", waiting_since =
    93	      the ticket's updated_at (DB-internal proxy for review entry time);
    94	    * a sprint item with a non-null status_proposal; kind = "status",
    95	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"].
    96	
    97	    Digest per base.py: {entity_id, kind, waiting_since}. Ticket rows carry
    98	    {id, state, fields(dict), updated_at}; item rows carry {id, status_proposal}.
    99	    """
   100	    digest: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   101	    for row in tickets:
   102	        state = row["state"]
   103	        if state == TicketState.needs_review:
   104	            digest.append(
   105	                {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "review", "waiting_since": row["updated_at"]}
   106	            )
   107	            continue
   108	        gating = GATING_FIELD.get(TicketState(state))
   109	        if gating is None:
   110	            continue
   111	        slot = row["fields"].get(gating.value)
   112	        if slot is None:
   113	            continue
   114	        proposal = slot.get("proposal")
   115	        if proposal is None:
   116	            continue
   117	        digest.append(
   118	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": gating.value, "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   119	        )
   120	    for row in items:
   121	        proposal = row["status_proposal"]
   122	        if proposal is None:
   123	            continue
   124	        digest.append(
   125	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "status", "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   126	        )
   127	    digest.sort(key=lambda entry: entry["waiting_since"])
   128	    return digest

     1	/* Data layer: JSON fetch wrapper (structured-error passthrough), the WS client,
     2	 * and the invalidation bus (SPEC §9). No client-side state store — the bus holds
     3	 * only a cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff delay, and one flush callback; every
     4	 * flush makes the active screen refetch. WS batches are an invalidation signal:
     5	 * onmessage reads only msg.cursor, never the events array.
     6	 * Classic script: attaches Planner.api and Planner.bus; defines window.__plannerDebug. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
    10	  var config = Planner.config;
    11	
    12	  // The sanctioned console hook: counters only, one console.debug per flush / open.
    13	  window.__plannerDebug = { flushes: 0, wsOpens: 0, cursor: 0 };
    14	
    15	  function makeError(code, message, extra) {
    16	    var err = new Error(message);
    17	    err.code = code;
    18	    if (extra) {
    19	      Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (key) {
    20	        err[key] = extra[key];
    21	      });
    22	    }
    23	    return err;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  function makePlannerError(envelope, status) {
    27	    var err = new Error(envelope.message);
    28	    err.code = envelope.code;
    29	    err.detail = envelope.detail;
    30	    err.status = status;
    31	    err.isPlannerError = true;
    32	    return err;
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  // --- fetchJson ---------------------------------------------------------------
    36	
    37	  function fetchJson(path, options) {
    38	    options = options || {};
    39	    var init = { method: options.method || "GET", headers: {} };
    40	    if (options.body !== undefined && options.body !== null) {
    41	      init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    42	      init.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    43	    }
    44	    return fetch(path, init).then(
    45	      function (response) {
    46	        return response.text().then(function (raw) {
    47	          return { response: response, raw: raw };
    48	        });
    49	      },
    50	      function () {
    51	        return Promise.reject(makeError("network", "network error"));
    52	      }
    53	    ).then(function (pair) {
    54	      var response = pair.response;
    55	      var raw = pair.raw;
    56	      if (response.ok) {
    57	        try {
    58	          return raw === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(raw);
    59	        } catch (parseErr) {
    60	          return Promise.reject(
    61	            makeError("bad_json", "invalid JSON in response", { status: response.status })
    62	          );
    63	        }
    64	      }
    65	      var parsed = null;
    66	      try {
    67	        parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    68	      } catch (parseErr) {
    69	        parsed = null;
    70	      }
    71	      if (parsed && parsed.error && parsed.error.code) {
    72	        return Promise.reject(makePlannerError(parsed.error, response.status));
    73	      }
    74	      return Promise.reject(
    75	        makeError("http_error", "HTTP " + response.status, { status: response.status })
    76	      );
    77	    });
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  Planner.api = { fetchJson: fetchJson };
    81	
    82	  // --- the invalidation bus ----------------------------------------------------
    83	
    84	  var cursor = 0;
    85	  var socket = null;
    86	  var debounceMs = config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    87	  var flushTimer = null;
    88	  var retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
    89	  var onFlush = null;
    90	  var started = false;
    91	
    92	  function flush() {
    93	    flushTimer = null;
    94	    window.__plannerDebug.flushes += 1;
    95	    console.debug("[planner] flush " + window.__plannerDebug.flushes);
    96	    if (onFlush) {
    97	      onFlush();
    98	    }
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  function schedule() {
   102	    clearTimeout(flushTimer);
   103	    flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, debounceMs);
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  function connect() {
   107	    var scheme = location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
   108	    var url = scheme + location.host + config.API.EVENTS_WS + "?since=" + cursor;
   109	    socket = new WebSocket(url);
   110	    socket.onopen = function () {
   111	      retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
   112	      window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens += 1;
   113	      console.debug("[planner] ws open since=" + cursor);
   114	    };
   115	    socket.onmessage = function (event) {
   116	      var msg;
   117	      try {
   118	        msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
   119	      } catch (parseErr) {
   120	        console.debug("[planner] ws bad json");
   121	        return;
   122	      }
   123	      if (typeof msg.cursor === "number") {
   124	        cursor = msg.cursor;
   125	        window.__plannerDebug.cursor = cursor;
   126	      }
   127	      schedule();
   128	    };
   129	    socket.onclose = function () {
   130	      socket = null;
   131	      setTimeout(connect, retryMs);
   132	      retryMs = Math.min(retryMs * config.WS_RETRY_FACTOR, config.WS_RETRY_MAX_MS);
   133	    };
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  Planner.bus = {
   137	    onInvalidate: function (fn) {
   138	      onFlush = fn;
   139	    },
   140	    start: function (ms) {
   141	      if (started) {
   142	        return;
   143	      }
   144	      started = true;
   145	      debounceMs = ms;
   146	      connect();
   147	    }
   148	  };
   149	})();

     1	"""Plan-tree transforms (§6.3) as pure functions ``PlanTree → (new_tree, effects)``,
     2	plus JSON serialization of the tree for the ``days.plan`` column and for event
     3	payloads that carry ``old_tree``. No side effects; the data layer persists the
     4	returned tree and applies the effects. Stdlib + planner contract modules only."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	from dataclasses import replace
     9	
    10	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict
    11	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanRoot, PlanTree
    12	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    13	    AddTicketToDay,
    14	    Effect,
    15	    EmitEvent,
    16	    ReplanChild,
    17	    ReplanRoot,
    18	)
    19	
    20	# A node address in the plan tree: the literal ``"root"`` or a child ``position``.
    21	# Mirrors the plan_node_accepted / plan_node_invalidated payload contract, whose
    22	# ``{node}`` value is ``"root" | <child position>``.
    23	NodeRef = str | int
    24	
    25	
    26	def tree_to_dict(tree: PlanTree) -> JsonDict:
    27	    """{root:{focus,status}, children:[{ticket_id,note,status,position}, ...]}.
    28	
    29	    NodeStatus serialized as its str value; child order preserved.
    30	    """
    31	    return {
    32	        "root": {"focus": tree.root.focus, "status": tree.root.status.value},
    33	        "children": [
    34	            {
    35	                "ticket_id": child.ticket_id,
    36	                "note": child.note,
    37	                "status": child.status.value,
    38	                "position": child.position,
    39	            }
    40	            for child in tree.children
    41	        ],
    42	    }
    43	
    44	
    45	def tree_from_dict(data: JsonDict) -> PlanTree:
    46	    """Inverse of ``tree_to_dict``. status parsed via ``NodeStatus(...)``."""
    47	    root = data["root"]
    48	    children = [
    49	        PlanNode(
    50	            ticket_id=child["ticket_id"],
    51	            note=child["note"],
    52	            status=NodeStatus(child["status"]),
    53	            position=child["position"],
    54	        )
    55	        for child in data["children"]
    56	    ]
    57	    return PlanTree(
    58	        root=PlanRoot(focus=root["focus"], status=NodeStatus(root["status"])),
    59	        children=children,
    60	    )
    61	
    62	
    63	def as_proposed(tree: PlanTree) -> PlanTree:
    64	    """Return a copy with root and every child status=proposed. The boundary
    65	    stores the judgment tree as a PROPOSED plan (§6.2)."""
    66	    return PlanTree(
    67	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.proposed),
    68	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.proposed) for child in tree.children],
    69	    )
    70	
    71	
    72	def accept_node(tree: PlanTree, ref: NodeRef) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
    73	    """§6.3: accepting a node sets it accepted; accepting root does NOT cascade.
    74	
    75	    A child with a ticket_id also yields an AddTicketToDay effect (appended at
    76	    end if absent). Effect order: EmitEvent(plan_node_accepted) then (for a child
    77	    with a ticket_id) AddTicketToDay.
    78	    """
    79	    if ref == "root":
    80	        new_tree = PlanTree(
    81	            root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
    82	            children=[replace(child) for child in tree.children],
    83	        )
    84	        return new_tree, [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": "root"})]
    85	
    86	    accepted: PlanNode | None = None
    87	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
    88	    for child in tree.children:
    89	        if child.position == ref:
    90	            accepted = replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted)
    91	            children.append(accepted)
    92	        else:
    93	            children.append(replace(child))
    94	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
    95	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": ref})]
    96	    if accepted is not None and accepted.ticket_id:
    97	        effects.append(AddTicketToDay(accepted.ticket_id))
    98	    return new_tree, effects
    99	
   100	
   101	def accept_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   102	    """§6.3 accept-all: root + every child accepted. Effects =
   103	    [EmitEvent(plan_accepted_all, {})] then AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id) for
   104	    each child with a ticket_id, in child position order. Idempotent add is the
   105	    data layer's job (a17)."""
   106	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   107	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
   108	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted) for child in tree.children],
   109	    )
   110	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_accepted_all, {})]
   111	    for child in tree.children:
   112	        if child.ticket_id:
   113	            effects.append(AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id))
   114	    return new_tree, effects
   115	
   116	
   117	def invalidate_root(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   118	    """§6.3: root + EVERY child → invalidated; request exactly one root replan."""
   119	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   120	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.invalidated),
   121	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated) for child in tree.children],
   122	    )
   123	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   124	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": "root"}),
   125	        ReplanRoot(),
   126	    ]
   127	    return new_tree, effects
   128	
   129	
   130	def invalidate_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   131	    """§6.3: ONLY the child at ``position`` → invalidated; root and every other
   132	    child keep their prior status. Requests exactly one child replan."""
   133	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
   134	    for child in tree.children:
   135	        if child.position == position:
   136	            children.append(replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated))
   137	        else:
   138	            children.append(replace(child))
   139	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
   140	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   141	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": position}),
   142	        ReplanChild(position),
   143	    ]
   144	    return new_tree, effects
   145	
   146	
   147	def reject_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[None, list[Effect]]:
   148	    """§6.3 reject-all: plan cleared. New tree is None (the data layer writes
   149	    days.plan = NULL). The old tree is preserved in the event payload."""
   150	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   151	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_rejected, {"old_tree": tree_to_dict(tree)})
   152	    ]
   153	    return None, effects

codex
[orchestration/tickets/T19-e2e-flows-b/plan.md §5/e28/e29] `section[data-screen="day"]` is wrong. `assets/app.js:59-62` creates the screen root as a `div`, and `assets/screens-day.js:22-23` sets `data-screen="day"` on that root; no Day `section` exists. Use a post-render selector that actually matches, e.g. `.plan-tree [data-node="root"]`, for the e28/e29 `open_page` ready selector.

[orchestration/tickets/T19-e2e-flows-b/plan.md §4/e31] `_reload_settle` does not reapply the harness’s settled-page discipline. The plan says `open_page(..., settled=True)` waits `flushes>=1` then re-anchors (`plan.md:72-76`; actual harness `tests/e2e/conftest.py:186-193`), but `_reload_settle` only waits selector + `wsOpens` (`plan.md:192-198`). For SPEC item 31 reload equivalence (`SPEC.md:310`), wait `flushes>=1` after reload and then re-wait the ready selector before taking the “after” snapshot.
